Every `path:line` below was re-read at commit `438e5e1a1c79d3c9735abf8e8ca0b735969a6a3c`. 1 claim(s) whose citations were not found there were dropped.

## Architecture

- run.sh: POSIX-sh launcher for every plugin hook: finds uv, builds the Python environment (synchronously for --warm, detached on cold start), and execs the hook under uv once the environment is ready. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L2))
- synthesize_now.py: Scans recent diary session blocks, groups them by project, and writes a short LLM or extractive status summary per project to now_dir/{project}.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L1))
- __init__.py: Package marker for the catalog generators. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/__init__.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/__init__.py#L1))
- banks.py: Model-free generator that merges shared banks' committed catalog fragments (or derived entries) into banks.json and reports cross-bank collisions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L1))
- base.py: GeneratorBase template method, plus GenerationResult and GenerationState: hash-gated, concurrency-bounded, retrying per-source LLM catalog generation with atomic writes. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L3))
- config.py: CatalogConfig dataclass with validation, and load_catalog_config() to read it from plugin userConfig options. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L1))
- dispatcher.py: Single entry point generate_catalogs() that runs the registered generators in a fixed order, with filtering, force, dry run and per-generator failure isolation. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L1))
- memory.py: MemoryGenerator: catalogs memory/*.md via the LLM into memory.json, preserving hand-authored fields and generating validated section_anchors. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L3))
- fleet.py: Joins the session registry, checkpoints and host-written roster/tmux files into one fleet reading, and renders it as AGENTS.md and fleet.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1))
- fsio.py: Shared filesystem helpers: the Claude config dir lookup and atomic text/JSON writes. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py#L1))
- hook_input.py: The single safe way a hook entry point parses its stdin JSON payload. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py#L1))
- job_effort.py: Reads the per-job '<job>_effort' plugin options and turns them into an optional effort kwarg for model calls. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py#L1))
- learnings_ledger.py: A hash ledger of which '## Session Learnings' blocks dream has already consolidated. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L1))
- memory_router.py: The router strategies (token_overlap, llm, llm_hybrid) that choose which memory, skill and resource catalog entries to inject into each prompt. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L1))
- memory_write_floor.py: Deterministic veto checks applied to every unattended memory write after the judge's verdict. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L1))
- project_identity.py: Maps a session cwd to a stable project name using project-map.yaml settings or git. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L1))
- rejections.py: An append-only, size-capped JSONL audit log of the items the memory judge dropped. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py#L1))
- routing_validation.py: A deterministic post-proposal gate that adds section-routing and duplicate warnings to dream proposals. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L1))
- runtime.py: Helpers for spawning children under uv, killing a child's whole process group on timeout, and placing cross-session lock files. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py#L1))
- section_loader.py: Parses 'file.md#Section' picks and extracts the matching H2 section (plus the file preamble) from memory files. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py#L1))
- session_registry.py: Writes, updates and garbage-collects the per-session JSON registry entries the multiplai hub reads. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L1))
- taxonomy.py: Defines the two-axis provenance × kind vocabulary for learnings, with its rankings, legacy mapping and parsing. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L1))
- thinking.py: Decides whether, and how, to send the `thinking=` keyword on mechanical model calls, with a fallback for older multiplai-core versions that don't accept it. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L1))
- timeparse.py: A leaf module holding the single ISO timestamp parser shared by the fleet code. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py#L1))
- transcript_distiller.py: Shrinks JSONL session transcripts into compact text chunks, without any LLM call, so they fit an extraction token budget. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L1))
- utilisation.py: Memory utilisation telemetry: records injections and two usage estimators in utilisation.jsonl, then aggregates, ranks, renders and compacts them. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L1))
- __init__.py: Package front for the expensive fleet collectors; re-exports their dataclasses and collect_* functions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/__init__.py:26-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/__init__.py#L26))
- backlog.py: Counts pending queues (learnings, dream proposals, extraction markers, INBOX files) for the fleet digest. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:82`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L82))
- common.py: Shared plumbing for the collectors: a bounded subprocess runner that never raises, and a TTL'd JSON cache file. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L1))
- git_repos.py: Finds git checkouts under the workspace and reports dirty trees, unpushed or never-pushed branches, and linked worktrees. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L1))
- jobs.py: Reads Claude Code background job state and the worker roster from the shared config directory (read-only). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L1))
- prs.py: Collects open PRs through the gh CLI, classifies bot vs human and CI state, serialises scans for the cache, and detects PR stacks. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L1))
- __init__.py: Public API of the code_facts package: re-exports the models, explore loop, partitioner and prompt builders. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/__init__.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/__init__.py#L1))
- explore.py: The explore loop: asks one agent per unit with bounded concurrency, then keeps only the facts whose citations pass a git-backed check. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L1))
- models.py: Pydantic models for agent answers (facts, citations, terms) and for the units of code agents are given. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py#L1))
- partition.py: Splits a Scope into explore Units, first by directory and then by line count. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py#L1))
- prompts.py: Builds the prompt text for the explore agents: citation rules, untrusted-content rules and the per-unit prompt. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py#L1))
- __init__.py: Package marker for the plugin's context-specific library, with a docstring that names the modules it holds. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/__init__.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/__init__.py#L1))
- bank_collisions.py: Reports cross-bank collisions (duplicate filenames, overlapping intent domains, duplicate H2 headings) when the catalog is built, and never edits anything. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L1))
- bank_git.py: Never-raising, timeout-bounded wrappers around git and gh for pulling, committing, pushing and opening PRs on bank repos. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L1))
- banks.py: Plugin-side handling of memory banks: listing configured banks, parsing and resolving refs, the sync TTL option, and fenced rendering of shared-bank content. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L1))
- checkpoint.py: Core of the checkpoint lifecycle: config, token measurement, trigger decisions, the writer's single-flight guard, pending handoff markers, retirement and sweeping, auto-compact estimation and building the rebuild seed. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1))
- checkpoint_drain.py: Queue that runs on the host for end-of-session checkpoints when the container is exiting, drained by the shared extraction-drain launch loop. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py#L1))
- conflict_edits.py: Deterministic, precision-first matcher that pairs verified learnings with the existing memory line they supersede and renders a `## Conflict Resolutions` section. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L2))
- costing_collector.py: Incremental, idempotent collector that prices Claude Code transcript JSONLs into a cost ledger, with skill/agent attribution and a one-time branch backfill. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L1))
- dream_triage.py: Splits a dream proposal's items into auto-apply, review and drop, using the rubric, the judge verdicts and the write floor, and renders receipts and summaries. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L1))
- extraction_drain.py: The single dequeue/launch implementation for deferred-extraction markers, also reused by the checkpoint queue, with stale recovery and quarantine. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L1))
- board.py: Maps build phases to kanban columns and records the change's card in .board.json, plus a BOARD: stdout line. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L1))
- budget.py: Accounts per-build and per-process token/USD spend and stops the build when a ceiling is reached. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L1))
- change_manager.py: Manages the specs/ directory: change creation, the artifact DAG and its status, templates and instructions, and archiving with merges of delta requirements. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L1))
- code_analysis.py: Runs the code_facts explore agents over the project at HEAD and renders the checked facts to codebase-analysis.md, facts.json and per-block fact sections. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L1))
- config.py: Build pipeline configuration: the BuildConfig dataclass, tier detection, reading multiplai.conf and specs/config.yaml, model and effort resolution, and reference and standards docs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1))
- env.py: Compatibility shim that re-exports the env and model helpers from multiplai_core.env. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/env.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/env.py#L3))
- gates.py: Pure-code quality gates between pipeline stages (baseline/RED/integration tests, test integrity, review scores, prototype, unknowns, docs freshness), plus parsers for the agents' report slots. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1))
- git_ops.py: The single home of every git/gh/skill subprocess the pipeline runs: inspection, worktree/branch lifecycle, commits, guarded discard, push and PR. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L1))
- intake.py: Saves the build's inputs (ticket, plan, interview, research) into the change dir and runs the INTERVIEW and RESEARCH phases, returning exit 3 while it waits on a human or the wrapper. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L1))
- models.py: Pydantic models and enums for all structured pipeline data: phases, board columns, review results and policy, mutation results, block state. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L1))
- orchestrator.py: The main /buildme pipeline: runs the phases from bootstrap through publish, with checkpoint-based resume, worktree management and PR publishing. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1))
- progress.py: Writes a tail-able, timestamped markdown progress file (build-progress.md) that external monitors can read. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L1))
- rubric.py: Detects the change type from keyword counts and generates the rubric.md that code review gates score against. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L1))
- sdk.py: Buildme's wrapper around multiplai_core's agent runner: four LLM call patterns, the repo trust gate, a concurrency limit, budget recording and the error taxonomy. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L1))
- spec_generator.py: Generates the OpenSpec artifacts in dependency order and runs the single-pass audits (tasks shape, unknowns gate, design audit). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L1))
- state.py: Hierarchical build state (BuildState, SpecGenState, TDDState, FindingsState) with atomic JSON checkpointing and resume. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L1))
- stop_record.py: Records what the git tree held when a step stopped, and checks the tree against that record before a resume keeps or discards anything. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L1))
- tdd_engine.py: Block-by-block TDD build engine: parses tasks.md, runs test-writer/implementer/refactorer agents behind RED, integrity, integration, review and mutation gates. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1))
- merge.py: Read-only git checks and the merge-agent call for the epic coordinator's merge of origin/<default> into a child's worktree. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:1-2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L1))
- records.py: The coordinator's per-attempt started/result JSON records and logs under agents/, plus marker parsing. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:1-2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L1))
- review.py: What the coordinator reads to review a child's PR: review-skill argv, the ledger, run locks, blocking findings and the round/final_fix/park decision. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:1-2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L1))
- store.py: The epic directory's location and files (epic.json, split.md, plans/), their models, parsing, atomic writes and approval hashes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L1))
- __init__.py: Package marker for the `findings` command, which fixes PR review comments through buildme's spec and TDD process. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/__init__.py:1-2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/__init__.py#L1))
- decisions.py: Writes buildme-decisions.json choosing which re-review findings `--auto` posts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L1))
- fetch.py: Reads a PR's open comments through `gh` and marks which authors are allowed and which bodies look like injection. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L1))
- loop.py: The resumable findings round loop: fetch, triage, spec, code, publish, review, with wip refs and stop records. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L1))
- models.py: Pydantic models passed between findings steps: PRComment, Verdict, Triage, TriageRecord, SpecChange. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py#L1))
- triage.py: Sorts each allowed comment into a verdict with a read-only agent and writes findings-r<N>.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L1))
- spec_steps.py: An empty file that marks llm_steps as a package. Its step module is spec_steps.py. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L1))
- spec_steps.py: LLM steps that write spec artifacts, run the explainer and the read-only design and tasks audits, and run codebase analysis. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L1))
- spec_steps.py: An empty file that marks prompts as a package; spec_steps imports prompt modules from it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:18`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L18))
- findings.py: Prompt templates for PR-comment triage, writing the spec delta, and self-checking the fix commits. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L1))
- review.py: The block code-review prompt, built partly from vendored text, and the orchestrator's prompt for adjudicating findings. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L1))
- budget.py: The per-walk spend ledger, with a circuit breaker and a $50 default ceiling, plus the per-process SPEND marker. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L1))
- config.py: Loads the run configuration (concurrency, model and effort per stage) from walkthrough.yaml, then multiplai.conf, then defaults. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py#L1))
- fetcher.py: Fetches vendor doc pages over httpx, checking every redirect hop against the SSRF guard and capping the response size. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L1))
- gates.py: Code-only checks for citations, partition, coverage, doc quotes, traces, written paragraphs, the glossary and sections. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L1))
- models.py: Pydantic models for repos, the target, boundary entries, agent answers and the checkpointed WalkState. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:166-169`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L166))
- netguard.py: The SSRF guard: rejects URLs that are not http(s) or that resolve to non-public addresses. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py#L1))
- orchestrator.py: Prepares the run directory and drives every stage in order, saving a checkpoint after each, with resume support. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:203-204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L203))
- partition.py: Splits the target's files into explore units of bounded size, using code_facts.partition. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L1))
- run.sh is the single POSIX-sh launcher every hook goes through. Its argument is either a hook script path or `--warm`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:2-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L2), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L73))
- run.sh looks for uv on PATH first. If that fails it falls back to $HOME/.local/bin/uv, because hooks run under a non-login shell. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:76-80`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L76))
- With no uv, a session hook prints an 'install uv' warning at most once every 24h (tracked by a marker file) and exits 0. `--warm` instead exits 1. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:122-123`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L122), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:133`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L133), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:146`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L146))
- To check whether the environment is ready, run.sh asks uv to import multiplai_core with `--frozen --no-sync`. It does not guess the venv path. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:150-153`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L150))
- `--warm` runs `uv sync` synchronously and exits with uv's status. On success it clears the `.warmup` marker no matter who owns it. On failure it records the exit code, but only if this run owns the marker. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:175-186`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L175))
- Warm path: if the environment is ready, run.sh execs the hook script under `uv run` before it looks at any marker. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:194-196`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L194))
- Cold start: whichever hook wins an atomic `mkdir .warmup` spawns a detached `sh -c` builder. The builder writes its own pid, runs `uv sync` into .warmup.log, and on failure leaves a `status` file holding the exit code. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:237`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L237), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:253-264`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L253))
- Marker triage: a `status` file means the build failed, and the marker is reaped after 15 minutes. A dead pid means the builder died, also reaped after 15 minutes. Any marker older than 60 minutes is reaped regardless. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:221-227`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L221))
- While the build is running or has failed, only context_manager.py and session_start.py print the notice to stdout. Every hook prints it to stderr, then exits 0. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:279-284`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L279))
- session_end, pre_compact, session_stop and session_notification save their stdin payload. A detached subshell polls every 2s, up to 300 times, then replays the hook. If the environment never comes up it logs a 'dropped' line to .warmup-deferred.log. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:293`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L293), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:304-313`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L304))
- Once .warmup-deferred.log passes 256 KiB it is rotated by an atomic `mv` to `.1`, so only one old copy is kept. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L101), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L110))
- synthesize_now.py splits each per-day diary file into `## Session:` blocks, which carry session id, timestamp and cwd. If the timestamp won't parse, it falls back to the file's mtime and then to now. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:43`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L43), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:76-84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L76))
- _scan_diary keeps blocks newer than the lookback window (48h by default), groups them by the project that resolve_project returns for the block's cwd, and drops blocks with no project. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L33), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:125-128`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L125))
- _summarize_project asks the model client for 3-5 bullet lines, with thinking/effort kwargs from lib. On any exception it logs and falls back to an extractive summary of truncated snippets. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:186-192`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L186), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:198-201`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L198))
- _write_summary writes `now_dir/{project}.md` atomically (tmp file, then os.replace). The header records the generated time, writing session, host, source diary files and project path. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:255-265`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L255), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:274-277`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L274))
- _writer_hostname takes the hostname from the session registry entry, rejecting path-like session ids. Otherwise it uses the local hostname, and if both fail it returns ''. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:217-226`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L217))
- main() drains stdin (where a hook payload may arrive, and is ignored) and reads `--project NAME` / `--project=NAME` from argv. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:340-345`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L340))
- GeneratorBase is a template method. run() loads state and the existing catalog, discovers sources, generates entries for changed sources, prunes deleted ones, checks for lost hand fields, and writes the catalog plus state unless this is a dry run or the client is a stub. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:296-310`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L296), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:334-335`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L334))
- A source is skipped only if its sha256 matches the stored hash AND an entry for it already exists, and only when force is off. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:168-171`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L168), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:453-458`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L453))
- Entries are generated concurrently, bounded by an asyncio.Semaphore of config.catalog_concurrency. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:400-408`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L400))
- If one entry fails, the previous entry is kept and the old hash restored, or the hash is deleted if there was none, so that source is retried on the next run. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:427-435`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L427))
- _call_llm retries only on status codes 429/500/502/503. It makes up to 3 retries with backoff of 0.1*2^attempt seconds; any other error is raised at once. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:30-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:629-635`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L629))
- State lives in a shared `.generation-state.json` in the catalogs dir, keyed by generator name. It stores last_run, source_hashes and entry_count. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:157-160`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L157), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:525-530`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L525))
- hand_field_losses compares the old and new catalogs for each preserved field. A field that becomes empty, whitespace or missing counts as lost. Each loss is logged as HAND_FIELD_LOST and added to result.errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:238-263`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L238), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:320-322`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L320))
- config.py defines the CatalogConfig dataclass. __post_init__ quietly resets invalid values to defaults or clamps them (keep_ratio to (0,1], qmd_candidate_limit at most 50, and so on). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:120-121`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L120), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:138-141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L138))
- load_catalog_config reads each option through multiplai_core.plugin_options helpers, each with its own default. It logs one INFO line naming the options that were not set (names only, never values). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:193-199`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L193), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:178-183`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L178))
- The dispatcher runs generators one after another in the fixed order memory, banks, diary, skills. memory and diary always run, banks runs only when shared banks exist, and skills runs only if enable_skills is on. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:31-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:61-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L61))
- If one generator raises, the dispatcher logs it and records an error GenerationResult, then carries on with the rest. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:159-161`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L159))
- Dry runs get a _StubModelClient (is_stub=True) instead of a real client. A real run calls create_client(component="catalogs") and falls back to the stub on any exception. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L141), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:168-173`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L168))
- MemoryGenerator catalogs every *.md file in the memory dir except CLAUDE.md, writing memory.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:104-105`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L104), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:135-137`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L135))
- Section anchors are only requested for files of at least 2 KB with at least 3 H2 sections. The code extracts the H2 names and gives them to the model as a closed list. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:68-69`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L68), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:206-208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L206))
- merge_entry copies hand-authored fields over from the existing entry. It then validates the model's section_anchors against the real H2 names (matched case-insensitively, rewritten to the file's spelling, deduplicated), drops invalid ones, and logs a warning if none survive. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:249-253`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L249), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:181-184`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L181))
- BanksGenerator builds banks.json without any LLM call. It adopts each shared bank's committed catalog.json fragment, re-keyed to `<bank>/<file>`. Files the fragment doesn't cover get deterministic derived entries. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:158-166`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L158), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:271-279`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L271))
- derive_entry builds a summary from the file's first prose paragraph (capped at 320 chars), with empty topics/keywords and `derived: True`. It adds anchors or H2 section names but no intent_domains. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:100-107`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L100), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L67))
- BanksGenerator runs collision detection over personal plus bank entries. It writes banks.json atomically, writes bank-collisions.md when collisions exist, deletes a stale report when there are none, and emits a log_event warning. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:288`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L288), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:325-334`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L325))
- generators/__init__.py only holds the package docstring. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/__init__.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/__init__.py#L1))
- fleet.py builds the fleet view by reading three existing stores: the session registry under sessions/*.json, each session's checkpoint.md, and an optional live_containers.json. It only aggregates: it makes no LLM or network calls. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:7-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L7), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:19-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L19))
- collect() reads the roster, the tmux pane map and the viewed markers once per pass. It then calls load_agent for every sessions/*.json, sorts agents newest first (ties broken by session_id), and computes collisions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1082-1096`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1082), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1113-1119`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1113))
- load_agent builds one Agent from a registry entry. It reads the branch from .git/HEAD on disk (no subprocess). If the session has a checkpoint, it fills intent, next_action and files from the checkpoint's 'Current intent', 'Next action' and 'Involved files' sections. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:941-951`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L941), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:723-727`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L723))
- _status_of maps a registry entry to one of working | waiting_input | idle | ended. An 'end' event means ended. A roster that is allowed to judge the entry overrides the clock. Otherwise an entry quiet for IDLE_AFTER_HOURS becomes idle, and a notification event means waiting_input. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:834-844`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L834))
- Agent.group decides which heading an agent is listed under. Disposition wins over liveness: parked sessions go under 'Parked', done or ended sessions are not listed, and the rest go under 'Needs you', 'Working' or 'Idle' by status. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:164-172`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L164))
- find_collisions reports files that two or more agents in the Working or Parked groups both list, heard from within COLLISION_MAX_AGE_HOURS. Paths are keyed by (repo identity, path relative to the checkout), so the same file in two worktrees of one repo collides. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1053-1062`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1053), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1005-1013`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1005))
- write_fleet_view writes AGENTS.md and fleet.json atomically into the data dir. Before writing fleet.json it reads the existing one so sections this pass did not collect are carried forward. It also deletes the retired fleet.txt. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1645-1657`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1645))
- carry_forward keeps the prs/repos/jobs/backlog/scheduled sections from the previous fleet.json, but only if they carry a collected_at stamp no older than CARRY_FORWARD_MAX_AGE (1 hour). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1570-1583`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1570), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1499`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1499))
- roster_dead_sids returns the session ids the host's container roster has observed to be gone, past a 1-hour grace period. It skips parked entries. gc_stale uses the result. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:572-585`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L572))
- render_agents_md writes a counts header, a section per group except Idle, a Collisions section, and the extra sections that are only present when the expensive collectors ran. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1288-1307`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1288))
- fsio.atomic_write writes to a mkstemp temp file in the same directory, chmods it to 0644, then os.replace()s it into place. On any failure it removes the temp file and re-raises. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py:36-48`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py#L36))
- fsio.claude_config_dir returns $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, or ~/.claude when the variable is unset or empty. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py:21-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py#L21))
- hook_input.read_hook_input parses the hook's stdin as JSON. Any read error, decode error or non-object payload returns {}. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py:21-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py#L21))
- job_effort maps each background job to a '<job>_effort' plugin option. Values not in KNOWN_EFFORTS log a warning and are ignored. effort_kwargs returns {} when nothing is set, so the effort keyword is left out of the model call. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py:42-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py#L42))
- learnings_ledger splits learnings files into '## Session Learnings' blocks. Each block is keyed by a 16-hex-character SHA-256 of a normalized projection with the label markers stripped. Dream then consolidates only blocks whose key the ledger has not seen. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:134-143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L134), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:20-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L20))
- The ledger is a JSON file at the 'dream' skill state dir/ledger.json. save() writes it with fsync and os.replace, and load() returns an empty ledger if the file is missing or corrupt (fail-open). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:202-204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L202), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:214-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L214))
- record() marks blocks as consolidated into a proposal, storing file, proposal and timestamp. prune() drops keys whose source file no longer exists. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:337-343`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L337), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:354-361`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L354))
- memory_router offers token_overlap (the default), llm, llm_hybrid and embeddings strategies, chosen by the memory_router plugin option. Each router's select_multi returns {corpus: [names]} for the memory, skills and resources corpora. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:194-202`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L194))
- TokenOverlapRouter scores each entry by the IDF-weighted overlap of its intent_domains with the prompt (plus the last response), plus a keyword boost capped at KEYWORD_CAP. anti_domains exclude an entry outright. An entry can only be picked if it matched at least MIN_DOMAIN_MATCHES distinct domain tokens or a verbatim domain phrase. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:617-632`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L617))
- _apply_policy returns nothing if the best eligible score is below MIN_SIGNAL (2.0). Otherwise it keeps entries scoring at least max(MIN_SIGNAL, keep_ratio × top), up to max_files. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:396-401`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L396))
- LLMRouter makes one model call for all corpora. The default model is claude-haiku-4-5 with a 25s timeout covering client creation too. If the call times out, fails, or returns non-JSON, it raises RouterCallFailed and falls back to the token_overlap router. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:896-907`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L896), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L64))
- HybridRouter lets the LLM pick, then keeps only picks whose file is in token_overlap's eligible set. The dropped picks are logged as HYBRID_GATE. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:1095-1103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L1095), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:1126-1129`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L1126))
- memory_write_floor.floor_check is a post-verdict veto on a memory write. It checks, in order: shared-bank write, unsafe filename, reserved filename (CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md), missing change verb, non-'add' verb, empty text. None means the write may proceed. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:224-240`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L224))
- path_refusal checks the resolved destination on disk. It refuses symlinked targets and paths that resolve outside memory_dir, and re-checks reserved names after resolving. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:265-278`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L265))
- project_identity.resolve_project maps a cwd to a project name. It tries, in order: configured project_roots (longest match wins), umbrella_roots (→ 'workspace'), then the detection strategy (git by default, falling back to the cwd basename). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:122-149`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L122))
- project_identity shells out to git with a 3-second timeout, using --git-common-dir so linked worktrees collapse onto the main repo name. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:97-101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L97), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L39))
- rejections appends one JSON line per item the judge dropped to rejections.jsonl, using O_APPEND and fsync. rotate() trims the file to its newest MAX_RECORDS (5000) lines through a temp file and rename. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py:128-133`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py#L128), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py:66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py#L66))
- routing_validation.validate_proposal parses a drafted dream proposal and produces warning lines from three checks: section existence (H2–H4) and new-section collision (H2 only); 8-gram verbatim duplicates against memory plus extra corpus; and Jaccard near-duplicates against corpus lines and within the batch. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:538-544`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L538), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:663-669`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L663))
- The routing gate only warns. append_routing_warnings appends a '## Routing Warnings' section, which reads '(none)' when the proposal is clean. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:701-708`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L701))
- parse_proposal_entries reads the '## Updates for `file`' blocks and their '### N.' entries into dicts with target, number, title, section, change, source, provenance, kind and text. Action items (### A{N}.) are skipped. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:208-224`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L208))
- runtime.run_supervised starts the child in its own session (start_new_session=True). On timeout it SIGKILLs the whole process group, reaps the child, and re-raises TimeoutExpired with whatever output was captured. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py:114-134`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py#L114))
- runtime.lock_path puts cross-session locks under <data_dir>/locks/<name>.lock on the shared workspace filesystem rather than /tmp, which is local to each container. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py:144-149`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py#L144))
- section_loader resolves 'file.md#Section' picks to the matching H2 section, ignoring H2 lines inside code fences. If the section is not found it returns the whole file, and a repeated H2 name returns all its bodies joined together. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py:180-188`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py#L180), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py:71-79`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py#L71))
- session_registry.record_event does a locked read-merge-write of sessions/<sid>.json. It refreshes hostname, in_container and last_event on every call, keeps keys it does not own, and on a 'start' event clears the disposition. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:250-260`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L250))
- record_disposition stores {state, reason (truncated to 500 chars), ts} under the 'disposition' key. It refuses to create an entry that does not already exist. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:326-331`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L326))
- gc_stale deletes ended entries after 7 days, other entries after 30 days, and roster-confirmed-dead entries straight away. It never deletes parked entries or entries with a pending or in-flight extraction marker. It re-checks staleness under the entry's flock before deleting, then sweeps orphaned .adopt/.lock/.tmp-* files. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:552-566`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L552), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:47-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L47))
- taxonomy defines closed sets for the two axes: five provenances and four kinds. It also defines strength and breadth rankings used when merging entries, and a read-only mapping from the legacy single 'type' field. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:74-82`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L74), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:168-175`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L168))
- taxonomy.parse_pair reads 'PROVENANCE/KIND' using the first whitespace-separated token of each half. Values outside the closed sets come back as None. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:275-280`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L275))
- thinking.py decides the `thinking=` config for the plugin's mechanical model calls (extraction, checkpoint writing, the memory doctor's duplication check, now-summaries, catalog generation). Extended thinking is off by default at these call sites. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L3))
- Disabling thinking means sending the config {"type": "disabled"}. Each subsystem has its own plugin option key, held in a `*_OPTION` module constant. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L68), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:79-88`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L79))
- thinking.py checks whether the installed multiplai-core accepts `thinking=` by reading the signature of ModelClient.query or run_agent with inspect. A callable that takes **kwargs counts as supported. If introspection fails for any reason, the target counts as unsupported. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:138-152`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L138))
- core_supports_thinking caches the probe result per target in the module-level dict _SUPPORT_CACHE, and logs one warning per target when core does not accept `thinking=`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:164-178`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L164))
- thinking_kwargs returns {"thinking": copy-of-config} or {}. The caller splats it into the model call, so the keyword is left out entirely when it cannot or should not be sent. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:244-245`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L244))
- timeparse.parse_ts turns an ISO string into a timezone-aware UTC datetime. It converts a trailing 'Z' to +00:00 and returns None for empty or unparseable input. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py:23-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py#L23))
- timeparse is kept as a leaf module so that the session-hook fast path (lib.fleet) can parse timestamps without importing fleet_sources and its subprocess-running collectors. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py:4-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py#L4))
- transcript_distiller shrinks Claude Code JSONL transcripts into compact text for LLM extraction without making any LLM call. It keeps user/assistant turns, stubs tool results, truncates long tool inputs, and drops thinking and image blocks. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:6-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L6), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:80-87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L80))
- transcript_distiller formats each turn as `[ts] [project] role: text` and packs turns into chunks of at most token_budget*4 characters (default 32,000 tokens). A single turn longer than the budget is truncated. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:22-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L22), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:231-235`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L231))
- distill_slice also returns last_ts, the newest timestamp it actually put into a chunk. Callers use it as a resume bookmark. Taking it from the same pass means a turn appended between two reads cannot be lost. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:211-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L211))
- transcript_distiller names a turn's project after the last component of its cwd, falling back to 'unknown'. It streams the file line by line, and any OSError simply ends the stream. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:121-125`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L121), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py:114-118`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/transcript_distiller.py#L114))
- utilisation.py records which memory sections were injected into each session, together with two separate usage estimates: a self-report from the session and an offline judge. The two are never blended into one number. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:17-18`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L17))
- utilisation data is stored in <data_dir>/utilisation.jsonl. Every write rewrites the whole file atomically (mkstemp, then fsync, then os.replace) and serialises records with sort_keys. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L91), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:244-251`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L244))
- Every read-modify-write of the utilisation file (upsert_session, compact) runs under an exclusive flock on a lock file from lib.runtime.lock_path. If the lock cannot be taken, the write goes ahead unlocked. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:284-290`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L284), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:333-344`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L333))
- injected_from_inject_events turns `context`/`inject` activity events into `injected` entries using sections_by_file and bytes_by_file. An empty section list means the whole file was injected. When one file had several sections picked, its bytes are split evenly between them and each entry records co_picks. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:381-395`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L381))
- inject_events_for_session reads the rotated activity-*.jsonl logs (and the legacy activity.jsonl) oldest first. It matches sessions on the first 8 characters of the session id and pre-filters lines on the substring '"inject"'. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:427-441`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L427))
- aggregate folds session and totals records into SectionStat counters. Judge verdicts carrying a judge_instrument stamp count toward the judge estimator; unstamped verdicts go into separate legacy_judge counters. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:768-773`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L768))
- rank splits sections into ranked and insufficient (fewer than MIN_OBSERVATIONS=5 estimator observations). Ranked rows are sorted worst first: zero estimated use comes first, then the highest bytes per estimated use. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:867-871`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L867))
- build_table returns the JSON contract that later consumers read. It carries a disclaimer, coverage counts, judge_instrument notes, ranked sections, insufficient_data, and never_retrieved together with a sufficiency flag that requires at least 10 sessions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:990-993`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L990))
- compact collapses session records older than 90 days into a single `kind: totals` record stamped judge_instrument_stamped=True. Records with no parseable timestamp are kept. dry_run reports the counts without writing anything. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:1239-1243`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L1239), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:1257-1267`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L1257))
- render_table produces a human-readable markdown table. It marks estimator disagreements with `!` and zero-use rows with an infinity symbol, and lists insufficient-data, never-retrieved and whole-file-only sections separately. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:1106-1110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L1106))
- The fleet_sources package holds the expensive collectors (git, gh, directory walks), which are kept off the hook path. Each one must never raise, must be bounded, and must report what it cannot know as unknown instead of zero. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/__init__.py:17-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/__init__.py#L17))
- backlog.collect_backlog counts non-blank learning lines, dream proposals matching processed-learnings-*.md, pending and failed extraction files, and top-level INBOX files. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:106-116`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L106))
- common.run is the single subprocess wrapper. It sets stdin=DEVNULL, a timeout (default 5s) and check=False, and turns not-found, timeout and OSError into Ran(ok=False, err=...). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:57-72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L57))
- common has a JSON cache in <data_dir>/fleet_cache.json with a 300s TTL. cache_write serialises its read-modify-write with an advisory flock on a .lock sidecar and writes through lib.fsio.atomic_write_json. Failures are only debug-logged. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:82-87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L82), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:133-138`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L133))
- git_repos.find_repos walks up to 3 levels under the workspace and descends into repos to find nested checkouts. It skips .worktrees, node_modules and any dot-directory. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:94-101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L94))
- git_repos._load runs git status, remote, rev-parse, for-each-ref and worktree list for each repo, stopping early once a 10s per-repo budget is used up. collect_repos runs repos in a thread pool of 8 workers. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:208-212`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L208), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:232-233`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L232))
- jobs.collect_jobs reads $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/jobs/<short>/state.json and daemon/roster.json. It judges a job stale from the state file's mtime (more than 6h old), never from a pid, and returns jobs newest first. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:130-141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L130))
- prs.collect_prs runs `gh pr list --json` for each repo slug on 16 workers with a 6s timeout. It sorts each failure into no_access (a standing permission fact) or errors. If every repo fails with an auth error, it sets available=False and clears the errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:196-204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L196), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:238-240`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L238))
- prs._rollup reduces statusCheckRollup to one word, pessimistically: any failure means 'failing', then any pending check means 'pending', then success means 'passing'. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:152-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L152))
- prs.stacks groups PRs into merge-ordered chains, where one PR's base is another's head in the same repo. The chain that can merge first comes first, a cycle guard stops loops, and a lone PR is not reported as a stack. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:323-340`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L323))
- code_facts is a shared package used by codebase-walkthrough, review and buildme. Callers pass in their own model-call function and path resolver; the package supplies the fact models, unit slicing, prompts and citation gating. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/__init__.py:3-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/__init__.py#L3))
- explore.check_citation resolves a citation's path through the caller's resolve function, then checks the quote with multiplai_core.citations.check_quote, which re-reads the cited lines from the commit with git show. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:76-81`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L76))
- explore.gate_answers rebuilds the gated output from the stored answers. It drops any fact with no passing citation (recording it as Dropped), assigns ids f1.. and c1.. in order, and keeps conventions only when asked to. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:191-194`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L191), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:211-216`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L211))
- explore.bounded runs async tasks under a semaphore inside a TaskGroup and re-raises the first underlying exception, not the ExceptionGroup, so callers can catch it by type. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:52-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L52))
- models.py defines the pydantic models. Agent answers (Citation, Fact, FilePurpose, Term, ExploreOutput) ignore unknown keys. Citation rejects line_end < line_start. The input models are Span, Scope, Unit and Dropped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py:21-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py#L21), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py:39-43`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py#L39))
- partition groups spans by parent directory in sorted order and fills units up to `limit` lines. A single span over the limit becomes its own unit flagged oversize. Unit ids are u1, u2, and so on. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py:36-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py#L36))
- prompts.explore_unit builds the explore prompt. It uses module wording when every span is a whole file and line-range wording otherwise, adds a re-ask focus list for files that were missed, and includes the conventions section only when asked. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py:64-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py:84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/prompts.py#L84))
- lib/__init__.py has no code, only a docstring. It says the generic core modules (paths, config, log_utils, model_client) now live in the separate `multiplai_core` package, and that this package holds the plugin's context-specific modules. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/__init__.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/__init__.py#L3))
- bank_collisions.py rests on one rule: a fact lives in exactly one bank. If two banks claim the same domain, that is treated as a defect in the corpus and reported when the catalog is built. It is not resolved when content is injected into a session. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:3-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L3))
- find_collisions runs three checks, cheapest first, and only between entries from different banks: (1) the same filename, compared case-insensitively; (2) at least MIN_SHARED_DOMAINS (2) shared normalised intent_domains, or one entry's domains wholly contained in the other's; (3) the same H2 heading across banks, checked only when `texts` is passed. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:138-142`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L138), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:157-164`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L157), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:177-184`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L177))
- An entry's ref is taken from the first non-empty field among source, path and file. Its bank comes from an explicit `bank` field, or else from the ref's prefix, defaulting to the personal bank. Items are sorted with personal first so the output order is stable. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:84-97`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L84), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:134`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L134))
- bank_collisions.py never edits anything. to_json produces the catalog's `collisions` key and render_report produces a markdown report, grouped by kind, that suggests `/memory-bank adopt` or narrowing intent_domains. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L28), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:205-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L205), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:220-223`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L220))
- bank_git.py runs every git and gh call through `_run`. `_run` resolves the executable with shutil.which, runs it with a timeout and check=False, and turns a missing tool, a timeout, an OSError or a nonzero exit into GitResult(False, ...). It never raises. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:64-85`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:7-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L7))
- Bank sync is `git pull --ff-only --quiet`, because a bank checkout is a consumer copy and a merge commit made by a background hook would never be resolved. A failed pull leaves the previously synced content in place (contract C4). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:135-139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L135), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:10-12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L10))
- The bank write path is stage_commit, which runs `git add -- <pathspec>` (never -A) and then commits. push_branch then pushes with --set-upstream, and open_pull_request runs `gh pr create`. Both use a 120s timeout, and the PR URL is returned in `output`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:142-154`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L142), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:157-158`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L157), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:176-186`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L176))
- banks.py re-exports what a bank is from multiplai_core.banks (config shape, names, modes). It adds what the plugin does with banks: resolving refs to files, reading the sync TTL, and rendering shared-bank content inside a fence. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:3-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:38-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L38))
- Memory refs are written as `file.md` for the personal bank and `<bank>/file.md` (optionally with `#Section`) for any other bank. split_ref removes the bank prefix before the section fragment, because a section name may contain '/'. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:12-14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:148-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L148))
- Shared-bank content is treated as untrusted because other people wrote it. render_shared_block puts an attribution heading above the content and wraps it with multiplai_core.untrusted.fence. SHARED_BANK_NOTICE tells the model that this content is data, not instructions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:199-204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L199), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:85-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L85))
- configured_banks returns Paths.resolve().memory_banks() and never raises. If that fails it logs a warning and returns only the personal bank. If resolving the memory dir also fails, it returns (). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:112-120`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L112))
- checkpoint.py implements a five-step lifecycle without calling an LLM: Measure the context tokens from the transcript, write a Checkpoint when a token band is crossed (via a detached writer), mark a Handoff (advise /clear), Rebuild at the next SessionStart, and Retire the checkpoint after the diary is written. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:26-27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L26))
- All checkpoint state is kept under <data_dir>/checkpoints/: per-session checkpoint.md, state.json and writing.marker files, plus pending/<project>.json handoff markers. state.json is read leniently ({} if missing or corrupt) and written atomically. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:36-41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L36), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:305-318`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L305))
- read_context_tokens scans the last 512 KB of the transcript backwards for the newest main-chain assistant usage record and sums input, cache-read and cache-creation tokens. If it finds no usage record at all, it doubles the window up to twice. Sidechain records are skipped. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:64-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:377-385`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L377), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:433-437`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L433))
- A checkpoint fires on three triggers. 'band' fires when a new token band is crossed. 'refresh' fires when the session has grown refresh_tokens (default 25K) since the last checkpoint, at any token level. 'stale' fires when the session is at least min_session_minutes old and its last checkpoint is at least stale_hours (default 0.5h) old, or it has never checkpointed. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:495-501`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L495), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:583-597`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L583))
- Only one checkpoint writer runs per session at a time, enforced by writing.marker; a marker older than 600s is treated as orphaned. spawn_writer launches checkpoint_writer.py detached (start_new_session=True), sends the payload as JSON on stdin and does not wait for it. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:601-607`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L601), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:648-657`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L648))
- A pending handoff marker is named `<project>__<host>.json`, or the legacy `<project>.json` when no hostname is known. Project and host are taken from the session's own registry entry before the current process's cwd or environment is used.
