([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:767`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L767), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:814-818`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L814))
- consume_pending_marker claims the marker with an atomic os.replace to a `.claimed-<sid8>` name. It throws away markers older than ttl_hours, puts back a marker whose session id matches the claiming session (unless allow_same_session is set), and always deletes the claim file in a `finally`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:894-898`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L894), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:911-919`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L911))
- retire_checkpoint deletes the session's directory with shutil.rmtree. It refuses while a writer is in flight or while a pending marker still points at the session, and returns (removed, reason_kept). sweep_checkpoints runs at SessionStart: it expires markers past their TTL, then collects directories with no activity for gc_days (7), up to 500 per run. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1044-1055`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1044), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1189-1205`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1189))
- autocompact_trigger_tokens reproduces Claude Code's native auto-compact formula from environment variables. It returns None when compaction is disabled or when the configured window is below the 200K fire gate. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1290-1308`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1290))
- A checkpoint counts as valid when at least 6 of the 11 MiMo H2 sections are present. build_rebuild_context wraps the checkpoint in REBUILD_PREAMBLE and REBUILD_SUFFIX to produce the SessionStart additionalContext. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1332-1338`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1332), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1359-1367`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1359))
- checkpoint_drain.py queues one JSON marker per session in pending_checkpoints/ (with a queued_at stamp added) and drains the queue through extraction_drain._launch_queue. Failures are quarantined in failed_checkpoints/, and a marker is requeued instead of launched while writer_inflight is true for that session. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py:58-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py#L58), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py:93-107`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py#L93))
- conflict_edits.py parses learning lines in both the taxonomy format and the legacy format. For each conflict candidate (provenance CORRECTION or EMPIRICAL, or legacy CORRECTION / trust: verified) it finds the single best-overlapping line in the target memory file, using Jaccard overlap of at least 0.35, and emits a supersede edit. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:58-65`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L58), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:142-144`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L142), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:306-312`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L306))
- content_words lowercases the text, removes stopwords, markdown emphasis and arrows, and keeps inline code spans as ordinary words. overlap_sets returns 0.0 when either side has fewer than 4 content words. Edits are sorted with the highest confidence first and rendered under `## Conflict Resolutions`. If there are no edits, the result is an empty string. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:206-209`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L206), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:225-228`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L225), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:333-334`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L333))
- screenable_lines is the shared line filter for 'does this already exist' scans. It skips structural lines, everything inside fenced code blocks, and lines of 40 characters or fewer, and returns (lineno, text, frozenset(words)). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:258-269`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L258))
- costing_collector.py walks $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/projects/**/*.jsonl, prices each assistant message via multiplai_core.costing, and appends one ledger record per API call. Byte offsets kept per file make each pass incremental, and a global msg_id set prevents billing a message twice. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:3-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:412-416`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L412))
- Within one pass, collect_file keeps the streaming snapshot of a message with the largest output_tokens, while keeping the attribution of its first occurrence. It stops at a torn last line (no trailing newline) so that line is read again next pass. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:352-360`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L352), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:250-252`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L250))
- SpanTracker attributes each cost record to a Skill, Agent/Task or Workflow by watching tool_use blocks and `<command-name>` slash commands. All spans close when the user next sends a real text prompt. Records inside nested spans get confidence 'approx', and subagent files are classified by their path layout. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L89), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:193-196`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L193), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:122-129`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L122))
- run_collect refreshes the pricing table first (soft dependency, never raises), appends records unless dry_run, saves the offset state and returns statistics that include fallback-priced models. run_backfill_branches rewrites the monthly ledger-*.jsonl files in place, adding branch/cwd and preserving any concurrent appends. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:71-79`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L71), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:444-455`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L444), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:545-560`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L545))
- dream_triage.py decides which proposed memory items may be applied without a human, in three layers: a code rubric over (provenance, kind), a model judge that can only lower the outcome, and a code write floor that can only refuse. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:18-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L18), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:39-41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L39))
- rubric_verdict: RULE and INTENTION always go to review. CORRECTION and DECLARATION apply for FACT and DECISION. EMPIRICAL and RESEARCH apply only for FACT. INFERENCE, or any unknown provenance, goes to review. A missing provenance is read as INFERENCE and a missing kind as RULE. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:199-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L199), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:185-190`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L185))
- classify parses the proposal's `### N.` entries and puts every item in review, tagging each with floor, rubric and routing-warning reasons, or 'unjudged' if it has none. apply_verdicts then folds in the judge verdicts keyed by (target, number) through _decide. That order is: redundant/drop wins, no verdict means review, judge doubt means review, the rubric must permit, FACT items with caution ≥1 need a supported citation, and the floor has the last word. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:559-570`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L559), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:651-654`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L651), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:695-706`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L695))
- reconciled_pair takes the more cautious of the extractor's label and the judge's label, separately for provenance and for kind, and reports whether they disagreed. reconciliation_detail records which side won each half. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:234-246`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L234))
- dream_triage.py produces three outputs: render_receipt, a markdown audit trail (Applied / Rejected / Left for you, with git revert instructions); render_summary, a short console block; and rejection_records, records for the rejection log. Items refused because they target a shared bank are pulled out by shared_bank_items so they can be sent as bank pull requests. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:810-813`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L810), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:723-725`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L723))
- extraction_drain.py dequeues markers by atomically renaming them from pending_extractions/ to processing_extractions/ and then touching them to refresh the mtime. It throws away markers it cannot parse. Stale processing markers (1800s) are requeued up to MAX_ATTEMPTS=3 and then moved to failed_dir. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:185-203`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L185), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:59-60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L59), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:125-137`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L125))
- _launch_queue is the launch loop shared by both queues. For each claimed marker it spawns the script detached with a JSON payload on stdin. If the launch fails, the marker is requeued. Once a child exists, a failure leaves the marker for stale recovery. With wait=True it counts children that exited nonzero. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:293-300`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L293), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:303-305`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L303))
- process_deferred_extractions sends extract_learnings.py the transcript path, not its contents, together with session_id, cwd, trigger and marker_path. The child deletes the marker when it succeeds. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:350-366`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L350))
- board.py is a state seam: it never renders a board or talks to a board service. It maps the build phase to a kanban column, writes the result to specs/changes/<name>/.board.json and prints a `BOARD:<slug>:<Column>` line on stdout. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:3-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L3))
- The `_PHASE_COLUMNS` table maps every BuildPhase to a BoardColumn. Bootstrap through spec generation map to Shaping, design audit through review to Planning, TDD_BUILD/DOCS_UPDATE/RESPEC/PUBLISH to In Development, COMPLETE to In Review and FAILED to Cancelled. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:91-93`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L91), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:116-118`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L116))
- `_record` writes the card atomically: it writes a `.tmp` file, then calls `os.replace`. It appends a history event only when the column changes, and on a move it prints the BOARD line and logs the move to the progress file. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:259-263`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L259), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:265-273`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L265))
- The branch, worktree_path and pr_url on the card are refreshed from the live BuildState on every call. They are never re-read from .build-state.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:255-257`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L255))
- `board_path` follows the change into the archive. When the active change directory is gone, it uses the newest archived directory that holds a .board.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:170-182`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L170))
- budget.py tracks per-build tokens and cost in a module-level singleton `BuildBudget`. A separate `ProcessSpend` total counts everything the process spent and is not cleared by `reset()` or `load_state`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:253-256`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L253), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:12-15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L12))
- `BuildBudget.check` raises BudgetExceededError, with a diagnosis attached, once a configured ceiling reaches 100%. At 80% it warns once: to the log, to stdout as `BUDGET:WARN:<pct>:<usd>`, and to the progress file as a BUDGET_WARNING section. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:187-193`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L187), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:194-199`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L194))
- `to_state`/`load_state` save the budget counters into the build checkpoint and restore them, so a resumed build keeps its earlier spend instead of starting with a fresh budget. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:225-230`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L225))
- `spend_marker` builds the `SPEND:<usd>:<tokens>` line that `build` and `findings` print on exit. It adds `:incomplete` when some skill's spend was unknown. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:272-276`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L272))
- change_manager.py manages the specs/ tree: changes/<name>/ for active changes, registry/ for the merged per-capability requirements, and archive/<date>-<name>/ for completed changes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:6-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L6))
- `ARTIFACT_DAG` is the hard-coded dependency graph of artifacts: proposal, use-cases, requirements, design, unknowns, tasks, rubric. `artifact_status` marks each artifact DONE, READY or BLOCKED from the files that exist on disk. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:96-97`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L96), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:351-361`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L351))
- `archive_change` merges the change's delta requirements into registry/ (unless merge_specs=False), then moves the change directory to archive/YYYY-MM-DD-<name>/ with shutil.move. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:452-461`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L452))
- `_apply_delta` handles the ADDED, REMOVED and MODIFIED sections of a requirements delta, in that order. ADDED is appended, REMOVED deletes matching `### Requirement:` blocks, and MODIFIED replaces them using a lambda, so backslashes in the text are inserted literally. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:503-513`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L503), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:554-555`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L554))
- `artifact_context` gathers the inputs for generating one artifact with an LLM: the template, the instruction text, the `context` key from specs/config.yaml, paths to its dependencies, the output path and the registry capability names. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:419-426`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L419))
- code_analysis.py produces checked, cited facts about a project's code using the shared `code_facts` package. It powers the CODEBASE_ANALYSIS phase (writing codebase-analysis.md and facts.json) and the TDD engine's per-block facts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:1-14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L1))
- `analyse` reads source files at HEAD and ranks them: files the spec names first, then the most imported. It fits them into at most `max_units` explore units, where one unit can be a slice of manifests and config files. It runs explore agents 4 at a time and keeps only facts whose citations match the commit. A unit whose call fails is recorded in `failed` and the other units continue. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:387-393`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L387), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:423-429`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L423))
- `write_facts_file` writes facts.json atomically, via a .json.tmp file and replace, in review's facts.v1 format (`kind: tree`). Its base_sha is git's empty tree. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:533-539`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L533), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:516-521`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L516))
- `block_facts_section` picks the saved facts that cite files a block names and re-checks each citation against the worktree's current HEAD. Facts with no surviving citation are dropped, and the header reports how many were dropped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:572-581`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L572))
- config.py defines `BuildConfig`, the full configuration for a run. `from_cli_args` builds it in a fixed order: tier detection, specs/config.yaml, a second tier detection, CLI flags, the BUILDME_REVIEW_MODEL env var, re-derivation of the per-step fallbacks, and finally test-command discovery. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1087-1103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1087), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1122-1131`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1122))
- `model_for(step)` resolves a step's model through a 6-link chain: models.<step>, models.<stage>, the per-stage field, models.default, `model`, then multiplai-core's default_model(). `effort_for` resolves effort with the same kind of chain. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:984-998`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L984), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1027-1033`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1027))
- `detect_tier` labels a run advanced or standard from the model that will actually run. Fable models, and Opus at version 4.5 or later, are advanced. A non-Anthropic model can be pinned with BUILDME_TIER or `tier:` in config, and is standard otherwise. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:223-238`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L223), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:209-212`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L209))
- `standards_text` builds the reviewer's standards block from two sources: the CLAUDE.md chain covering the changed paths, then any `standards_files`. Each document is capped at 12000 chars using `summarize_reference_doc`, which cuts on section boundaries and always includes a full index of the sections. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1597-1604`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1597), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:81`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L81))
- `reference_docs_text` inlines the stack and framework reference docs from $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/reference/dev/ into the spec-generation prompt, each capped at 24000 chars. `_announce_references` prints `REFERENCES:` once per process. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1802-1825`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1802), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1795-1799`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1795))
- `detect_frameworks` looks for Django (manage.py or a pyproject/requirements dependency), FastAPI, and React or Next (package.json dependencies) to choose extra reference docs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1691-1702`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1691))
- `discover_test_command` picks the test command from the first root file found, in this order: pytest.ini, pyproject.toml, setup.py, Package.swift, Cargo.toml, go.mod, package.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:668-676`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L668))
- env.py has no logic of its own. It re-exports multiplai_core.env helpers (load_env, resolve_model, pick_effort, ...) so existing `from .env import` call sites keep working. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/env.py:3-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/env.py#L3))
- gates.py holds pure-code quality gates. Each returns a GateResult whose `action` hint (fix_tests, spawn_fix_agent, restore_tests, halt_build, ...) tells the pipeline how to recover. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1))
- `run_test_suite` runs the repo's test command with shlex.split and no shell. If the repo is not trusted (BUILDME_TRUST_REPO), or the command times out or is missing, it returns exit code None, which callers must read as 'could not verify'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:71-91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L71))
- `red_gate` passes only when the suite exits non-zero with a real behavioural failure signature (AssertionError, FAILED, ...). If the suite passed, the action is `rewrite_tests`. If collection or syntax errors show up, the action is `fix_tests`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:261-272`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L261))
- `unchanged_tests_gate` compares sha256 hashes of the test files, taken when the RED gate passed, with their current hashes. A change with no `TEST CHANGE REQUIRED:` declaration fails with `restore_tests`. A declared change passes but is flagged for the reviewer. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1007-1009`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1007), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1040-1048`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1040))
- `parallel_command` adds `-n auto` to a pytest command only when parallel is 'auto', the arguments have not already chosen workers, xdist will load, and a `python -c "import xdist"` probe succeeds in pytest's own environment. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:171-182`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L171))
- `review_score_gate` treats spec compliance as a hard floor. After that it fails on rubric dimensions at or below the critical score, then on a weighted average (scores discounted by reviewer confidence) below `min_weighted_average`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:878-887`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L878), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:909-914`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L909))
- `unknowns_gate` fails with `regenerate_unknowns` when a detected new dependency has no `## <dep>` section in unknowns.md, or when its Edge cases or Assumptions list has no filled bullet. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1152-1157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1152))
- `prototype_gate` requires a prototype directory containing NOTES.md and at least one other artifact file, with the PROVES, DISPROVES, OPEN_QUESTIONS and STATUS slots present and PROVES filled in. If the agent reports BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT, the action is `prototype_blocked` and the stage is not retried. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:484-493`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L484))
- git_ops.py is the only module that runs git or gh. Every call is a fixed argv list with shell=False. Nothing in it merges, rebases, force-pushes or deletes a branch; the one exception is the guarded `discard_to`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:1-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L1))
- `_run` never raises for a missing binary or a timeout. It returns a GitResult with return code 127 or 124. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:100-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L100))
- `_run_skill` starts skill subprocesses (pr, review, walkthrough, spec-extract) in a new session. On timeout or interrupt it SIGKILLs the whole process group, and it adds the skill's `SPEND:` line to the process spend total in budget.py. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:191-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L191), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:226-236`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L226))
- `discard_to` refuses unless the target sha is an ancestor of HEAD and no commit since that sha touches files outside the project directory. It then runs a soft reset, a restore of `.`, and a clean that excludes the bookkeeping files. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:923-929`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L923), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:949-960`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L949))
- `resolve_new_branch` picks a free `buildme/<change>` branch and worktree path, trying suffixes -2 to -50. It raises GitLifecycleError if an existing branch has commits not on base, or if that can't be determined. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:436-455`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L436))
- `open_pr_with_skill` runs the pr skill and reads its stdout: the `PR:<url>` line gives the PR URL and `PR_DIAGNOSIS:` lines give diagnoses. Push and PR failures never raise. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:1000-1008`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L1000))
- intake.py saves everything a build starts from as files in specs/changes/<name>/ (ticket.md, plan.md, interview.md, interview-questions.md, research.md, research-request.md, split-proposal.md), so the spec-stage commit puts them on the build branch. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:3-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L3))
- Ticket text, ticket replies and research are treated as untrusted. They are wrapped in an <untrusted-content> fence through multiplai_core.untrusted.fence; fenced() joins the fence lines into one string. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:19-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L19), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:215-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L215))
- INPUT_FILE_FLAGS lists four input flags. Each row gives the args attribute, the CLI flag, the phase that reads the file and the name of the copy saved in the change dir. resolve_input_paths turns relative paths into absolute ones against --project-dir, because the process cwd is the scripts directory. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:69-74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L69), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:85-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L85))
- check_input_files reports a missing input file only while its phase is still ahead and no copy exists in the change dir. Otherwise it logs, clears the flag and lets the build use the copy. The orchestrator exits 2 when it returns an error. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:116-123`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L116), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:85-88`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L85))
- config_commit_state shells out to `git ls-files --error-unmatch` and `git status --porcelain` to classify specs/config.yaml as committed, uncommitted or no-repo. Any OSError or SubprocessError reads as no-repo. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:163-180`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L163))
- run_interview_phase supports three interview modes. `ticket` asks on the ticket and may return WAITING_EXIT. `self` runs a questioner and an answerer, and a failure there is non-fatal. `user` writes interview.md from the wrapper's --interview-summary. Every mode then advances to INTERVIEW_DONE, prints PHASE:INTERVIEW:COMPLETE and records the board. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:393-411`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L393), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:430-434`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L430))
- In ticket mode the build writes interview-questions.md, sets state.interview_waiting, prints `INTERVIEW:WAITING:<path>` and returns exit 3. A re-run that brings --interview-replies renders the questions and the fenced replies into interview.md. A questioner exception fails the build: state.fail, a board failure record, and exit 1. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:524-533`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L524), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:502-509`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L502))
- render_interview puts answers whose grounded_in is empty under '## Assumptions', never under '## Answers'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:312-325`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L312))
- run_research_phase handles research in this order. A --research-path is copied into research.md. An existing research.md is reused. Otherwise one decide_research call runs: needed=false writes the reason into research.md, needed=true writes research-request.md, prints RESEARCH:NEEDED and returns WAITING_EXIT. If the decision call fails, a note is written and the build continues. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:575-585`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L575), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:594-604`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L594))
- models.py holds the pydantic models and enums for all structured data in the pipeline: BuildPhase, BlockStatus, BoardColumn, the review models, mutation results, BlockInfo, and so on. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L1))
- ReviewScore.effective_score shrinks a score toward a neutral value in proportion to confidence (neutral + confidence*(score-neutral)), rather than multiplying it, so a low-confidence reviewer does not look harsher. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:145-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L145))
- ReviewGatePolicy is the single source of the review thresholds: min_weighted_average 3.5, critical_score 1.0, neutral_score 3.5. specs/config.yaml code_review.gate can override them. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:116-132`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L116))
- ReviewResult.passed_with requires both verdicts: spec compliance (no missing and no misunderstood items) and the score threshold with no failing dimension. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:275-280`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L275))
- FindingAdjudication.accepted_indices treats a finding the adjudicator gave no verdict on as ACCEPTED, so a silently dropped finding is never lost (fail-safe). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:207-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L207))
- orchestrator.run_orchestrator is the main entry point. It re-binds to an existing worktree, finds or creates the BuildState, then runs the phases in order, each guarded by state.is_phase_complete: BOOTSTRAP, INTERVIEW, RESEARCH, CODEBASE_ANALYSIS (+spec-extract), EPIC_SPLIT, SPEC_GENERATION, DESIGN_AUDIT, PLAN_REVIEW, the size gate, PROTOTYPE, REVIEW, TDD_BUILD, DOCS_UPDATE, RESPEC, archive, PUBLISH. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:32-44`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L32), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:110-115`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L110))
- The orchestrator talks to the SKILL.md wrapper through stdout markers such as PHASE:<X>:COMPLETE, WORKTREE:, BRANCH:, PR:, RESULT:SUCCESS and BUILD:COMPLETE:. It also records each transition on the board through board.record. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:162-163`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L162), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:474-476`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L474))
- Error handling in run_orchestrator works like this. SystemExit, KeyboardInterrupt and CancelledError are recorded as an interruption (the checkpoint is re-saved, the state is not marked FAILED) and re-raised. Any other exception logs, posts a board failure if the build had not yet succeeded, and returns 1. A BUDGET summary is always written in the finally block. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:485-502`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L485), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:503-508`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L503))
- _run_bootstrap creates the build's own git worktree and branch first, through _setup_worktree. It refuses with ConfigNotCommittedError when specs/config.yaml is uncommitted, or differs from the version at --base-ref. It then creates specs/ and the change dir, saves the intake files, and writes a stub config.yaml if none exists. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1099-1107`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1099), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1131-1139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1131))
- _run_spec_extract_step runs the spec-extract skill as a subprocess inside CODEBASE_ANALYSIS and commits the registry files it applies. The step is skipped in only mode, in epic children, when spec_extract is false, when the repo is not trusted, or when the registry is not empty. Exit 5 (locked) returns 1. A failed run that leaves a dirty registry also returns 1. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:965-978`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L965), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1014-1017`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1014))
- _run_publish pushes the branch and opens the PR by running the pr skill (`pr_pipeline open --no-push`) through uv. It never raises. It returns False with a manual-commands diagnosis when there is no origin remote, the push fails, or the PR skill fails. The PR URL is checkpointed immediately. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1678-1697`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1678), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1729-1733`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1729))
- The size gate (_run_size_gate) is not a BuildPhase. It re-runs on every resume until PROTOTYPE is behind the build. When the plan should be split it writes split-proposal.md, prints SPLIT:REQUIRED:<path> and returns 3, until --accept-size is given (the acceptance is remembered in state.size_accepted). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1193-1210`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1193))
- The DOCS_UPDATE phase stages only the paths the agent reported that resolve to real files inside the project (_docs_paths). Files it changed but did not report are only warned about, never committed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:766-775`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L766), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:693-705`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L693))
- Codebase analysis is skipped when the project has no source files. _has_source_files walks the tree and skips vendor and spec dirs. A successful analysis is written to config.codebase_analysis_path and recorded in spec_gen.codebase_analysis_path. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:875-898`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L875))
- progress.py's ProgressWriter appends timestamped (UTC) markdown entries to build-progress.md. initialize() never truncates: an existing file gets a RESUMED section, a new one gets a header plus LAUNCHED. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:59-72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L59), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:139-140`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L139))
- progress.py trims what it writes: evidence keeps the last 15 lines, a diagnosis the first 40 lines, and spec-impact surprises the first 20. A `contradicts` spec impact gets a WARNING heading. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:98`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L98), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:120`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L120))
- rubric.py classifies the change as frontend, backend, fullstack or infra by counting keyword regex hits in proposal, design, tasks and requirements/*.md. It then generates rubric.md with one llm_call using RUBRIC_PROMPT. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:58-67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L58), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:100`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L100))
- sdk.py wraps multiplai_core.agent_runner.run_agent in four call patterns. It adds a repo trust gate, a concurrency semaphore (10 calls at once), budget accounting (including partial usage from failed calls) and the LLMCallError taxonomy. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:3-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:47-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L47))
- Any tool-using call (agent_call, or llm_call with allowed_tools) needs BUILDME_TRUST_REPO=1/true/yes (set by --trust-repo). Without it the call raises RepoTrustError, because the agents run in bypassPermissions mode with prompts built from the repo's specs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:93`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L93), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:157-158`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L157))
- The structured calls retry on validation failure (max_retries=1 by default). The retry appends the error and the JSON schema to the prompt. When retries run out they raise LLMCallError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:298-306`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L298))
- spec_generator.run_spec_generator creates the OpenSpec artifacts in ARTIFACT_DAG order: proposal, then use-cases, requirements and design, then unknowns, tasks, rubric. It then advances to DESIGN_AUDIT and runs the audit stage. It returns 0 or 1 and catches all exceptions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:5-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L5), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:73-76`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L73))
- _generate_all_artifacts loops over cm.ready_artifacts. It skips ids already in spec_gen.completed_artifacts and raises RuntimeError on a deadlock. Iterations are capped at len(ARTIFACT_DAG)*2. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:88-104`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L88))
- Each audit (tasks shape, unknowns gate, design audit) follows the same pattern: findings drive exactly one regeneration, then an optional report-only re-check, with no loop. The regeneration is committed with _commit_change_dir. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:947-955`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L947), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:614-616`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L614))
- The deterministic scans always run, even when the LLM audit fails. scan_placeholders flags TBD, TODO and similar text. scan_no_behaviour_blocks flags tasks.md blocks that produce nothing and say they add no code, because such blocks would fail the TDD RED gate. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:629-632`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L629), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:960-961`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L960))
- _generate_unknowns writes unknowns.md with one explainer per new dependency. The calls run concurrently with asyncio.gather(return_exceptions=True), and a failed explainer becomes a placeholder section the gate will catch. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:528-540`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L528))
- state.py's BuildState is the root checkpoint, serialized as JSON. checkpoint() writes atomically: a temp file in the same directory, then fsync, then os.replace. It also snapshots the process's budget spend unless keep_budget is passed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:299-315`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L299))
- BuildState.complete() checkpoints and then renames .build-state.json to .build-state.complete.json. Resume never looks for the completed file. The orchestrator uses it to refuse re-running a published change without --force. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:351-356`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L351), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:49-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L49))
- SpecGenState and TDDState hold boolean flags for steps that leave no proof on disk: tasks_audit_done, explainers_done, design_audit_done, plan_review_done, refactor_all_done, mutation_done and others. On resume these flags, not file existence, decide whether a step re-runs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:35-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L35), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:96-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L96))
- FindingsState and FindingsRound track post-build PR review-comment rounds through the steps fetch, triage, spec, code, publish and review. Each step is added to `started` before it runs and to `finished` after. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:107-113`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L107))
- stop_record.py records the tree (HEAD plus a fingerprint for each dirty path) when a step stops. others_work later compares the current tree with that record and returns a one-line refusal reason, or None when the resume may keep or discard the tree. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:45-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L45), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:102-105`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L102))
- others_work refuses in these cases: git cannot list changes; there is no record but there are dirty paths; a path differs from the record; HEAD moved to a commit that is not on origin_ref. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:106-117`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L106))
- tdd_engine.py is the block-by-block test-first build engine. It parses tasks.md into blocks, runs TDD cycles for each block, gates each block on integration tests and quality reviews, and exits with 0 (success), 1 (build failure) or 4 (agent timeout). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:357-361`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L357))
- parse_blocks splits tasks.md on `## N. Name` headers. It reads a `Satisfies:` line, an `Interfaces:` section with `- Produces:`/`- Consumes:` bullets, and either checkbox tasks (standard format) or a free description (advanced format). A missing file returns [] with a warning. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:452-460`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L452), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:487-494`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L487))
- run_tdd_engine configures the budget, loads or creates BuildState, builds TDDState from tasks.md, runs a baseline test gate once, then loops over the blocks. For each block it runs run_block_tdd and then _run_integration_and_review, followed by a whole-change refactor, mutation testing and an optional entry-point check. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2139-2148`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2139), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2189-2204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2189), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2210-2217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2210))
- run_block_tdd stamps a pre-block baseline commit (the empty-tree SHA in a repo with no commits). It then runs the test writer, the static and LLM test-quality gate, and the RED gate, then the implementer, then an optional refactorer. Each phase is committed as `<phase>(block-N): name`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1100-1105`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1100), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:104-108`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L104), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1190-1193`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1190))
- The static weak-test scan fails a block when 20% or more of its test functions match weak patterns, or when it finds no `def test_` at all. A failing scan goes to an LLM auditor; if the auditor confirms it, the test writer gets one retry, and weak tests after that fail the block. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:676-679`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L676), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:879-883`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L879))
- When the RED gate passes, the engine stores sha256 hashes of the block's test files. unchanged_tests_gate later compares against these in each writable window. A change that the agent declared (flagged) passes, but its claim is recorded and the baseline is reset to the new hashes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:923`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L923), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1422-1431`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1422))
- If the integration gate fails, up to 3 integration-fix attempts run, and the last one is 'escalated' to a different model step. Then up to 3 review iterations run: each review is adjudicated, recorded, and scored, and an implementer fix follows a failing score unless it was the last iteration. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1476-1478`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1476), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1592-1593`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1592))
- _adjudicate_review_findings has an LLM judge accept or reject each reviewer finding. Only accepted findings reach fix agents; rejected ones go in rejected_findings with a REJECTED reason added to their evidence. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:772-786`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L772))
- A block refactor is verified by the integration gate plus unchanged_tests_gate with no report, so the TEST CHANGE REQUIRED escape hatch is not available to the refactorer. A refactor that fails verification is discarded with git_ops.discard_to, and the block itself is not failed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1353-1356`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1353), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1370-1372`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1370))
- Fix on pass: when a block passes review, one extra fix pass runs on its accepted findings from a clean checkpoint commit. The fix is kept only if the tests stay green and the block still passes review; otherwise the tree is rolled back while keeping the review round records, and the findings are written to unfixed-findings.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1789-1797`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1789), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1727-1733`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1727))
- Mutation testing (cosmic-ray, Python only) runs after the whole-change refactor and refuses to run on a dirty tree. If mutants survive, one test-writer round runs, and that round is discarded if it edited any non-test file. mutation.md is written and committed, and the step fails below mutation.min_score. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2824-2829`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2824), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2902-2905`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2902))
- The engine prints machine-readable `BLOCK:` lines to stdout when a block completes or its review is exhausted. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1682`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1682), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1653`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1653))
- epic/merge.py supplies what the coordinator's merge step reads and runs. Every git command runs on the child's worktree root <W>, and only run_merge_agent writes anything (through an agent). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:4-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L4))
- merge.plan uses `git merge-tree --write-tree` (which changes nothing) to split the files into those the agent may resolve (version-field-only conflicts, CHANGELOGs, lockfiles) and refused conflicts. It returns None when git cannot tell. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:182-185`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L182), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:290-300`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L290))
- not_resolutions checks a merge result against all three sides. Version values must match _expected/_next (when both sides raised the version, take the next one after main's). CHANGELOGs must interleave both sides' lines in order. Lockfiles must load with no duplicate keys and hold only keys and values from either side. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:381-385`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L381), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:505-508`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L505))
- merge.ready decides whether <W> may be merged into. It checks for uncommitted work first, then whether HEAD is the reviewed head (or that head plus accepted merges of origin/<default>), then origin. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:630-631`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L630))
- The merge step's test command comes from `tdd.test_command` in <P>/specs/config.yaml, falling back to discover_test_command. suite_command adds `-n auto` through gates.parallel_command when it is allowed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:768-775`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L768), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:786`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L786))
- epic/records.py writes one started record and one result record (both JSON), plus a log, for each agent or subprocess the coordinator runs, under agents/<NNN>-<kind>-<child>. A started record with no result is an interrupted attempt. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:4-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:90-95`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L90))
- records.finish reads the marker lines from the attempt's log (WORKTREE/BRANCH/PR/SPEND and any WORD:WORD line except progress lines) and writes the exit code, markers, the last PR URL and the spend to result.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:146-150`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L146), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L34))
- epic/review.py runs the review skill as a `uv run ... python -m review_pipeline` subprocess and reads its ledger file (<data_dir>/reviews/ledger.jsonl) without importing review_pipeline. Unreadable ledger lines are skipped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:55-56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L55), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:86-92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L86))
- review.decide picks what follows a review with blockers: `round`, `final_fix` or `park`. An open HIGH gets another round, and parks once the cap is reached. A MEDIUM gets a round unless the verifier rated its impact harmless (under worth `breaks`). At the cap, MEDIUMs alone get one final fix. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:205-208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L205), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:192`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L192))
- review.lock_held tests whether a review is running by briefly taking a non-blocking shared flock on `<parent>/.locks/<name>.lock`. If the lock file cannot be opened, the lock counts as not held. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:131-139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L131))
- epic/store.py owns the epic directory `<data_dir>/buildme/epics/<owner>-<repo>--<epic>/`, which holds epic.json (the pydantic Epic model), split.md (a table of children) and plans/<id>.md. Approval records sha256 hashes of these files, and changed_file detects later edits. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:127-130`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L127), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:306-309`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L306))
- The table in split.md is the source of truth on approval: parse_split reads it back, so hand edits are used. read_children rejects an epic with fewer than two children. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:12-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:285-286`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L285))
- findings/ implements `python -m build_pipeline findings`, which fixes a PR's review comments through buildme's own spec and TDD process. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/__init__.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/__init__.py#L1))
- The findings loop runs rounds of the steps triage → spec → code → publish → review. Its state lives in <change>/.findings-state.json, and it stops on DONE (nothing new to act on), MAX_ROUNDS, or BUDGET (--max-usd). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:66-73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L66), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:707-712`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L707))
- _step records each step as started before it runs and finished after, saving a checkpoint each time. A triage or spec step that fails is kept under a wip ref and then discarded back to where it started, and a stop record is saved for the resume. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:236-247`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L236), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:210-214`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L210))
- In the code step, a block the TDD engine fails is kept under a wip ref, discarded, recorded as 'failed', and skipped, so the round carries on with the remaining blocks. A red baseline suite stops the step instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:516-528`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L516), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:511-515`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L511))
- Under --auto the review step writes buildme-decisions.json and posts through `review_pipeline post --decisions`. Without --auto it marks review_waiting and exits 3, so a human can post the findings and then re-run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:549-562`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L549), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:570-572`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L570))
- fetch.py reads, through git_ops._run_gh, the unresolved review-thread comments (GraphQL, paginated), the issue comments and the review bodies. It drops the pipeline's own replies and the pr skill's notices, and raises FetchError when any read fails. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:274-275`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L274), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:183-184`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L183))
- fetch.is_allowed allows a bot only when it is listed in findings.allow_bots. Other authors are allowed when they are on the configured list, or, under `collaborators`, when their role_name is admin, maintain or write (one cached API call per login). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:309-319`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L309))
- triage.py runs one read-only agent (Read, Glob, Grep) per allowed comment that does not match an injection pattern, and renders findings-r<N>.md with one row per finding id or comment across rounds. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:24-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L24), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L141))
- decisions.write_decisions chooses which re-review findings --auto posts: every listed Code finding, plus Tests findings on non-test source files, optionally filtered by severity and by code_only. Docs and Trim findings are never posted. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:77-84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L77), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:101-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L101))
- findings/models.py defines the pydantic data passed between the steps: PRComment (with an untrusted body that is shown only through fenced_body), the Verdict enum, Triage, TriageRecord and SpecChange. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py:72-75`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py#L72), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py:19-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py#L19))
- spec_steps.py holds buildme's LLM steps for writing spec artifacts and for the design and tasks audits. Spec generation goes through llm_call(). The audits run as agent_call() subagents with read-only tools. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L3))
- generate_artifact builds a prompt with _build_prompt and makes one llm_call. Its system prompt forbids tools, the model and effort come from the 'spec_artifact' settings, and spend is labelled spec:<artifact_id>. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:130-141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L130))
- _build_prompt picks a template by artifact_id: proposal, requirements, design, tasks or unknowns. Any other id raises ValueError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:163`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L163), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:225`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L225))
- Dependency artifacts are read from config.change_dir, and requirement files from change_dir/requirements/*.md. A missing file becomes a placeholder string such as '(not available)' or '(no specs yet)' instead of an error. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:234-237`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L234), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:247-249`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L247))
- When the context lists registry capabilities, a 'Current behaviour (specs/registry)' section is appended to the prompt. It holds the text of specs/registry/<name>.md files and is capped at 60,000 characters. Whole files are dropped from the end and named, with their requirement names, until the text fits. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:43-44`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L43), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:291-294`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L291))
- The design and tasks audits run through _audit_agent_text with the read-only tools Read, Grep and Glob and at most 30 turns. A failed subagent is only logged as a warning, and its partial output, or an empty string, is returned. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:35-39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L35), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:81-83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L81))
- run_design_audit reads proposal.md, design.md, tasks.md and the requirement files, and detects the change type with rubric.detect_change_type. It parses the agent's text as a JSON list of gaps and returns [] when the text is not JSON. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:362-363`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L362), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:382-384`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L382))
- run_tasks_audit checks that tasks.md is split into vertical slices rather than layers. It keeps only dict findings and logs a warning for anything it drops. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:388-391`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L388), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:419-424`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L419))
- run_explainer writes one dependency's section of unknowns.md. It is allowed research tools (WebSearch, WebFetch, Read, Glob, Grep) and up to 12 turns. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:332-338`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L332))
- run_codebase_analysis hands off to code_analysis.analyse with a call function that charges all spend to 'codebase_analysis'. It writes facts.json next to the report and returns rendered markdown. A missing project directory gives '(new project — no existing code)', and a project with no commit gets no_commit_report. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:474-475`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L474), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:484-490`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L484))
- findings.py holds three prompts for `build_pipeline findings`. TRIAGE_PROMPT runs one read-only agent per PR comment, SPEC_DELTA_PROMPT makes one toolless call per round, and SELF_CHECK_PROMPT reviews the round's fix commits. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L3))
- Triage must pick exactly one verdict: code_fix, spec_change, question or not_defect. The comment body sits inside an <untrusted-content> fence and is treated as data. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:39-42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L39), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:23-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L23))
- CODE_REVIEW_PROMPT is built at import time by joining the project's own text with vendored blocks from Anthropic's pr-review-toolkit. It asks for JSON holding strengths, missing/extra/misunderstood, scores, issues and findings. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:3-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:12-18`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L12))
- Reviewers report an issue only at confidence 80 or above. Anything less certain becomes a finding, and FINDING_ADJUDICATION_PROMPT has the main-model orchestrator accept or reject each finding by index. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:80-81`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L80), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:218-222`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L218))
- The walkthrough's budget module wraps multiplai_core's SpendLedger in WalkthroughBudget, whose default ceiling is $50. It also keeps a per-process spend tally, printed as SPEND:<usd>:<tokens>. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:31-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:63-65`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L63))
- load_config builds WalkConfig from multiplai.conf keys walkthrough_*, then lets walkthrough.yaml in the run directory override them. Defaults are concurrency 4 and max_turns 60.
