([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py:49-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py#L26))
- fetcher.fetch_url fetches vendor doc pages with httpx and follows redirects by hand, running the SSRF check on every hop. It allows at most 5 redirects, caps bodies at 5 MB, and retries timeouts, connection errors and 5xx after 1s and 3s. It never raises and returns a Fetched with an error string. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:23-27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L23), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:71-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L71))
- netguard._assert_safe_url accepts only http and https, rejects metadata hostnames, and rejects any resolved IP that is private, loopback, link-local, reserved, multicast or unspecified. IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses are unwrapped and checked too. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py:38`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py#L38), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py:55-60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py#L55))
- gates.py holds the walkthrough's code-only checks, none of which calls a model. A quote is checked against `git show <sha>:<path>`, never against what an agent read. citation_gate splits a workspace path into a repo and a path, then calls multiplai_core.check_quote. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:1-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:57-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L57))
- partition_gate requires every file to be in exactly one unit and no unit to exceed the line limit, unless the unit is a single oversize file. file_coverage_gate lists files over 30 lines that have no surviving fact. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:93-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L93), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:121-124`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L121))
- trace_gate checks each hop of a scenario. Hop 1 must contain the seed line. Each later hop must be called by name in the previous hop's quote, and its lines must either define that name or hold a boundary entry. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:188-195`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L188))
- write_gate rejects a paragraph that has an unknown [[cN]] id, a path:line not covered by a gated citation, a backticked identifier that ripgrep cannot find in the snapshots or docs, or a Mermaid label that is not a file, symbol or repo. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:265-272`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L265), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:242-243`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L242))
- models.py defines WalkState, the whole checkpointed run state, and the stage order. The stage field holds the last stage completed, and past() compares positions in STAGES. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:21-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L21), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:197-198`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L197))
- Every path an agent sees is workspace-relative: the repo key, a slash, then the path inside the repo. Gated citations get ids c<N>, and hop citations get s<scenario>h<hop>. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:214-215`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L214))
- The orchestrator runs repos → symbols → references → boundary → partition, which are code-only stages, then explore → docs → trace → write, which call agents, then render. It saves walkthrough-state.json after every stage so a run can be resumed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:197-200`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L197), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:248-249`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L248))
- The boundary stage drops symbol and reference entries whose quotes fail the gate and writes boundary.json to the run directory. It also snapshots every other repo that references the target. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:170-180`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L170))
- When the budget is exceeded, the orchestrator saves the ledger and state, writes a FAILED progress line, logs a budget_stop event and re-raises. A DocsError becomes a WalkError telling the user to resume with --docs or --no-docs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:234-240`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L234), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:245-247`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L245))
- partition.py asks code_facts to split the target's files into units of at most --unit-lines lines, then rewrites each path as workspace-relative. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/partition.py:18-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/partition.py#L18))
- progress.py extends multiplai_core's ProgressWriter with a single STARTED line. The skill session tails the file until it sees DONE or FAILED. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/progress.py:14-16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/progress.py#L14))
- prompts.py builds the prompts for the explore-boundary, docs, trace and write stages. Write agents must refer to code only by [[cN]] ids, must not type file paths or code blocks, and must not draw Mermaid diagrams. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py:116-124`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py#L116))
- render.py turns [[cN]] into path:line links whose title is the cited quote, and [[snippet:cN]] into code read with git show, capped at 40 lines. Links point at the GitHub blob URL when the remote is on GitHub, otherwise at a percent-encoded relative path. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:42-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L28))
- write_outputs writes <name>-walkthrough.md and, outside agent mode, <name>-walkthrough.json for the review-viewer. The JSON uses git's empty tree as its base, so every file in the module counts as changed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:504-511`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L504), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:368-370`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L368))
- sdk.agent_call_structured is the walkthrough's only way to call a model. It checks repo trust and the budget, runs run_agent with an allow-list of tools, records usage, and parses the answer into a pydantic schema. On failure it asks once more: an answer that did not parse gets a no-tools one-turn reformat, and a failed run gets the full prompt again. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py:82-83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py#L82), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py:145-147`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py#L145))
- state.py saves and loads WalkState as walkthrough-state.json using multiplai_core.checkpoint. The write is an atomic replace, and the read returns None when the file is missing or unreadable. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/state.py:15-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/state.py#L15))
- symbols.collect walks Python files with ast and records what the target defines (function, class, url, celery-task, management-command, model) and what it calls out to (import, http, setting, env, cache). Files in other languages only add a 'no symbol pack' warning. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py:11-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py#L11), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py:285-286`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py#L285))
- target.py resolves the target directory to a repo root, subpath and HEAD sha. It extracts a `git archive` snapshot that leaves out .env* files, binaries, skipped directories and files over 2 MB, then lists the target files and skips lockfiles, generated files and migrations. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:161-170`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L161), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:231-237`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L231))
- target.git refuses any git subcommand other than rev-parse, show, archive, ls-files and ls-tree. Every call is a fixed argv run with shell=False. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:66-67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L66))
- untrusted.py is the walkthrough's entry point to multiplai_core.untrusted.defang. is_fetchable_url rejects anything that is not http or https, contains whitespace or control characters, or is longer than 2048 characters. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py:46-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py#L46), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py:83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py#L83))
- Each agent stage is an `async def run_<stage>(state, ctx) -> state` that skips tasks already answered. Only RepoTrustError and BudgetExceededError get out of a stage. Any other agent failure is recorded in state.errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/__init__.py:3-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/__init__.py#L3))
- explore runs one agent per file unit and one per group of up to 60 boundary entries, split into inbound per repo, outbound, data and config. It gates the facts, then asks each task once more about files or entries still uncovered. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/explore.py:22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/explore.py#L22), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/explore.py:112-119`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/explore.py#L112))
- trace picks seeds in this order: webhook routes, routes called from other repos, Celery task names, then other routes, capped at --scenarios (default 6). A broken trace gets one retry. If it still breaks, the scenario keeps its hops up to the last good one and records stops_at. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py:35-38`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py#L35), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py:111-114`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py#L111))
- write runs one tool-less agent per section, with max_turns=2, on at most 60,000 characters of material. Paragraphs that fail write_gate get one rewrite. Whatever still fails is moved to the section's cut list. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py:186-188`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py#L186), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py:225`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py#L225))
- deepen's render_report.py renders a candidates JSON file into self-contained HTML using the Jinja template templates/report.html.j2. By default it writes to $TMPDIR/architecture-review-<ts>.html and prints the path, and with --open it opens the file in a browser. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py:36-46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py#L36), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py:64-67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py#L64))
- pr_pipeline's CLI has three subcommands: open, edit and render. Exit code 0 means done, 1 means a gate rejected the body, and 2 means refused or failed. Callers parse stdout lines such as PUSHED:, PR:, PR_EDITED:, DIFF: and PR_DIAGNOSIS:. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/__main__.py:3-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/__main__.py#L3))
- render_body discovers and parses the PR template and reads the sources. Only when a section needs prose or a checkbox needs ticking does it make one model call. It then runs the open gates, asks once more with the failure reason if they fail, and raises GateFailed on a second failure. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:75-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L75), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:87-106`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L87))
- cmd_open optionally pushes the branch, then runs `gh pr create` with a temporary body file that is deleted in a finally block. Draft is the default unless --ready is passed or pr.draft is false. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:188`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L188), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:216-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L216))
- On a body already managed by the skill, cmd_edit merges in only the marked sections it owns. On a hand-written body it puts the managed header first, keeps the existing text, appends missing sections, and posts a notice comment on the PR. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:343-349`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L343), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:360-361`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L360))
- The pr gates check four things without a model: template headings appear in count and order, checkbox lines are present, model-filled sections are non-empty and contain no markers, and the body is at most 2,500 words. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/gates.py:131-137`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/gates.py#L131))
- github.py makes every gh and git call through _run, which never raises. A missing binary gives a Result with code 127 and a timeout gives code 124. gh calls go through a separate _run_gh so tests can stub them. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py:58-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py#L58), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py:69-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py#L69))
- markers.py wraps each section the skill owns in `<!-- pr:begin key -->`/`<!-- pr:end key -->`. A 'written' marker pair lists every key the skill has ever written, so a later edit can tell a section a human deleted from one never written. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py:3-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py:47-48`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py#L47))
- template.py looks for a PR template in .github/, the repo root and docs/, matching names in any case. If none is found, or a template directory holds several, it falls back to the skill's standard template. parse splits Markdown into Sections and does not treat lines inside fences or HTML comments as headings. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:34-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L34), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:235-236`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L235))
- prompts/__init__.py holds the prompt blocks every review stage shares: the workspace description, dependency folders, the untrusted-content fence, citation rules, the diff, commits, files, the PR description and the GitHub branch-rule summary. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:55-56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L55))
- Text written by outsiders (the PR description, GitHub branch rules, explain-stage facts) is wrapped in an `<untrusted-content source=...>` fence, and the prompt tells the agent that it is data and not instructions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:12-15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:101-104`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L101))
- settings_block turns the base branch's GitHub rules into a verdict. With no required_status_checks rule, it tells agents that no CI job blocks a merge and that any claim that CI 'gates' a merge should be reported. When the rules could not be read, it tells them not to assume CI blocks. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:125-130`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L125), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:136-138`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L136))
- find.py builds one finder prompt per review dimension (diff-bugs, callers, history, conventions, tests, trim). A change review and a tree review get different task wording (DIMENSION_TASKS and DIMENSION_TASKS_TREE). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:71-72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L71), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:128-129`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L128))
- build() assembles a change-review prompt from a list of parts, drops the empty ones, adds the repository's rules only for the conventions dimension, and joins everything with blank lines. A tree target goes to _build_tree instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:231-233`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L231), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:248-250`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L248))
- facts_block lists the explain stage's checked facts with their fact and citation ids. Once the text would pass FACTS_MAX_CHARS (40,000), it stops and writes a '[... N more facts cut ...]' line. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L25), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:47-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L47))
- An extra finder (build_extra) gets only its own chunk of the diff and file list. It is told to list every HIGH or MEDIUM defect and never to read a whole file. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:288-291`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L288), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:314`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L314))
- Finders return JSON with `findings` (claim, severity, file, lines, failure_scenario, citations, dimension) and `needs`. A need is something the finder could not check, with a cause of `no-access` or `unreachable`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:171-182`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L171))
- The merge prompt sends a group of overlapping findings as JSON and asks for `duplicate_sets`, each holding at least two ids. Findings with no duplicate are left out. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py:10-12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py#L10), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py:33-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py#L33))
- The repeats prompt shows this round's findings next to findings the person rejected in earlier rounds, along with the person's notes, and asks which new findings repeat a rejected one. A finding does not count as a repeat if the cited code changed in a way that creates the defect anew. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py:30-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py:43-44`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py#L43))
- The verify prompt asks a fresh agent for status (confirmed, refuted or unverifiable), reason, citations, expected_behaviour, topic, impact and needs. The trim-specific meanings are added only when the finding is a trim finding. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:10-16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L10), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:117`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L117))
- IMPACT_RULES allow `breaks-users` or `breaks-business` only when three things hold: the path is reached under default or documented settings, the harm follows from this defect alone, and no other check stops it. Otherwise the impact is `correctness-only` or `hygiene`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:34-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L34))
- The review stages run in the order find → verify → merge → repeats → assess. Each is an `async def run_<stage>(state, ctx)` that returns at once if the state is already past it and skips items it already has results for. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py#L1))
- stages/__init__.py defines RunContext (config, snapshot path, diff, progress writer, target_dir, counts, gate_reasons) and the helpers relative_path and fix_citation. These strip the snapshot or repo root and leading './' from paths the agents write. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py:27-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py#L27), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py:42-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/__init__.py#L42))
- The merge stage groups confirmed and unverifiable findings with a union-find over `linked`. Two findings are linked when their anchors overlap in the same file, or any of their repository citations overlap, within MERGE_LINE_GAP = 2 lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:33-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L33), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:67-78`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L67))
- merge_set picks one finding to keep: a confirmed one before an unverifiable one, then the most severe. The kept finding takes the highest severity in the set, the deduplicated union of all citations, and every finder that reported any member. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:125-128`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L125), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:144`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L144))
- apply_duplicate_sets adds each merged-away finding to state.merged, with the id it was merged into and a reason, and removes it from state.findings. _record_merges then marks the find-stage check entries with fate 'merged'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:195-197`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L195), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:153-154`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L153))
- run_merge stores each group's answer in state.merge_answers as soon as it arrives, so a budget stop keeps the answers already paid for. It records one AgentCheck per group, with tool calls, turns and cost. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:241-243`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L241), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:226-230`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L226))
- run_verify asks a verifier for each finding that has no verdict yet, up to VERIFY_TRIES = 3 calls. A verdict that fails verdict_gate is turned into `unverifiable`, and the gate's reason is put in front of the verifier's own. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L55), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:176-180`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L176))
- After all verdicts are in, unverifiable findings are lowered one severity step and their original severity is saved in state.original_severity. If a finding is waiting on a need, it is not lowered below MEDIUM. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:117-123`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L117), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:217-221`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L217))
- Needs from an unverifiable verdict are passed through gated_need and added to state.needs, each blocking its finding, with source 'verifier'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:203-206`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L203))
- important_assessments labels a shown trim finding `important`, which takes it out of the list, when the test's behaviour is rated breaks-* or a citation falls under a `## Critical paths` glob. Findings merged into it count too. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:105-113`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L105))
- calls.py applies explore's dispatch rule to the group and write calls. A key is asked when it has no answer, or only an error answer, and fewer than MAX_ATTEMPTS tries, over at most two rounds. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py:32-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py#L32), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py:79-88`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py#L79))
- ask_one checks the budget, removes the old error answer, counts the attempt and saves before calling. CallFailed is stored as an `{"error": ...}` answer cut to 200 characters. Budget, backend and trust errors give back the attempt they counted, save, and are returned. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py:53-61`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py#L53))
- explore.run runs one code_facts agent per scope unit, 4 at a time, with read-only tools on the snapshot. It raises BudgetStopped when the budget stopped a task, and re-raises a backend or trust error. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:53-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L53), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:152-156`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L152))
- explore.finish writes facts.json in review's format (kind `tree`, base_sha set to git's empty tree) and report.md, rebuilding both from state in unit order so that a resumed run writes the same bytes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:186-191`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L186), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:218-221`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L218))
- The group step is a single tool-less call with one turn that sorts public symbols into capabilities. The Python gate then forces kebab-case unique names, puts every symbol in exactly one capability (unplaced ones go to `unassigned`), and splits any capability with more than 40 symbols by module. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:44-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L44), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:126-127`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L126))
- group.finish writes capabilities.json from the gate. It returns None (not grouped) when the group call has no answer or only an error answer. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:220-225`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L220))
- The write step makes one agent call per capability, labelled `write:<capability>`. The agent gets read-only tools on the snapshot, the capability's symbols, the facts about their files and the test files, and returns requirements with scenarios and citations. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py:106-109`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py#L106), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py:46-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py#L46))
- render.py writes registry/<capability>.md in buildme's format (## ADDED Requirements, ### Requirement:, #### Scenario:, WHEN, THEN, CITES, EVIDENCE), then report.md and summary.json. It then deletes snapshot/, removing the marker first. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py:36-43`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py#L36), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py:94-98`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py#L94))
- report.write_output is how every output file gets written: first to `<name>.tmp`, then os.replace'd over the target, so a stop leaves either the old file or the new one. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/report.py:33-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/report.py#L33))
- runlock.hold takes an exclusive non-blocking flock on `<repo>/.locks/<object>.lock` and writes the holder's pid, host and start time into it. If another process holds the lock, it raises Locked. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py:76-80`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py#L76), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py:83-87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py#L83))
- scope.compute takes the `.py` files at the head commit (under --path for a tree, or the changed files for a PR). It skips submodules, symlinks, binary files, files over 200,000 characters and linguist-generated or vendored files, collects public symbols with ast, and splits the files into units of 6000 lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:172-178`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L172), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:204-205`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L204))
- Test files (`test_*.py`, `*_test.py`, anything under tests/) are passed to the agents as evidence, but their symbols are never specified. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:89-91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L89), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:201-202`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L201))
- sdk.agent_call_structured is the only way spec-extract calls a model. It runs the agent, parses the answer into the schema, and if parsing fails asks once for a tool-less one-turn reformat. Any remaining failure ends as CallFailed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:219-228`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L219))
- sdk._run checks repo trust and the budget before every call. AgentRunError reasons that are setup failures become BackendUnavailable, other failures become CallFailed, and a partial result's usage is still recorded. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:165-166`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L165), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:179-186`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L179))
- state.py defines the State model saved as `<run>/state.json` through multiplai_core.checkpoint. It holds the target, steps_done, scope, explore, group and write answers and attempts, the budget, and apply_stop. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py:51-62`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py#L51), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py:92-93`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py#L92))
- target.resolve builds a Target from exactly one of --tree, --pr or --dir. For a PR it uses `gh pr view`, checks that both commits are in the clone, and computes the merge-base. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:252-254`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L252), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:295-298`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L295))
- target.run_folder chooses `<root>/<repo>/<object>/` while holding its lock. If a folder holds another target's run (another remote or another path), it moves on to `<name>@<owner>` or a hashed path name. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:401-405`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L401))
- target.snapshot writes the head tree's blob bytes into `<run>/snapshot/` (not via git archive) and writes a marker last. Symlinks that lead outside the tree are left out and named on stderr. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:626-635`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L626))
- import_dir copies a --dir source into `<run>/source/`, leaving out node_modules, .venv and similar, and commits it with a fixed author and the run's clock, so the same files at the same time give the same commit. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:456-463`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L456), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:497-498`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L497))
- video-edit's pipeline.py is an argparse CLI. Each subcommand (render, lint-overlay, sfx-suggest, capture, proof-card, prep, correct, proof, shots, timeline, frame, snap, cursor, check, outdir, review, music-sample, contact-sheet, prefs, make) maps to a cmd_* function that returns an exit code. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:488-492`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L488), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:650-651`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L650))
- cmd_render renders into a hidden `.<stem>.partial<suffix>` file and renames it over the output only when done. It removes the old `<stem>.edl.json` sidecar before the rename and writes the new one after, and a failed sidecar only prints a warning. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:62-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L62), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:73-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L73))
- cmd_review serves the job page until stopped. It turns SIGTERM and SIGHUP into KeyboardInterrupt so the server always closes and unpublishes its token files, and it never prints the token. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:403-415`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L403), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:389`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L389))
- gitdata.py gives the review viewer read-only git access. Every git call has a fixed argv, runs with no shell and with stdin closed. Network access happens only during target resolution: `gh pr view`, fetching a PR's refs, or `git fetch origin` when `fetch=True`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:4-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L4))
- gitdata.git() passes `-c color.ui=never -c core.quotepath=off`, removes GIT_EXTERNAL_DIFF and GIT_PAGER from the environment, sets GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0, and raises GitError carrying git's stderr when the exit code is not in `ok`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L92), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:96-108`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L96))
- Unified diffs are parsed by counting each hunk's body from the line counts in its `@@` header, never by looking at a line's first characters. A deleted `-- comment` line would otherwise look like a file header. parse_unified turns the diff into rows of kind ctx/add/del/gap. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:131-134`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L131), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:160-164`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L160))
- file_view builds the full-file view at head with the diff folded in. A binary file gets only a flag. A file deleted at head shows its first 120 old lines. A file over 4000 lines is cut down to its hunks (±3 lines) and the cited ranges (±12 lines), with gap rows in between. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:32-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L32), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:385-392`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L385))
- The file route serves only an allow-list of paths: changed files, finding files, citation and verifier-citation paths, and the files listed at head. Any other path raises PathNotInReview. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:245-257`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L245))
- definitions() handles go-to-definition. It checks the name against an identifier regex, builds a POSIX ERE (no GNU escapes, so it behaves the same on macOS), runs `git grep` at head, and keeps at most 50 hits that fall inside the allowed paths. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:263-265`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L263), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:305-307`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L305))
- parse_target sorts the user's text into a kind (pr, range3, range2, path, branch) without running git. An existing directory beats a range, because a path like `../other-worktree` contains `..`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:544-549`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L544))
- _resolve_pr reads PR metadata through `gh pr view --json`. If the head or base commits are not in the clone, it fetches `refs/pull/<n>/head` plus the base branch with no destination refspec, which writes only objects and FETCH_HEAD. The base is then merge-base(PR base commit, head). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:820-824`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L820), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:838-840`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L838))
- A directory target with an upstream reviews only the commits not yet pushed: the base is merge-base(upstream, HEAD) and the slug ends in `--unpushed`. If nothing is unpushed, it raises TargetError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:879-888`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L879))
- review_findings finds every review's latest findings.json under a reviews root, in three layouts: the 0.46 `<root>/<slug>/`, the current `<root>/<repo>/<object>/`, and a 0.42 `<root>/<repo>/<object>/<slug>/`. It skips `legacy`, `.locks` and `.partial-` folders. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:603-609`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L603))
- A tree review is a Target whose base_sha is git's empty tree. tree_target lists every file at the commit (optionally under a path) and builds a slug of the form `<repo>--tree[-<sub>]`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:942-946`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L942), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:966-969`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L966))
- pr_status makes one `gh pr view` call with a 6-second timeout and returns check counts, mergeable, draft and review decision for the page header. summarize_checks counts SUCCESS/NEUTRAL/SKIPPED as passed and FAILURE/ERROR/CANCELLED/etc. as failed. Anything else, including a run whose status is not COMPLETED, counts as pending. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:743-751`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L743), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:491-493`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L491))
- mailbox.py keeps message files beside a review: inbox.jsonl (page→session), outbox.jsonl (session replies), decisions.json and viewed.json (replaced atomically), and server.json. Each file has a single writer, and rows are appended with one write() on an O_APPEND descriptor, so no locking is needed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:3-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:45-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L45))
- Mailbox.create makes the directory mode 0700 and its files mode 0600, because questions about a private codebase live there. It also tightens files left with wider modes by older versions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:110-119`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L110))
- write_decision and set_viewed read, modify and rewrite their JSON map under a threading.Lock, using atomic_write (temp file in the same directory, then os.replace). The temp file is unlinked if anything fails. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:143-149`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L143), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:173-186`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L173))
- The viewer's models.py holds the pydantic contracts for findings.json v1, checks.json v1, walkthrough.json v1 and the mailbox rows. These models are the source of truth that `export-schema` writes into the committed JSON Schema files. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:1-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L1))
- Viewer models.finding_id is a stable id: the first 10 hex characters of sha1 over file, line_start and claim. findings_digest is a sha256 of the whole dumped file, identifying one version of its content. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:281-289`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L281))
- netinfo.py picks the server's bind address. It binds 0.0.0.0 inside a container and 127.0.0.1 otherwise, unless REVIEW_VIEWER_HOST overrides it. A container is detected from MULTIPLAI_CONTAINER=1/0 first and /.dockerenv second, never from uname. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py:18-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py#L18))
- display_urls lists URLs to try in order: REVIEW_VIEWER_URL_HOST first, then (in a container) an OrbStack `<hostname>.orb.local` URL and the first address from `hostname -I`, otherwise http://127.0.0.1. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py:55-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py#L55))
- budget.py wraps multiplai_core's SpendLedger with a default ceiling of $50 per target. Each target's ledger lives in a ContextVar, so concurrent batch targets each record against their own ledger. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py:5-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py#L5), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py:28-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py#L28))
- checks.py builds the checks record from tool-call names and inputs only, never their results, so file contents cannot leak into it. `seen` rates how an agent came to a cited line: read, searched, diff, prompt, fetched or not-seen. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py:3-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py:120-148`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py#L120))
- marked_citation attaches two marks to a citation: `gate` (web for a URL, otherwise pass/fail from citation_gate) and `seen`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py:151-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py#L151))
- config.load_config layers settings: defaults, then multiplai.conf keys (review_finder_model, review_verifier_model, review_effort, review_explain), then `<out>/review.yaml`. A broken multiplai.conf only logs a warning and falls back to defaults. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:117-127`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L117))
- The default finder dimensions are diff-bugs, callers, history, conventions and tests. Trim and extra are added separately. Default concurrency is 4 and max_turns is 60. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:49-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L49))
- gates.py holds checks written in plain code that never call a model. citation_gate re-reads the quote at head with git (or, for a dependency citation, at the dependency's pinned commit). finding_gate requires a known severity, a changed file (unless the dimension is `pre-existing`), at least one citation, a first citation that is neither web nor dependency, and every repo citation reproducing. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:1-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:88-97`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L88))
- verdict_gate lets a `confirmed` verdict through only if at least one of its non-web citations reproduces. On failure the action is `downgrade`, and the caller lowers the verdict to unverifiable. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:110-130`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L110))
- need_gate keeps the shell commands suggested in needs readable and safe to copy. A command must be one line under 300 characters, contain none of `; & | > < ` $(`, and use a read-only verb of a known CLI (gh, gcloud, kubectl, git, curl without write flags, SQL without writes, …). gated_need blanks a failing command and keeps the need. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:135-145`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L135), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:266-274`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L266))
- reason_kind maps a raw gate reason to a fixed rule name. Raw reasons can quote citations, so only the rule name reaches activity.jsonl and checks.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:22-36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L22))
- ledger.py appends a `started` line and a `finished` line to `<data_dir>/reviews/ledger.jsonl` for every run. The file is only ever opened for appending. A failed write is logged and never raised. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:8-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L8), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:73-85`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L73))
- ledger.interrupted returns True when the last started/finished line for this target_dir and head_sha is `started`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:112-119`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L112))
- The pipeline's models.py parses agent output leniently (extra keys are ignored) but enforces two rules at parse time: a finding needs at least one citation, and Finding.id is always recomputed from (file, line_start, claim), overwriting whatever the model sent. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:52-56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L52), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:99-102`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L99))
- ReviewState is the checkpoint. It records the last completed stage, findings, verdicts, rejected/merged findings, per-finder results, stored merge/repeats/assess answers (so a resume asks nothing twice), needs, budget, timings and the checks record. STAGES fixes the run order. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:29-31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L29), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:406-408`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L406))
- VerifierAnswer requires `impact` and `topic` unless the status is refuted, so a missing one fails parsing and triggers the re-ask. The plain Verdict blanks unknown topics and impacts to "". ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:233-239`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L233))
- orchestrator.run_state saves the state, then writes the ledger `started` line, then runs the stages. In `finally` it writes a `finished` line whose `stopped` is None, budget, verify-incomplete or failed:<Exc>. KeyboardInterrupt, CancelledError and SystemExit write no end line, which is how the ledger marks an interrupted run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:135-139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L135), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:117-118`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L117))
- _run_stages extracts a head snapshot into `<target_dir>/tree`, then runs each stage not yet past (explain only when config.explain is set). It saves review-state.json after every stage. On BudgetExceededError or VerifyIncomplete it closes the timings, saves and re-raises. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:208-225`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L208))
- After the stages, export writes findings.json and checks.json, render writes the markdown, the stage becomes `done`, and the snapshot is deleted. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:254-266`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L254))
- prepare resolves and gates the target, takes the run lock, raises InterruptedRun (exit 4) when the ledger shows an unfinished run whose saved state is on the same head, keeps the previous round, and writes the target files. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:337-352`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L337))
- _choose_folder picks `<out>/<repo>/<object>/`. If that folder belongs to another remote it switches to `<repo>@<owner>/`. If it belongs to another ref of the same remote it appends 6 hex of the ref's sha256. After three tries it raises ReviewError suggesting --out. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:418-433`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L418))
- batch runs reviews concurrently, limited by a semaphore (default 2). A failing target is recorded and printed without stopping the others. Afterwards the severity rollups and runs.jsonl are rewritten. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:657-686`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L657))
- assess_only re-runs the repeats and assess stages on a deep copy of a saved state, compares the labels with viewer/decisions.json, and writes nothing to the review. assess_report renders the comparison as markdown. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:563-577`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L563))
- progress.py subclasses core's ProgressWriter and adds only a STARTED line. The session tails progress.log until it sees DONE or FAILED. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/progress.py:14-17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/progress.py#L14))
- render.py writes review-<slug>.md, summary-<slug>.md and checks-<slug>.md per target, rendered from findings.json v1 dicts. Only confirmed or unverifiable findings without a hidden assess label are listed and counted, grouped into Code/Tests/Docs/Trim sections. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:309-315`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L309), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:40-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L40))
- write_rollups writes `<SEV>-only.md` for each severity across targets. write_runs writes runs.jsonl with one line per run from every findings.json and kept round, deduplicated and sorted by generated_at. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:572-582`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L572), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:590-592`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L590))
- code_link builds a full-sha GitHub blob link for a GitHub remote and a `path:lines` code span otherwise. Dependency paths under .review-deps are never linked. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:107-115`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L107))
- rounds.keep_round copies the previous run into `rounds/<NN>-<sha12>/` before a new run overwrites it. It copies into a `.partial-` name and renames when done. A run that stopped before export is kept without the exported files. A round already kept is not copied again. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:233-248`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L233))
- rounds.load_rounds gathers earlier-round findings, keeping the latest copy of each id. A person's decision is attached only if its timestamp is not after the next round's generated_at. Otherwise the finding falls back to a rejection carried over by a `repeat` assessment. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:277-282`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L277), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:445-451`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L445))
- history_elsewhere finds earlier rounds of the same target written under older directory names: pre-0.46 slugs started with the checkout's directory name. It matches on the slug suffix and the same origin, walking `<out>`, `legacy/` and the old INBOX/reviews. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:340-349`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L340))
- runlock.hold takes an exclusive non-blocking flock on `<out>/<repo>/.locks/<object>.lock` and writes the holder's pid, host and start time into it. A held lock raises Locked. The kernel releases the lock when the process dies. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py:47-48`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py#L47), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py:76-80`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py#L76))
- sdk.agent_call_structured is the pipeline's only way to call a model. Before every call it checks repo trust and the budget. Only allow-listed tools are given (read-only tools, plus web tools for finders and verifiers), and reads are confined to the snapshot through read_roots. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:48-51`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L48), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:125-139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L125))
- If an answer does not parse, sdk makes exactly one re-ask. When text came back, the re-ask is a one-turn, no-tools reformat of that text. When the run failed, the prompt is re-run with the error appended. A second failure raises AgentCallError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:184-200`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L184))
- sdk.recording() uses a ContextVar to collect every attempt's tool calls, turns and cost inside a block, so concurrent stage tasks each fill their own record. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:71-84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L71))
- state.py only names review-state.json and delegates the atomic save and the tolerant load (None when the file is missing or unreadable) to multiplai_core.checkpoint. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py:18-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py#L18))
- timings.py records time intervals under state.timings, keyed by run, stage name or find:<dimension>. A resume appends a new interval, so wall time leaves out the gaps between runs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py:3-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py#L3))
- target.resolve turns exactly one of branch, pr, range or tree into base and head shas, storing any problem in Resolved.problem. target_gate then fails on a problem, a missing sha, an empty diff, or a tree with no files left. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:261-274`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L261), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:339-355`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L339))
- For a tree review, tree_files skips binary files, files over 200,000 characters, lockfiles, files marked linguist-generated or linguist-vendored, and submodules. Each skipped file and its reason go to skipped.txt. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:41-45`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L41), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:483-485`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L483))
- snapshot_head extracts head with `git archive` into a directory for the agents, so they read exactly the bytes the gates check. A `.review-head` marker lets it reuse a snapshot already at that sha. Extraction uses tarfile's `data` filter. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:683-697`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L683))
- import_dir copies a directory that is not under git into `<target_dir>/source/` (skipping node_modules, .venv, build output and .git) and commits it with a fixed author and no user git config. If the contents have not changed, it reuses HEAD. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:489-493`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L489), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:656-660`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L656))
- branch_rules reads GitHub's rules for the base branch through `gh api`. If the call fails it returns None, logs a warning and appends a Need containing the command a person could run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:213-222`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L213))
- brand.load reads a JSON brand file. It rejects any key outside the seven it knows, resolves relative font and logo paths against the brand file's own directory, and requires every colour to be #RRGGBB. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:62-65`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L62), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:67-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L67), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:79-80`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L79))
- brand.ass_colour turns #RRGGBB into the ASS subtitle colour form &HAABBGGRR, which is alpha first and then the channels in reverse order. Brand.caption_font prefers the bold font file over the regular one. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:91-94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L91), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:48-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L48))
- captions.group_words splits the words into runs at every sentence end and at every pause longer than MAX_GAP_S (0.3 s). Each run is then split into lines with max_chars as a hard limit and words_per_line as a soft cap, and short lines are joined to a neighbour. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:54-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L54), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:180-187`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L180))
- Caption timing: a line appears LEAD_S (0.08 s) before its first word and stays up to HOLD_S (0.6 s) after its last. forward_only then makes every event start no earlier than the previous one ends, so captions never overlap. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:33-38`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L33), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:230-239`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L230))
- With highlight on, caption_events emits one ASS event per word: the whole line, with the spoken word in the accent colour. build_ass writes the full .ass text, with a Caption style, a Headline style, and Dialogue lines pinned with \pos. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:207-212`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L207), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:287-290`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L287))
- captions.font_family gets a font's family name from fontTools, then from fc-query, and falls back to the file stem. missing_glyphs returns None when neither checker is available. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:330-344`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L330), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:369-374`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L369))
- composite.render is the whole render. In order it: loads the brand, loads the words if needed, probes the source size, validates the EDL, lints the overlays, writes the subtitles, cuts each segment, renders the title card, builds the crossfade graph, adds overlays, music and sfx, and runs one final ffmpeg composite. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:502-508`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L502), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:516-520`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L516))
- _cut_segments writes one near-lossless intermediate file per segment (seg00.mp4 and so on, CRF 14) with ffmpeg. A segment's audio is replaced with silence when it is muted or its speed is above 4. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:179-180`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L179), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L46))
- Audio dropouts that prep found (audio_gaps.json in the prep cache) are filled with silence in render too. The aresample async filter is added only to segments that overlap a gap. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:168-170`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L168), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:195-196`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L195))
- build_filter_complex joins the clips with concat when the transition length is 0 (a hard cut) and with xfade/acrossfade otherwise. It overlays the logo when one is set. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:284-288`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L284), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:302-305`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L302))
- Music is looped, loudness-normalised to -16 LUFS, faded in and out, and sidechain-compressed against the narration so it ducks under speech. Sound effects are mixed in after the music with amix normalize=0 and an alimiter. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:537`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L537), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:550-554`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L550), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:497-498`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L497))
- _burn_subtitles maps the words to output time, checks that the caption font can draw every character (it raises ValueError if not, and only warns if it cannot check), writes subs.ass into the work directory, and returns a subtitles filter. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:393-402`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L393), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:403-406`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L403))
- In 'keyed' mode an overlay's key colour is removed with colorkey, plus despill for green or blue keys. lint_overlays refuses the EDL before anything renders if any overlay page has a lint error. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:434-438`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L434), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:459-460`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L459))
- The EDL is a tree of dataclasses built by EDL.from_dict. xfade_before gives the crossfade into each segment: an explicit transition's duration if one is declared, 0 when the segment continues straight on from the previous one in the source, and 0.5 s otherwise. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:214-220`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L214), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:268-274`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L268))
- EDL.validate raises ValueError on plan-breaking mistakes, such as a source that points at the 720p proxy or a final segment that is zoomed without hold. It returns warning strings for legal but questionable choices, such as zooms longer than 12 s.
