- A malformed pr: config or a bad pr.template is never refused. It is logged as a warning and replaced by defaults or by discovery, so a typo can quietly use the wrong template. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:62-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L62))
- render_report fills in default values for missing candidate fields by changing the input payload in place, and uses StrictUndefined, so any other missing template variable is an error. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py:48-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py#L48), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py:41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py#L41))
- I found no prompt-injection text aimed at the reader in these files. The instructions in findings.py and review.py are prompt templates the code sends to its own agents, so they are data. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:66-67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L66))
- When a merge agent fails, its group is stored as an empty answer. A resume will not ask again, so that group stays unmerged for good. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:236-238`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L236))
- A trim finding never links to a non-trim finding in merge, even when both cite the same lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:51-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L51))
- merge_set has a side effect: it rewrites state.original_severity for the kept finding, using the highest original severity among the members. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:139-143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L139))
- Diffs over 120,000 characters, descriptions over 12,000 characters and commit lists over 200 entries are cut silently in prompts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L9), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L84))
- The tree prompt changes one schema description with a string `.replace`. If anyone rewords SCHEMA's 'path of the changed file the bug is in', the replace silently stops working. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:282`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L282))
- There is no tree wording for `history`, so calling build with dimension 'history' on a tree target would raise KeyError. The stage simply never runs it there. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:126-127`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L126))
- If a verifier rates a non-trim finding about `tests` as breaks-*, the impact is quietly lowered to correctness-only, with only a line added to state.errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:167-170`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L167))
- Every trim finding is forced to LOW at the end of verify, whatever severity the finder gave it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:222-224`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L222))
- VerifyIncomplete is raised only after every other verifier has finished, so the verdicts that did come back are kept for the resume. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:209-211`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L209))
- render.render adds 'render' to steps_done without checking whether it is already there. explore.finish and group.finish do check before adding theirs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py:114-116`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py#L114), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:226-227`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L226))
- calls.py takes MAX_ATTEMPTS and CONCURRENCY from explore.py, so changing them in explore also changes the group and write steps. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py:29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py#L29))
- plan_text writes the 40-symbol cap as a literal instead of using group.MAX_SYMBOLS, so changing the constant leaves the plan text out of date. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:232-233`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L232))
- The trust gate reads only the environment variable SPEC_EXTRACT_TRUST_REPO, accepting '1', 'true' or 'yes'. Without it every call raises RepoTrustError (exit 4). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:145-146`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L145))
- A reformat that cannot run because of budget, backend or trust becomes CallFailed, not the original error, so the paid attempt still counts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:229-231`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L229))
- The default budget is $80 per run folder, counted across every resume, and spend accounting failures are only logged. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L60), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:138-140`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L138))
- On a filesystem without flock, or when the lock file cannot be opened, the run logs a warning and continues unlocked. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py:69-74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py#L69))
- State and Target use extra='forbid', so a state.json written by a newer version with extra fields is refused instead of loaded. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py:52-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py#L52))
- The --dir import runs `git add -A -f`, so files the copied .gitignore would exclude are committed anyway. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:503-506`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L503))
- A scope that splits into more than DEFAULT_MAX_UNITS = 20 explore units of 6000 lines each is refused. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:43-45`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L43))
- A once-saved scope is never recomputed, so a resumed --pr run keeps its original file list even if the PR has moved on. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:19-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L19))
- pipeline.py imports its stages by adding its own directory to sys.path, and the `make` subcommand only prints the workflow steps without doing anything. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L23), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:475`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L475))
- render refuses with exit 2 when the EDL file is the same path as the sidecar render would write, compared with samefile so case-insensitive disks are handled. The music bed defaults to -18 dB. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:51-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L51), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L33))
- Every script directory with third-party deps must be listed as a workspace member. An unlisted pyproject.toml gets its own stray .venv, and scripts/lint_workspace.py fails CI on it. ([`pyproject.toml:15-19`](../../../pyproject.toml#L15))
- code_facts has its own pyproject.toml but is deliberately not a workspace member. uv rejects a member that is also reached through a path source, so it is pulled in as an editable path dependency. This is an exception to the root rule that every such directory must be a member. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:51-55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L51), [`pyproject.toml:32-46`](../../../pyproject.toml#L32))
- The [sdk] extra on multiplai-core is required, not optional. Nothing injects claude-agent-sdk at runtime, and without the extra AgentSDKClient quietly falls back to the API backend. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml#L3))
- Do not add a separate claude-agent-sdk pin next to multiplai-core[sdk]. In deep-research, `claude-agent-sdk>=0.1.0` undercut core's `>=0.2.116,<0.3` floor and resolved to 0.1.56. That version misparses the CLI's result message and raises `Claude Code returned an error result: success` after a full generation. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:10-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L10))
- Each member repeats the multiplai-core git source locally. An installed plugin is only a copy of its subtree, with no workspace root above it, so without the local source uv would look up multiplai-core on PyPI, where it does not exist. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:40-46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L40))
- video-edit's playwright==1.63.0 is an exact pin. It must match PLAYWRIGHT_VERSION in the multiplai-container Dockerfile, because each Playwright release expects one specific Chromium build. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:21-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L21))
- OpenCV is the headless build on purpose (the container has no display). Do not swap in opencv-python. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:10-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L10))
- multiplai-core tracks the mutable `main` branch, so the committed uv.lock is the only thing that fixes which core version is used. Dependabot bumps it weekly. ([`pyproject.toml:49-53`](../../../pyproject.toml#L49))
- The dev setup is not uniform across members. buildme, review-viewer and research-pipeline define only a `dev` extra with no [dependency-groups], so a bare `uv run ... pytest` will not install pytest there. review-viewer's dev extra also leaves out pytest-asyncio. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml:13-14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml#L13), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:26-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L26))
- plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt looks like a dependency file but declares nothing. Edit scripts/pyproject.toml instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt#L3))
- The viewer's models.py defines `class Assessment(_Strict)` twice: once for the review's judgement of a finding and once for the walkthrough badge. The second definition rebinds the module-level name, so `models.Assessment` refers to the walkthrough one. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L73), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:355`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L355))
- target.snapshot_base has unreachable code after `return dest`: an older single-call `git archive` path left behind. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:753-755`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L753))
- The viewer says it restates the pipeline's base/head rules, but branch resolution differs. The viewer tries the local `refs/heads/<name>` first. The pipeline uses only `origin/<branch>`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:855`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L855), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:289`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L289))
- The two packages cannot import each other, so constants and rules are copied by hand: EMPTY_TREE, REVIEW_FILES, NOT_OBJECT_NAMES, sanitize_slug, finding_id. A change in one package must be mirrored in the other. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:438-441`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L438), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:40-41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L40))
- Every agent call raises RepoTrustError unless REVIEW_TRUST_REPO is 1, true or yes, which `--trust-repo` sets. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:104-118`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L104))
- A running review and a killed one leave the same unclosed `started` ledger line. interrupted() cannot tell them apart, so the run lock must be taken before asking it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:18-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L18))
- If the filesystem does not support flock, or the lock file cannot be opened, the run lock logs a warning and the run continues unlocked. hold() also creates only `.locks/`, not its parents. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py:66-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py#L66))
- config: setting verifier_model also sets merger_model, and the repeats and assess stages run on merger_model. find_with_facts is silently switched off (with a warning) unless explain is on. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:158-159`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L158), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:148-150`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L148))
- Readers skip lines they cannot parse: mailbox silently, ledger.read with a warning. A corrupted row disappears instead of raising. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:67-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L67), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:104-106`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L104))
- In the pipeline, _pr_view calls json.loads on gh's output with no handling, so non-JSON output raises JSONDecodeError rather than TargetError. The viewer's _gh_json does wrap it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:185-187`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L185), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:724-727`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L724))
- The comment on GateResult.action lists only keep, reject and downgrade, but the code also uses `blank` (need_gate) and `exit` (target_gate). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:250`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L250), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:251`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L251))
- Findings labelled repeat, low-value or important are neither listed nor counted in the markdown; they appear only in the appendix. In critical mode, only breaks-users and breaks-business findings are listed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L27))
- gates.py functions must not have names starting with `test`, or pytest will collect them as tests. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L7))
- Any new top-level directory the pipeline writes inside a target folder must be added to rounds.NOT_KEPT. Otherwise keep_round's handling changes and the guarding test fails. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:64-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L64))
- Decisions in viewer/decisions.json are keyed by finding id alone. They apply to an earlier round's copy only if made before the next round was generated. A decision with no readable timestamp always applies. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:24-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L24))
- In a container the viewer binds 0.0.0.0, exposing it on the network. The only protection is the token checked on every /api call. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py:31-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py#L31))
- Mailbox rows larger than 64 KiB raise MailboxError instead of being written. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:29-48`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L29))
- The review-viewer's models forbid unknown keys while the pipeline's models ignore them. A field added to pipeline output must also be added to the v1 contract, or validation of findings.json fails. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:31-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:53-56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L53))
- STATE_FILE = "review-state.json" is defined separately in rounds.py and state.py. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:172`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L172), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py:15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py#L15))
- The brand logo and the EDL logo have different defaults: brand uses top-left at 0.16 of width, EDL uses bottom-right at 0.06. render copies the brand logo in only when the EDL has none, mutates edl.logo, and forces start_at=0.0. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:34-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L34), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:92-93`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L92), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:509-511`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L509))
- Frame 'stack' passes the focus x to both panels (fx, fx). Panel B's horizontal focus is never set on its own, and focus y is ignored for stacks. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:139`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L139))
- When no work_dir is passed, render creates a temp directory with mkdtemp and never deletes it. The intermediates (seg*.mp4, title.mp4, subs.ass) stay on disk. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:513-514`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L513))
- The brand background colour is used for padding only when the EDL names a brand file. Otherwise layouts.DEFAULT_BG is used, and the title card's #0a0a0a is hard-coded regardless of brand. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:517`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L517), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:239`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L239))
- A music 'prompt' never generates music. It prints a notice and quietly falls back to the synthetic 'calm' pink-noise bed. Music fade-in and fade-out are hard-coded to 1 s and 2 s. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:595-600`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L595), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:538-539`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L538))
- Speed above 4x silently mutes a segment, in both composite and timeline. A word, caption or click inside such a segment gets no output time and is dropped. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:18`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L18), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L47))
- The same crossfade arithmetic exists in three places: EDL.total_duration, timeline.place_segments and composite.build_filter_complex. Changing one without the others makes captions drift from the render. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:277-279`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L277), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:7-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L7))
- prep's CACHE_ROOT is computed at import time, so changing WORKSPACE after `stages.prep` is imported has no effect. Cached artifacts (proxy, audio, whisper.json, transcript.json) are reused whenever the file exists; to redo them you delete them by hand. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L22), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:209-211`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L209))
- The SSH bridge falls back to a hard-coded key path /home/agent/.ssh/build_key and the host host.docker.internal. It uses StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new, which trusts the first host key it sees. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:42-45`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:486`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L486))
- scenedetect must be on PATH, which means running through `uv run --project`. Otherwise prep raises RuntimeError. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:259-263`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L259))
- The transcribe-skill source (a) does not exist yet: the transcribe skill has no word-level JSON output. In practice every transcript comes from mlx_whisper. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:21-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L21))
- Frame 'speaker' is effectively unusable today. It needs speaker labels, which come from diarization, and that is 'planned, not built'. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:494-498`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L494))
- captions._charset catches only ImportError around fontTools. A font file that fontTools cannot parse raises instead of falling back to fc-query, unlike font_family, which catches every Exception. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:348-352`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L348), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:337-338`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L337))
- Overlays with no mode default to 'keyed' with a #00FF00 key. Any pure green in the page itself becomes transparent. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:169-171`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L169))
- edl.py and timeline.py, and edl.py and sfx.py, import each other. edl.py breaks the cycle with function-local imports; moving them to module level causes a circular import. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:337`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L337), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:377`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L377))
- The synthesize node never passes thinking= to llm_call. The per-node thinking setting for 'synthesize' (conf or --thinking) is silently ignored there, while verify does pass it. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py:60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py#L60))
- Likely issue, read from the code but not run: _get_output_format fills {base_header} and {yaml_appendix_format} with str.replace but never calls .format(). It is then passed in as a value to REDUCE_PROMPT.format, so {date}, {research_type} and doubled braces such as {{claim would reach the reduce prompt literally. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:337-340`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L337), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/synthesize.py:272`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/synthesize.py#L272))
- In map-reduce synthesis a failed map chunk is only logged and skipped, so the report can quietly omit those findings. Only when every chunk fails does it return a 'synthesis failed' stub. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:97-106`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L97))
- The _append_failed_sources placement depends on the LLM writing the exact '<!-- STRUCTURED DATA' marker that the prompt template asks for. If the marker is missing, the table is appended at the end. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:304-305`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L304), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/synthesize.py:227`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/synthesize.py#L227))
- DEFAULT_MODEL and PARSE_MODEL are read from multiplai.conf once, at import time. conf_* lookups re-read the file on every call, deliberately, with no cache. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:61-62`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L61), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:38-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L38))
- An unrecognised THINKING= value is ignored with one warning per (task, value) pair, not treated as off. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:153-162`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L153))
- Adversarial challenge turns on automatically for the thorough preset unless --no-challenge is given. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:427-432`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L427))
- Gate tokenisation keeps only ASCII [a-z] words of 3 or more characters, with an English stopword list. Non-English or accented queries can tokenise to almost nothing, and an empty question token set counts as 'vacuously related'. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:41-42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L41), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:206-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L206))
- Stage progress is based on the enum's declaration order. Inserting a new Stage in the middle changes what is_complete reports for existing checkpoints. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py:161-163`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py#L161))
- mark_source_extracted keeps only the first 2000 characters of page content, and every call rewrites the whole checkpoint. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py:214-220`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py#L214))
- Usage and concurrency counters in sdk are process-wide globals. Call reset_accumulated_usage and reset_sdk_concurrency_stats at pipeline start, or the numbers carry over between runs in the same process. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py:99-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py#L99), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py:126-131`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py#L126))
- ProgressWriter writes with plain write_text and append, not atomic_write_text. initialize truncates any existing progress file. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/progress.py:32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/progress.py#L32))
- Term **catalog**: JSON file (memory.json, banks.json, and so on) of per-file summary entries that the router reads to decide what context to load. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L42))
- Term **shared bank**: A team-shared repo of memory files that consumers subscribe to. Its entries carry a `bank` field and are keyed `<bank>/<file>`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:4-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L4))
- Term **fragment (catalog.json)**: The catalog a bank commits itself, which consumers adopt verbatim instead of regenerating it with an LLM. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:16-17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L16))
- Term **derived entry**: A deterministic, model-free entry built for a bank file with no fragment entry, marked `derived: True`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:106`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L106))
- Term **collision**: The same fact appearing in more than one bank (or in a bank and the personal catalog), reported in bank-collisions.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:296-297`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L296))
- Term **section_anchors / gloss**: Per-H2-section entries with a one-line gloss, so the router can load a single section instead of the whole file. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:82-85`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L82))
- Term **hand-authored / preserved fields**: Catalog fields curated by humans (sections, bundle, co_retrieve_for, intent_domains, anti_domains) that must survive regeneration. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:106-108`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L106))
- Term **intent_domains / anti_domains**: Short phrases describing the tasks a file is (or is not) relevant to, used for routing. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:223-224`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L223))
- Term **bundle / co_retrieve_for**: Hand-written routing hints that group files or pull related files in together, consumed by lib/routing_logic.py. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:217-219`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L217))
- Term **now file**: The per-project status summary at now_dir/{project}.md, made from recent diary sessions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:268`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L268))
- Term **diary**: Per-day markdown files (YYYY-MM-DD.md) holding one `## Session:` block per Claude session. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:102-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L102))
- Term **.warmup marker**: A directory under scripts/ holding `pid` and `status` files. It records an in-flight or failed environment build and guards against starting two builds at once. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L83))
- Term **stub model client**: A fake client with is_stub=True that returns '{}'. Used for dry runs and when no real client is available; it blocks catalog persistence. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:182-192`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L182))
- Term **qmd**: External chunk-level search index used to retrieve the resources corpus at prompt time, replacing a resources catalog. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:40-41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L40))
- Term **extractive summary**: Fallback now-file summary made of truncated diary snippets, used when the LLM is unavailable. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:150-151`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L150))
- Term **front**: An agent with a claim on your attention right now: one in the Needs you, Working or Parked group, but not Idle. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1155`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1155))
- Term **disposition**: How a session was left (active, parked or done), set by the extraction pass. It is a separate axis from liveness status. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:65-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L65))
- Term **roster**: live_containers.json, written by the host-side kit launcher from docker ps; it lists which containers actually exist. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:199`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L199))
- Term **dream**: The background job that consolidates learnings files into memory-update proposals. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:12-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L12))
- Term **floor**: The final deterministic veto on a memory write. It can refuse a write but never approve one. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L1))
- Term **bank**: A named memory store. 'personal' is local; any other bank is shared with other people over git, and local writes to it are refused. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:51-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L51))
- Term **provenance / kind**: The two learning-taxonomy axes: where a piece of knowledge came from (sets confidence) and what sort of thing it is (sets blast radius). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L1))
- Term **section anchor / section ref**: A pick written as 'file.md#Section' that loads a single H2 section instead of the whole file. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py#L3))
- Term **preamble**: The text before a memory file's first H2 (date stamp, purpose, routing hints). Section picks drop it, so the caller prepends it. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py:115-116`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/section_loader.py#L115))
- Term **umbrella_roots**: Configured parent directories whose sessions resolve to the 'workspace' project instead of a specific project. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L14))
- Term **.adopt marker**: A file the hub writes next to a registry entry. This module only removes it when it is orphaned. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:18-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L18))
- Term **Routing Warnings**: The section the validation gate appends to a dream proposal for human review. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:702`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L702))
- Term **DolceEngine**: Italian-looking proper noun appearing only as an example directory path in a fleet.py comment; not a concept the code uses. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:994`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L994))
- Term **mechanical call site**: A model call that parses, classifies or summarises input the model can see in full, so it gains little from extended thinking. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L3))
- Term **self-report (estimator A)**: The session's own end-of-session extraction pass naming the memory sections it relied on, with evidence. Cheap but biased upward. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:8-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L8))
- Term **offline judge (estimator B)**: A sampled, cheap-tier model call from memory_maintainer that compares what was injected against a distilled transcript. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:12-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L12))
- Term **legacy_judge**: Judge verdicts with no instrument stamp, written before the stamp existed. They are kept separate from, and never comparable to, the judge column. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:545-546`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L545))
- Term **judge_instrument**: The stamp on a session record saying what produced its judge verdicts: plugin version, model, and whether thinking was on. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L141))
- Term **co_picks**: How many sections of the same file were picked in one turn. That file's bytes were split evenly between them. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:66-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L66))
- Term **totals record**: The output of compaction: per-section running counters that replace session records older than 90 days. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:51-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L51))
- Term **contract C4**: The rule that a missing judge verdict means 'not judged' and must never be counted as 'unused'. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:47-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L47))
- Term **dream / dream proposal**: A run that processes accumulated learnings into proposals, stored as dreams/processed-learnings-*.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L25))
- Term **INBOX**: A top-level workspace folder where notes, screenshots and links land before they are swept. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:93`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L93))
- Term **slug**: A GitHub repo identifier in owner/name form, parsed from the origin remote URL. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L55))
- Term **no_access**: Repos the gh token cannot see. This is a standing configuration fact, not a failure to retry. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L94))
- Term **stack**: A chain of open PRs where each one is based on the previous one's head branch, so they must merge in order. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:295-296`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L295))
- Term **roster / short**: daemon/roster.json lists the live background workers; `short` is a job's short id, which is also its directory name under jobs/. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:86-87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L86))
- Term **DolceBot / DolceEngine**: Names of workspace projects (Italian 'dolce' = sweet). They are cited as the example of nested repos under PROJECTS/. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:74-75`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L74))
- Term **unit / oversize**: A batch of spans given to one explore agent, limited by line count. Oversize marks a single span that is over the limit on its own. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py:106-110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py#L106))
- Term **side (base/head)**: Which commit a citation's lines belong to: head is after the change (or the only commit), base is before it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py:35-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/models.py#L35))
- Term **memory bank**: A directory of memory files. 'personal' is the user's own bank; shared banks are git repos written by other people and synced in. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:21-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L21))
- Term **no-duplication**: The rule that each fact or topic is owned by exactly one bank. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:150`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L150))
- Term **adopt**: Replacing your personal copy of a file with the bank's copy (`/memory-bank adopt`). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:222`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L222))
- Term **intent_domains**: Routing phrases in a catalog entry that the router matches prompts against. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:19-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L19))
- Term **MiMo**: The spec ('MiMo Code') for the 11-section structured checkpoint format. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:179-180`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L179))
- Term **band / handoff / refresh / stale**: Checkpoint terms. A band is a token threshold (100K, 200K). Handoff is the point (200K) where the user is advised to /clear. Refresh and stale are the token-growth and age re-checkpoint triggers. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:75-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L75))
- Term **pending marker**: JSON file under checkpoints/pending/ that points the next SessionStart in the same project and host at the checkpoint to rebuild from. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L41))
- Term **sidechain**: Subagent traffic in a transcript (isSidechain). Its usage figures describe a different context window. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:352-353`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L352))
- Term **DolceBot**: Name of a user project used in examples and incident notes (e.g. the `dolcebot-team` bank). 'Dolce' is Italian for 'sweet'. It is a proper name, not a code concept. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L14))
- Term **Mem0 pattern**: Superseding a contradicted memory line in place, triggered by a conflict, instead of appending a new one. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/conflict_edits.py#L2))
- Term **dream / proposal**: The memory consolidation pass that drafts a proposal of `## Updates for` items. dream_triage decides which of those items are applied automatically. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:800`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L800))
- Term **judge / rubric / floor**: The three triage layers: a model that may only lower an item's outcome, a code table over provenance and kind, and code write-safety checks that may only refuse. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:47-48`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L47))
- Term **receipt**: Markdown audit file listing everything applied without human review, with git revert instructions. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:786`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L786))
- Term **drain**: A process that dequeues marker files and launches the slow child scripts, either in the container at session start or on the host after the container exits. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L9))
- Term **span**: In costing, the Skill, Agent or Workflow context a cost record is attributed to. A span opens at the invoking tool_use or command and closes when the user next speaks. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:25-26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L25))
- Term **change**: One unit of work buildme builds. It lives in specs/changes/<name>/ and holds proposal, design, tasks and similar documents. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L9))
- Term **registry**: specs/registry/: the merged, current requirements, one file per capability. A change's delta requirements are folded into it on archive. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L17))
- Term **delta requirements (ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED)**: A change's requirements file lists additions, modifications and removals against the registry, rather than restating the full spec. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:504`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L504))
- Term **unknowns / explainer gate (B1)**: unknowns.md documents each dependency that is new to the project: its contract, edge cases and falsifiable assumptions. It must be written before the build depends on that dependency. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:107-111`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L107))
- Term **block**: One section of tasks.md (`## N. Name`). The TDD engine builds it as a unit, through test writer, implementer and refactor. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:221-226`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L221))
- Term **tier (advanced / standard)**: A label derived from the running model. It switches task granularity (blocks vs checkboxes), agent scope and implementer prompt style. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1830-1833`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1830))
- Term **stage (spec / agent / review)**: The three groups of model-calling steps. A setting under models: or efforts: for a stage applies to every step in that stage. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:436-437`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L436))
- Term **Pi model**: A non-Claude model given as `pi:<model>@<provider>`, which buildme runs on its Pi backend. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:297-299`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L297))
- Term **adjudication**: The orchestrator's model judges reviewer findings before any is applied. If it is turned off, findings are dropped, not auto-applied. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:928-932`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L928))
- Term **RED gate**: A TDD check that freshly written tests fail for a behavioural reason before any implementation exists. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:262`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L262))
- Term **slot (STATUS:, SURPRISES:, SPEC_IMPACT:, SEARCHED:, DOCS_IMPACT:)**: A required labelled line that agents end their reports with. Gates parse these slots with regexes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:565`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L565))
- Term **TEST CHANGE REQUIRED:**: The marker an implementer must write to declare a legitimate edit to a test file after RED. With the marker, the integrity gate passes but flags the change for review. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:560`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L560))
- Term **bookkeeping files**: buildme's own state files (.build-state.json, .board.json, build-progress.md, pr-sections/ and similar). They are never committed and never deleted by a discard. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:642-650`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L642))
- Term **WIP ref**: A ref under refs/buildme/wip/ that keeps a failed step's work reachable after discard_to moves the branch back. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:721-723`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L721))
- Term **owner seat**: The role that owns a board column (product, eng, author, reviewer, ...). It names an agent seat in a future 'dark factory', not a person. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:73-74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L73))
- Term **explore unit / slice**: A chunk of up to 6000 lines of files that one code_facts explore agent explains. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:49-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L49))
- Term **change dir**: specs/changes/<name>/, the folder that holds one change's spec artifacts, inputs and checkpoint. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L3))
- Term **wrapper**: The SKILL.md skill that runs the pipeline. It reads the stdout markers and handles the interactive steps. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L8))
- Term **WAITING_EXIT**: Exit code 3: the build is waiting on a human or the wrapper (interview replies, research, split acceptance). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:38`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L38))
- Term **untrusted-content fence**: A wrapper around external text (tickets, replies, research) that marks it as data, not instructions, for the LLM. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:20-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L20))
- Term **mode (scratch / brief / only)**: The build mode. scratch starts from an idea, brief loads docs first, and only builds from existing specs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:3-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L3))
- Term **checkpoint / .build-state.json**: The serialized BuildState, rewritten after every transition. It is the only crash-recovery record. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:286-289`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L286))
- Term **epic / EPIC_SPLIT**: A phase that decides whether the input is one change or several child changes. Several stops the build with one plan per child. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:24-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L24))
- Term **board / BoardColumn**: The 11-column kanban ('dark-factory board') that the build's card moves across. It is written to .board.json and to BOARD: stdout lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:83-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L83))
- Term **respec**: A phase that proposes, but never applies, a spec delta built from the implementation notes collected during the build. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:60-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L60))
- Term **unknowns / explainers**: unknowns.md: one explainer per dependency that is new to the project, covering its contract, edge cases and assumptions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:484-487`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L484))
- Term **adjudication / finding**: A finding is one discrete claim from a reviewer. The orchestrator accepts or rejects each finding before anything acts on it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:168-174`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L168))
- Term **fix-on-pass**: An extra fix run on a block that already passed review. It is recorded in FixOnPassState so a resume can roll it back. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py:524-525`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/models.py#L524))
- Term **stop record (StepStop)**: HEAD plus a content fingerprint for each dirty path, captured when a step stops, so a resume never discards someone else's work. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:45-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L45))
- Term **spec-extract / registry**: A separate skill that extracts baseline specs from existing code into specs/registry/. It runs inside CODEBASE_ANALYSIS. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:947-948`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L947))
- Term **size gate / split proposal**: A check after plan review that stops oversized plans with split-proposal.md until --accept-size is given. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1173`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1173))
- Term **tier**: The build tier recorded on the state: advanced or standard. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:192`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L192))
- Term **bypassPermissions / --trust-repo**: The agents run with tool calls auto-approved. --trust-repo or BUILDME_TRUST_REPO=1 is the explicit opt-in that allows this. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:86-91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L86))
- Term **RED gate / GREEN evidence**: Proof that the new tests fail for the right reason before implementation (RED), and the passing suite output after it (GREEN). Both are given to the reviewer as claims to check. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:895`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L895))
- Term **TEST CHANGE REQUIRED**: A declaration an implementer writes when it had to change a test. Integrity accepts the change as flagged and passes the claim to the reviewer. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1237-1239`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1237))
- Term **SPEC_IMPACT: contradicts**: An agent's report that the block could only be built against the spec. With respec.halt_on_contradiction set, this stops the build. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1045-1047`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1045))
- Term **fix on pass**: One extra fix of the accepted findings after a block has already passed review. It is kept only if the block still passes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1791-1793`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1791))
- Term **survivors (mutation)**: Mutants that cosmic-ray created on the changed lines and that the tests failed to catch. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2642-2644`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2642))
- Term **epic / child**: An epic is a large change split into child builds (each with its own plan and PR) that a coordinator runs, reviews and merges. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:8-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L8))
- Term **parked**: A child status meaning the coordinator stopped work on it and a person must act. merge_park and repo_park keep it parked. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:50-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L50))
- Term **<W> / <P>**: <W> is a child's worktree root (its WORKTREE: path). <P> is its project directory inside that worktree. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:4-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L4))
- Term **item 40.3 etc.**: Item numbers in plan-buildme-epic-mode-2026-10-12.md that docstrings cite as the specification for each behaviour. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L33))
- Term **marker**: A machine-readable stdout line such as WORKTREE:, PR:, SPEND:<usd>:<tokens>, or SPLIT:REQUIRED:<path> that the coordinator parses from logs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L26))
- Term **review ledger**: The review skill's append-only JSONL file of started/finished review events, which buildme reads for rounds and spend. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L30))
- Term **Trim finding**: The review's advisory suggestion to remove a test or shorten a comment. It is never auto-posted. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:11-12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L11))
- Term **Verdict (code_fix, spec_change, question, not_defect)**: The triage agent's classification of a PR comment, which decides whether the comment leads to a code fix, a spec change, an answer, or a rebuttal. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py:19-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/models.py#L19))
- Term **bookkeeping**: buildme's own state files (.build-state.json, .board.json, build-progress.md), which are excluded from diffs and snapshots. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2491-2493`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2491))
- Term **workspace-relative path**: A path that starts with the repo key and then gives the path inside that repo, e.g. DolceEngine/DolceChannex/urls.py. Agents can open it in the snapshot, and gates split it back into a repo and a path. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L3))
- Term **gate**: A check written in code, never a model, that accepts or rejects agent output, e.g. by confirming a quote exists at the commit. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L1))
- Term **boundary (inbound/outbound)**: What the module exposes and what it touches: places outside it that use it, and the APIs, data stores and config it reaches. The boundary is saved to boundary.json. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py:22-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py#L22))
- Term **seed / hop / scenario**: A scenario is an end-to-end trace that starts at a seed (a route or task entry point). Each step, or hop, is the definition of something the previous hop calls. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py:100-101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py#L100))
- Term **[[cN]] / [[snippet:cN]]**: Citation-id placeholders that write agents use. The renderer replaces them with path:line links or with the cited code. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py:118-120`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/prompts.py#L118))
- Term **SPEND marker**: The last stdout line, SPEND:<usd>:<tokens>, which buildme reads to add a child run's model spend to its own. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:52-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L52))
- Term **SSRF**: Server-side request forgery: tricking the fetcher into requesting internal addresses such as cloud metadata at 169.254.169.254. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py:27-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py#L27))
- Term **defang / untrusted-content fence**: Text written by outsiders is placed inside <untrusted-content> tags, and its fence markers are neutralised so it cannot break out and act as an instruction. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py:68-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/untrusted.py#L68))
- Term **llms.txt**: A vendor's index of documentation pages written in Markdown. The walkthrough only fetches doc pages listed there or found in local docs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L3))
- Term **EMPTY_TREE**: The hash of git's empty tree. Using it as the base makes the review-viewer show every module file as changed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:368-370`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L368))
- Term **Bruno collection**: A folder of API request files for the Bruno HTTP client. The walkthrough maps the API paths the code calls to its requests but never calls the API itself. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py:342`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/render.py#L342))
- Term **specs/registry**: One file per capability describing what the code does today. A change's requirements must sort what they touch under MODIFIED, REMOVED or ADDED against it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:45-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L45))
- Term **vertical slice**: A task block that can be run end to end once it is finished, as opposed to a horizontal layer such as 'schema' or 'API'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:390-392`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L390))
- Term **explainer / unknowns.md (B1 explainer gate)**: A per-dependency research step that writes documented edge cases (the 'Whisper-silence class' of surprises) into unknowns.md. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:316-320`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L316))
- Term **removed-behavior**: A review dimension for an invariant, guard, error path or test that the diff deleted and did not put back. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py:210-211`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/review.py#L210))
- Term **code_fix / spec_change / question / not_defect**: The four verdicts triage can give a PR comment. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py:45`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/prompts/findings.py#L45))
- Term **managed body / written list**: A PR body that starts with <!-- pr:managed v1 -->. Its 'written' marker pair lists every section key the skill has ever written. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py:22-26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py#L22))
- Term **PR_DIAGNOSIS**: A one-line stdout message the pr skill prints to explain a refusal or something worth noting; callers keep only these lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:141-145`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L141))
- Term **DolceEngine / DolceChannex**: Example repo and app names (an Italian-named project with a Channex integration) used in docstrings to illustrate workspace paths. They are not code identifiers. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L27))
- Term **dimension**: One aspect a finder reviews (diff-bugs, callers, history, conventions, tests, trim). Each gets its own agent. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:235`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L235))
- Term **trim finder**: An advisory finder that suggests removing, merging or shortening low-value tests and comments. Its findings are always LOW. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:54-55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L54))
- Term **tree review**: A review of code as it stands (a whole repository or a directory) rather than a diff. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L3))
- Term **pre-existing**: The dimension value for a finding in a file the change did not touch but makes reachable or wrong. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:204`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L204))
- Term **needs**: Things an agent could not check, each with a cause (no-access or unreachable), a read-only command and a `where`. They block the finding until a person supplies them. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:7-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L7))
- Term **Critical paths**: A `## Critical paths` heading in coding-standards.md listing globs. Tests that cover those paths are never suggested for removal. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:32-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L32))
- Term **survivor**: The finding in a duplicate set that is kept, and that the others are merged into. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:127`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L127))
- Term **unit**: A batch of whole files, about 6000 lines, that one explore agent reads. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:42-43`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L42))
- Term **capability**: One area of behaviour that callers rely on, grouping public symbols. Each one gets its own registry spec file. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:168-169`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L168))
- Term **dispatch rule (item 7)**: Ask a key only when it has no good answer and fewer than 2 attempts. Count and save the attempt before each call, and give it back on a budget, backend or trust stop. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L3))
- Term **row R<n> / Decided <n> / item <n>**: References to numbered rows, decisions and items in the spec-extract plan document that the code implements. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py:13-15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py#L13))
- Term **snapshot**: `<run>/snapshot/`: the head commit's blobs written out for the agents to read. The gates read git directly instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:10-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L10))
- Term **EDL**: Edit decision list: the JSON describing segments, music and overlays that video-edit renders. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:492-493`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L492))
- Term **sidecar**: The `<stem>.edl.json` written next to a rendered mp4. It records the EDL so the review page can draw the timeline. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:69-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L69))
- Term **keyed / alpha / panel**: The overlay modes an EDL overlay can use. Keyed means chroma-keyed on a key colour, #00FF00 by default. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:506-508`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L506))
- Term **uv workspace**: A uv feature that resolves several Python projects together into one uv.lock and installs them into one shared environment. ([`pyproject.toml:1`](../../../pyproject.toml#L1))
- Term **PEP 723 inline metadata**: Dependency declarations inside a script's own `# /// script` comment block. The repo used this before the workspace replaced it. ([`pyproject.toml:3-4`](../../../pyproject.toml#L3))
- Term **UserPromptSubmit hooks**: Claude Code hooks that run when the user submits a prompt and inject context, under a time budget. ([`pyproject.toml:7`](../../../pyproject.toml#L7))
- Term **[sdk] extra**: Optional-dependency set of multiplai-core that pulls in claude-agent-sdk at core's own version floor. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L10))
- Term **multiplai-core**: The team's shared core library, installed from the spikelab GitHub repo. It is not on PyPI. It provides run_agent, env loading and the untrusted-content helpers. ([`pyproject.toml:53`](../../../pyproject.toml#L53))
- Term **code facts / citations re-read at that commit**: Claims about code whose line-range citations are checked again against the exact commit; a claim that fails the check is dropped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- Term **citation gates**: Checks in code that reject review findings whose citations do not hold up. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- Term **BDD capability specs**: Behaviour-driven (Given/When/Then style) specifications of what code can do, written in buildme's registry format. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- Term **editable path source**: A uv source that installs a local directory in development mode, so changes to it are picked up without reinstalling. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L46))
- Term **Dependabot**: GitHub's automated dependency-update bot. It reads manifests and lockfiles, not PEP 723 blocks. ([`pyproject.toml:8-9`](../../../pyproject.toml#L8))
- Term **object folder**: The output folder for one reviewed thing under its repo folder: pr-<n>, branch-<ref>, range-<a>..<b>, tree or tree-<path>. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:526-528`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L526))
- Term **round**: One completed (or stopped) run on a target. Earlier rounds are kept under rounds/<NN>-<sha12>/. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L46))
- Term **tree review / EMPTY_TREE**: A review of all files at one commit rather than a change. The base is git's empty-tree sha, so every file reads as added. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:36-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L36))
- Term **finder / dimension**: An agent that looks for defects from one angle (diff-bugs, callers, history, conventions, tests, plus trim and extra).
