([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L49))
- Term **verdict / verifier**: A fresh-context agent's judgement on a finding: confirmed, refuted or unverifiable, with its own citations. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:194-198`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L194))
- Term **need**: Information the review could not get, with an optional read-only command or place where a person could get it. It is never run by the pipeline. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:176-181`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L176))
- Term **trim**: An advisory finder and section that judges tests and comments the change adds. It never blocks a merge. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L37))
- Term **assess label (useful, still-open, low-value, repeat, important)**: How a shown finding relates to earlier rounds or to its own value. It never changes a verdict. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:358`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L358))
- Term **critical mode / impact**: Mode `critical` lists only findings whose verifier-rated impact is breaks-users or breaks-business. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:121-123`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L121))
- Term **seen mark**: How an agent came to a cited line: read, searched, diff, prompt, fetched or not-seen. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py:30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/checks.py#L30))
- Term **ledger**: The append-only ledger.jsonl with started/finished/migrated lines for every run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L34))
- Term **.review-deps**: The snapshot folder holding the source of git dependencies pinned in uv.lock, as <name>@<commit12>. The gates check citations into it against the pinned commit. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:153-155`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L153))
- Term **.partial- prefix**: A hidden name for a round copy still being written. Readers skip it, and it is renamed when complete. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L73))
- Term **0.46 / 0.42 layout**: Older output layouts that are still read: `<out>/<slug>/` (0.46) and a review nested inside a new-layout folder (0.42). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:606-609`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L606))
- Term **mailbox**: Files beside a review that carry questions, decisions and answers between the browser page and the Claude session. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L3))
- Term **walkthrough**: A session-written guided tour of a change: steps with anchors, optional mermaid diagrams, assessments and risk inputs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:384-394`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L384))
- Term **ASS**: Advanced SubStation Alpha, a styled subtitle format that ffmpeg's subtitles filter (libass) burns into the video. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L1))
- Term **xfade / crossfade / hard cut**: How one segment joins the next: an overlapping fade of the transition's length, or no overlap when the duration is 0. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:263-267`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L263))
- Term **proxy**: A 720p re-encode of the source, used only for analysis. Rendering from it is refused because the result would be blurry. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:634`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L634))
- Term **dead span**: A stretch of the recording where nothing happens on screen: black, static, or only low motion such as a cursor blink. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:56-60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L56))
- Term **money shot**: A short zoomed close-up on the important moment of a screen demo. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:324-325`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L324))
- Term **retake**: A line the speaker said again. prep lists likely retakes, and the later take is kept by default. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:557`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L557))
- Term **follow**: A camera move that pushes in on each click target and pans between them, rendered with ffmpeg zoompan. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:50-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L50))
- Term **keyed / colorkey / despill**: An overlay mode that removes the page's solid key-colour background (as with a green screen) so the video shows around the graphic. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:160-162`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L160))
- Term **sidechain / ducking**: Compressing the music against the narration so the music gets quieter while someone speaks. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:541-542`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L541))
- Term **LUFS / loudnorm**: A loudness unit; the music bed is normalised to -16 LUFS before it is turned down. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:104`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L104))
- Term **letterbox**: Bars above and below the picture when a landscape source is padded into a portrait output. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:515`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L515))
- Term **diarization**: Telling speakers apart in audio. It would supply the speaker labels that frame 'speaker' needs. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:497`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L497))
- Term **mlx_whisper / SSH bridge**: Whisper speech-to-text running on Apple Silicon (Metal GPU). From the container it is reached over SSH on the macOS host. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:34-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L34))
- Term **Ciao (Italian)**: Italian for 'hello'. It is the example word in the transcript contract, whose language code "it" means Italian. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L7))
- Term **dell'intelligenza (Italian)**: Italian for 'of the intelligence'. Whisper splits it into tokens ("dell" + "'intelligenza") that the converter joins back into one word. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:95`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L95))
- Term **Parakeet / FluidAudio**: Other speech-recognition and diarization engines expected to arrive through the transcribe skill. This skill never calls them directly. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:16-17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L16))
- Term **defang**: Make external text inert inside a prompt by stripping control and bidi characters and neutralising fence markers, so a page cannot break out of its data fence. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py:68-72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py#L68))
- Term **<untrusted-content> fence**: The prompt markers that wrap fetched page text so the model treats it as data, never as instructions. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py:4-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py#L4))
- Term **REASSESS**: An LLM stage after reading that checks the framing and flags suspect claims, possibly triggering refinement or verification searches. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py:240-241`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py#L240))
- Term **load-bearing / conflation / convenience-bias claims**: Categories of suspect claims from REASSESS: one the conclusion rests on, one that merges distinct things, and one that may be believed because it is convenient. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py:250-252`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py#L250))
- Term **preset (micro/quick/standard/thorough)**: A named depth setting that controls source count, fetch budget, link depth and summary level. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L17))
- Term **summary level (gist/structured/detailed)**: Report length and format: about 300–500, 1,000–2,000, or 2,500–4,000 words. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L16))
- Term **Strategy C**: The default fetch path, where findings are extracted inside the fetch SDK call straight from the page. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py:150-152`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py#L150))
- Term **ReputationTier**: Source credibility tiers, from authoritative down to unreliable. Gates count only authoritative and established sources as credible. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:250`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L250))
- Term **map-reduce synthesis**: Splitting too many findings into chunks, summarising each (map), then merging the summaries into one report (reduce). ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L3))
- Term **HyperFrames**: The external Apache-2.0 project (heygen-com/hyperframes) that the caption grouping and timing rules were taken from. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:18-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L18))

## Dependencies

- pyproject.toml: Virtual uv workspace root that lists every script directory as a member and sources multiplai-core from git branch main, so there is a single uv.lock and a single environment. ([`pyproject.toml:23`](../../../pyproject.toml#L23))
- requirements.txt: Leftover file containing only comments; it says deps moved to scripts/pyproject.toml and that test tools come from CI. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Declares the memory-engine hook and skill dependencies (multiplai-core[sdk], pyyaml). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for the shared code-facts library, which produces citation-checked facts about code. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L2))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for build-pipeline, the deterministic build pipeline behind the buildme skill. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for walkthrough-pipeline, which explains a module using agents and citation-checked claims. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml#L2))
- pyproject.toml: Declares jinja2 for the deepen skill's report renderer. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for pr-pipeline, which opens and edits GitHub PRs that follow the PR template. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L2))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for review-viewer, the local web viewer for review findings; it makes no model calls. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for review-pipeline, the find/verify/merge/render code-review pipeline. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L2))
- pyproject.toml: Declares pyyaml for the skill-creator promotion and scaffolding scripts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for spec-extract-pipeline, which writes BDD capability specs from Python code. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L2))
- pyproject.toml: Declares the video-edit pipeline's CLI tools and libraries: scenedetect, headless OpenCV, Playwright, tqdm, click and multiplai-core. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Declares the Google API client and OAuth dependencies for the gmail skill. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Declares slack_sdk for the slack skill. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml:1`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pyproject.toml: Package manifest for research-pipeline, the deep-research skill, with search and fetch dependencies (Tavily, Exa, trafilatura, httpx). ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4))
- The repo-root pyproject.toml is a virtual uv workspace root named `multiplai-marketplace`. It never builds a distribution (`package = false`), declares no dependencies of its own, and owns the single lockfile and environment that every bundled script uses. ([`pyproject.toml:20-29`](../../../pyproject.toml#L20), [`pyproject.toml:27-29`](../../../pyproject.toml#L27))
- The workspace exists to fix slow hooks. Before it, scripts used PEP 723 inline metadata with `uv run --no-project` and pinned multiplai-core by git tag, so uv contacted GitHub on every run. The UserPromptSubmit hooks took 12-68s and overran their 10s/30s budgets. After consolidation, hook latency fell from 20.8s to 0.05s. ([`pyproject.toml:3-8`](../../../pyproject.toml#L3), [`pyproject.toml:11-13`](../../../pyproject.toml#L11))
- The workspace has 13 members: the multiplai-context scripts, eight multiplai-dev skill script directories (buildme, codebase-walkthrough, deepen, pr, review-viewer, review, skill-creator, spec-extract), video-edit, gmail, slack and deep-research. ([`pyproject.toml:31-46`](../../../pyproject.toml#L31), [`pyproject.toml:33-34`](../../../pyproject.toml#L33))
- multiplai-core is fetched from git at https://github.com/spikelab/multiplai-core, tracking branch `main` with no pin. Dependabot moves uv.lock to the latest core weekly, and CI runs the test suites against it before merging. ([`pyproject.toml:48-53`](../../../pyproject.toml#L48), [`pyproject.toml:49-50`](../../../pyproject.toml#L49))
- All manifests require Python >=3.12. ([`pyproject.toml:24`](../../../pyproject.toml#L24), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml:5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml#L5))
- plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt pins nothing; it holds only comments. Runtime deps now live in scripts/pyproject.toml. anthropic, claude-agent-sdk and pyyaml arrive transitively through multiplai-core. CI supplies pytest per run with `uv run --with pytest`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt:1-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/requirements.txt#L1))
- multiplai-context-scripts (the memory-engine hooks and skill entry points) depends on `multiplai-core[sdk]` and `pyyaml>=6.0`. It was consolidated from 26 PEP 723 blocks on 2026-08-04. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml:1-2`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml:10-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/pyproject.toml#L10))
- code-facts 0.1.0 is the shared library that works out what code does. Agents read line ranges at a commit and return facts, and a fact is kept only if its citations re-read correctly at that commit. It depends on `multiplai-core[sdk]` and `pydantic>=2`, builds the `code_facts` wheel with hatchling, and is shared by codebase-walkthrough, review and buildme. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:6-11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L6), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:29-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L29))
- code-facts does not make model calls itself. Callers pass in a call function built on `multiplai_core.run_agent()`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:7-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L7))
- build-pipeline (the buildme skill) depends on multiplai-core[sdk], pydantic>=2.0, pyyaml>=6.0, python-dotenv>=1.0, ruamel.yaml>=0.19 and code-facts. Its dev extra adds pytest, pytest-asyncio, jsonschema>=4 and pytest-xdist. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:14-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L14), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L31))
- In buildme, ruamel.yaml is used only by `build_pipeline setup` (setup.py), which edits specs/config.yaml while keeping comments and key order. Every other reader uses PyYAML. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:18-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L18))
- A buildme test validates the facts.json written by the analysis phase against review/schema/facts.v1.schema.json, which is why jsonschema is in dev. CI runs the suite with `-n auto` (pytest-xdist). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:28-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L28))
- walkthrough-pipeline finds a module's callers, callees, data and config, explains them with agents, and keeps only claims whose citations re-read at the commit. It depends on multiplai-core[sdk], pydantic>=2, pyyaml>=6, python-dotenv>=1, httpx>=0.27 and code-facts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml:9-14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml#L9))
- deepen-scripts (the deepen skill's report renderer) depends only on jinja2>=3.0 and is not a buildable package. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml:1-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- pr-pipeline opens and edits GitHub PRs whose body follows the repo's PR template, with the template's structure checked in code. It makes one model call through run_agent. python-dotenv is there because multiplai_core.env.load_env reads the model call's credentials from .env. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:12-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L12))
- review-viewer is a local web viewer for code-review findings and makes no model calls. Its server only moves messages between the web page and JSONL files that the live session watches, so it depends on plain multiplai-core (no [sdk]) plus pydantic>=2. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml:7-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml#L7))
- review-pipeline runs the review skill's stages (find, verify, merge, render), with citation gates enforced in code. It depends on multiplai-core[sdk], pydantic>=2, pyyaml>=6, python-dotenv>=1 and code-facts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:9-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L9))
- skill-creator-scripts (promotion and scaffolding scripts) depends only on pyyaml>=6.0. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml:1-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml#L1))
- spec-extract-pipeline writes BDD capability specs in buildme's registry format from the Python code of a commit, a directory or a PR. Its explore and write calls go through run_agent. It depends on multiplai-core[sdk], pydantic>=2 and code-facts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L4), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:9-12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L9))
- video-edit-scripts declares scenedetect>=0.7, opencv-python-headless>=4.9, tqdm>=4.66, click>=8.1, playwright==1.63.0 and multiplai-core. scenedetect is used as a CLI binary that prep.py shells out to, alongside ffmpeg's silencedetect/blackdetect filters. Only stages/cursor.py imports cv2. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:16-25`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L16))
- video-edit renders overlays in headless Chromium driven by Playwright. Its `capture` command prints text from web pages inside an untrusted-content fence, which comes from multiplai_core.untrusted. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L21), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:26-28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L26))
- gmail-scripts talks to Google/Gmail APIs through google-api-python-client>=2.198.0,<3, google-auth-oauthlib>=1.4.0,<2 and google-auth[requests]>=2.56.3,<3, plus multiplai-core. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml:8-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml#L8))
- slack-scripts talks to Slack through slack_sdk>=3.27,<4, plus multiplai-core. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml:6-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml#L6))
- research-pipeline (deep-research) uses web search and fetch services: tavily-python>=0.5, exa-py>=2.18.1, trafilatura>=1.6 and httpx>=0.27. It also depends on multiplai-core[sdk], pydantic>=2.0, pyyaml>=6.0 and python-dotenv>=1.0. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:16-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L16), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L23))
- In research-pipeline, Playwright is an optional `[browser]` extra (playwright>=1.40). It is imported lazily as a fallback for JS-rendered pages and needs `playwright install chromium`. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:27-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L27))
- Four of the manifests show how the code loads configuration: python-dotenv reads credentials from .env (via multiplai_core.env.load_env in pr), and buildme setup edits specs/config.yaml with ruamel.yaml. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:12-13`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:18-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L18))
- generate_catalogs(config, generators=None, force=False, dry_run=False) is async and returns list[GenerationResult]. It raises ValueError for unknown generator names. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:107-112`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L107), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:49-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L49))
- Every generator's run takes keyword-only `force`, `dry_run` and `force_enable` and returns a GenerationResult. The dispatcher always passes force_enable. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:280-282`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L280), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:155-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L155))
- Generators are constructed with keyword-only `config` and `model_client`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:138-140`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L138), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:153`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L153))
- GenerationResult is a dataclass with fields generator, total_sources, skipped, generated, pruned, errors (list[str]) and dry_run. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:66-80`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L66))
- GeneratorBase subclasses must define name, catalog_filename, discover_sources() and build_prompt(). They may override parse_response, merge_entry, hash_source, entry_needs_regeneration, _call_model and preserved_fields. - load_catalog_config() -> CatalogConfig. CatalogConfig.effective_diary_model returns model_diary, or model when model_diary is blank. - banks.py exports BANKS_CATALOG, BanksGenerator, COLLISION_REPORT and derive_entry(bank, path, text). - fleet.collect(data_dir, now=None) -> Fleet reads both stores and joins them; it never raises on bad input. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1082-1083`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1082))
- fleet.write_fleet_view(data_dir, now=None) -> Path renders AGENTS.md and fleet.json and returns the AGENTS.md path. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:1627`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L1627))
- fleet.fleet_json(fleet, now, generated_at=None, existing=None) -> str serializes the Fleet. Pass the previous document as existing so uncollected sections are carried forward. - fleet.roster_dead_sids(data_dir, now=None, grace=None) -> set[str] returns session ids the host roster has observed to be gone; it is fed into session_registry.gc_stale(dead_sids=...). - hook_input.read_hook_input() -> dict ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py:21-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py#L21))
- job_effort.effort_kwargs(name) -> dict[str, str] is splatted into model calls; job_effort(name) returns the validated effort string or None. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py:54-55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py#L54))

- memory_write_floor.floor_check(item) -> Optional[str] reads target, change and text from any object; path_refusal(memory_dir, filename) checks the resolved path on disk. - project_identity.resolve_project(cwd, config=None) -> Optional[str]. Pass config to avoid re-reading project-map.yaml on every call. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py:108`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/project_identity.py#L108))

- section_loader exposes h2_names(text), preamble(text), parse_section_ref(name), extract_section(text, section_name) and load_picked_content(name, file_text). - taxonomy exposes pair(record), parse_pair(text), format_pair(provenance, kind), normalize_legacy(record), weakest_provenance(values) and broadest_kind(values). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:254-256`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L254))
- thinking_kwargs(option_name, *, target=QUERY, default=False) -> dict is the intended call-site helper. Use it as `client.query(..., **thinking_kwargs(OPT))`. - parse_ts(raw) -> datetime | None is the shared timestamp parser. fleet_sources.common re-exports it. - collect_backlog(data_dir, learnings_dir=None, dreams_dir=None, inbox_dir=None) -> Backlog. Directories not passed are derived from data_dir. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:76-81`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L76))
- run(args, *, cwd=None, timeout=DEFAULT_TIMEOUT) -> Ran, cache_read(data_dir, key, ttl=300) and cache_write(data_dir, key, value) are the shared collector helpers. - Each collector returns plain records: find_repos/collect_repos(workspace, max_depth=3) return RepoState objects, collect_jobs(cfg=None, now=None, stale_after_hours=6) returns BackgroundJob objects, and collect_prs(slugs) returns a PRScan. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:227`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L227), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:96-100`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L96), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:214`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L214))

([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:31-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L31))

- find_collisions(entries, *, texts=None, min_shared_domains=MIN_SHARED_DOMAINS) -> list[Collision]; Collision is a frozen dataclass with kind, refs, banks and detail fields. - GitResult(ok, output, detail) is truthy when ok. The functions run_git(args, *, cwd, timeout), pull_ff_only(path, *, timeout), stage_commit(path, *, pathspec, message), push_branch(path, branch, *, remote='origin') and open_pull_request(path, *, title, body, base, head) all return GitResult. - Checkpoint config and triggers: load_config() -> CheckpointConfig; read_context_tokens(transcript_path, after_ts=None) -> int; checkpoint_trigger(tokens, state, cfg) and staleness_trigger(data_dir, session_id, state, cfg) each return a reason string or None. - Handoff and rebuild API: write_pending_marker(data_dir, cwd, session_id, tokens, *, hostname=None) -> Path; consume_pending_marker(data_dir, cwd, new_session_id, cfg, *, allow_same_session=False) -> dict | None; build_rebuild_context(checkpoint_text, tokens) -> str. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:821-828`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L821), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:856-863`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L856), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1359`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1359))
- Writer and GC API: spawn_writer(payload) -> bool; claim_writer/release_writer/writer_inflight(data_dir, session_id); retire_checkpoint(data_dir, session_id, pending_owners=None) -> (bool, str); sweep_checkpoints(data_dir, cfg) -> (expired, retired). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:623`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L623), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1015-1019`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1015), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1135`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1135))
- Checkpoint queue API: queue_pending_checkpoint(data_dir, payload) -> Path | None; process_pending_checkpoints(data_dir, writer_script, *, wait=False) -> DrainResult; pending_checkpoint_count(data_dir) -> int. - Conflict API: conflict_section_for(learnings_text, memory_contents, today=None) -> str is the convenience entry point. - Costing API: run_collect(config_dir, state_path, *, dry_run=False, refresh_pricing=True) -> dict of stats; collect_file(path, *, project, known_ids, file_state=None, session=None, sidechain=False, base_span=None) -> (records, new_file_state); run_backfill_branches(config_dir) -> dict. - Drain API: claim_pending_markers(pending_dir, processing_dir, failed_dir=None) is a generator of (path, payload); process_deferred_extractions(data_dir, extract_script, *, wait=False) -> DrainResult(launched, failed); pending_count and processing_count(data_dir) -> int. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:152-154`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L152), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:334-336`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L334), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:63-73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L63))
- `column_for(phase, block_status=None) -> BoardColumn` is a pure lookup. `record(config, state, phase=None, *, block_status, column, note, progress)` returns the new column on a move and None when the card did not move. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:297`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L297), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:320-333`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L320))
- `BudgetExceededError(message, *, diagnosis='')` carries a `.diagnosis` text that the orchestrator shows to the user. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:53-62`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L53))
- `normalize_change_name(name)` returns a kebab-case slug and raises ValueError when a non-empty name normalizes to nothing. board.py, git_ops.py and BuildConfig.change_dir all use it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:37-55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L37))
- `ChangeManager(specs_dir)` provides init_specs, create_change(name), artifact_status, ready_artifacts, change_status, artifact_context, list_changes and archive_change(change_dir, merge_specs=True). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:313-317`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L313), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py:446`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/change_manager.py#L446))
- `analyse(project_dir, *, call, max_units, name, change_dir=None)` is async and returns an Analysis, or None when the project has no git commit. `render_markdown`, `write_facts_file` and `block_facts_section` consume its output. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:387-388`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L387), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:551`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L551))
- `BuildConfig.from_cli_args(args)` is the main constructor. `model_for(step)` and `effort_for(step)` raise KeyError for a step that is not in STEP_STAGES. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1087-1088`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1087), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1012-1015`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1012))
- `BuildConfig.rebind_project_dir(new_project_dir)` points the config at the worktree. change_dir, state_file_path() and progress_file_path() are derived from project_dir and specs_dir, so they follow automatically. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1362-1372`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1362))
- Gate functions take plain values and return a GateResult, for example `integration_gate(test_command, project_dir, *, parallel='off')` and `unchanged_tests_gate(before, after, implementer_report='')`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:212`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L212), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:970-974`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L970))
- The report-slot parsers are parse_agent_status, parse_implementation_note, parse_searched, parse_docs_impact and parse_test_change_claims. In each one the last occurrence of a slot wins. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:321-329`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L321))
- `GitResult(argv, returncode, stdout, stderr)` is frozen and has `.ok` and `.command`. `GitLifecycleError` is the RuntimeError raised when the pipeline refuses to start. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:60-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L60))
- `commit_stage(config, message, paths)` commits only when config.pipeline_branch is set and always adds BOOKKEEPING_EXCLUDES. `commit_tree(repo, message, label)` commits everything under the repo except bookkeeping. Both return a SHA or None. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:656-671`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L656), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:694`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L694))
- `preflight(repo_path, *, worktree_requested)` returns the repo root. It raises GitLifecycleError when the path is not a repo, has no commits (if a worktree is requested), or has a dirty tracked tree. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:468-471`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L468))
- run_orchestrator(config: BuildConfig, args) -> int is the main pipeline entry point. Exit codes: 0 success, 1 failure, 2 bad input file, 3 waiting or refused published re-run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:32-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L32))
- run_interview_phase(config, state, args, state_path, progress) -> int | None and run_research_phase(...) -> int | None return an exit code to stop with, or None to continue. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:376-377`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L376), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:551`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L551))
- check_input_files(args, state, change_dirs) -> str | None returns an error message or None, and clears flags whose files have already been copied. resolve_input_paths(args) mutates args in place. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:100`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L100), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L77))
- llm_call(prompt, *, model, effort, max_turns=1, system_prompt, allowed_tools, call_timeout=600, budget_label) -> str raises LLMCallError or LLMCallTimeoutError on failure. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:133-143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L133))
- agent_call(prompt, *, allowed_tools, model, effort, max_turns=50, cwd, call_timeout=1800, budget_label) -> AgentResult never raises on an agent failure. It returns AgentResult(success=False, timed_out=...) carrying any partial output. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:198-208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L198), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:251-256`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L251))
- llm_call_structured(prompt, schema, ...) -> T and agent_call_structured(prompt, schema, *, allowed_tools, ...) -> T return a validated pydantic model or raise LLMCallError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:274-284`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L274), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:309-321`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L309))
- BuildState methods: checkpoint(path=None, *, keep_budget=False), load(path) (classmethod), advance_to(phase, path), fail(project_dir, path), complete(path) -> Path, is_phase_complete(phase) -> bool, plus TDD helpers advance_block, mark_block_status and all_blocks_done. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:279`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L279), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:335-339`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L335))
- stop_record.record(project_dir, ignore=None) -> StepStop | None and others_work(project_dir, stop, *, ignore, head, origin_ref) -> str | None. Neither ever raises. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:55`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L55), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:79-82`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L79))
- run_spec_generator(config, args=None) -> int and run_design_audit_stage(change_dir, config, state, state_path) -> list[dict]. The latter is shared by the spec generator and the orchestrator's DESIGN_AUDIT phase. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:1071-1076`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L1071))
- PRE_PUBLISH_CONTRIBUTORS is an extension hook. Each callable (config, state, docs_dir) -> Path | None adds one section file to the PR body. A contributor that raises is logged and skipped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1512-1515`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1512))
- ProgressWriter(path) exposes initialize, log_phase, log_block, log_agent, log_review, log_evidence, log_diagnosis, log_spec_impact and log_board. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py:40-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/progress.py#L40))
- detect_change_type(change_dir) -> str and generate_rubric(change_dir, config) -> str (async). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L36), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L77))
- run_tdd_engine(config, args, *, standalone=True) -> int is the engine's entry point. standalone=False means the caller (the orchestrator or a findings round) owns the checkpoint and phase advancement. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2064`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2064))
- parse_blocks(tasks_path) -> list[BlockInfo] and assemble_context(block, config, role, blocks=None) -> str build the block list and the context bundle given to each role's agent prompt. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:445`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L445), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:531-536`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L531))
- run_test_quality_check(test_files_content, contracts, config) -> GateResult is the static weak-test scan. capture_build_diff(config, state) -> str returns the whole-build diff, capped, and '' on git failure. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:643`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L643), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:2249`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L2249))
- _TEST_PATH_RE in tdd_engine is imported by findings/decisions.py, so it works as a shared, language-agnostic test-path regex even though its name is private. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:71-74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L71), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:152-153`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L152))
- merge.plan(w, first, second) -> Plan | None and merge.not_resolutions(w, first, second, result) -> list[str] | None are the coordinator's read-only planning and verification calls. Plan is a NamedTuple of (files, refused). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:166`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L166), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L208))
- merge.run_merge_agent(config, w, files, branch_name, default) -> AgentResult makes the single `merge` agent call, with the tools Read/Edit/Bash and max 40 turns, in <W>. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:789`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L789), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:31-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L31))
- merge.ready(w, reviewed, origin_ref, default) -> str | None returns a reason why <W> may not be merged into, or None when it may. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:625`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L625))
- records.start(epic_dir, kind, child, argv, *, head_sha=None, pr_round=None) -> Attempt and records.finish(attempt, exit_code, *, interrupted, extra=None) -> dict bracket each process the coordinator runs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:85-86`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L85), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L143))
- review.decide(found, pr_round, cap, worth) -> (action, reason) and review.review_argv(worktree, number) -> argv are the coordinator's review entry points. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:195`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L195), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L59))
- store.epic_dir_for(repo, change_name) -> Path, load_epic(epic_dir) -> Epic (raises EpicFileError) and save_epic(epic_dir, epic) are the epic persistence API. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:127`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L127), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:173-186`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L173))
- decisions.write_decisions(review_dir, severities=None, code_only=False) -> Path raises FileNotFoundError or ValueError, and writes nothing in those cases. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py:87-94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/decisions.py#L87))
- fetch.fetch_comments(repo, pr, since, *, cwd=None) -> list[PRComment] and fetch.prepare(comments, settings, cache, *, repo, cwd=None) set `allowed`/`injection` on each comment in place. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:265-266`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L265), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:322-328`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L322))
- loop.run_findings(run: FindingsRun) -> int is the findings command's loop. FindingsRun carries the config, PR, repo, branch, change_dir, flags, and the Seams that tests inject. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:628`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L628), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:103-113`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L103))
- triage.triage_round(config, change_dir, round_n, comments, *, done, on_triaged, call) -> list[TriageRecord] and write_findings(change_dir, round_n, records, spec_changes) -> Path. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:92-97`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L92), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:173-177`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L173))
- generate_artifact(artifact_id, context, config, *, interview_summary, research, codebase_analysis, reference_docs, audit_findings) -> str is async and returns the artifact's markdown. context comes from change_manager.artifact_context(). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:86-96`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L86))
- run_design_audit(change_dir, config) and run_tasks_audit(change_dir, config) are async and return list[dict] of findings with category, severity, description and suggestion. An empty list means clean, or an answer that could not be parsed. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:341`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L341), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:387`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L387))
- run_codebase_analysis(project_dir, config, *, call=None) -> str. Tests pass `call` to replace the model call. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:451`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L451))
- run_explainer(dep, config, usage_context='') -> str, where dep is a dependencies.NewDependency. It returns one markdown section, and the caller joins the sections together. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py:315-321`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/llm_steps/spec_steps.py#L315))
- The walkthrough budget API is start(max_usd, state), get_budget(), record(usage, *, label), check(*, stage) and spend_marker(). record never raises, and only check can stop a run. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:45-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L45), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py:68-69`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/budget.py#L68))
- load_config(run_dir, *, max_cost_usd) -> WalkConfig. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py:42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/config.py#L42))
- fetch_url(url, client, *, allowed_host=None, request_timeout=15.0, max_retries=2) -> Fetched. Fetched.ok is true for a 2xx status with no error. client() returns an httpx.AsyncClient with the pipeline's User-Agent. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:69-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L69))
- citation_gate(repos, citation) -> GateResult, trace_gate(repos, seed, hops, places) -> (good_hops, reason), and write_gate(text, *, citations, corpus, labels) -> list of failure reasons. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L57), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:265`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L265))
- orchestrator.prepare(opts, runs_dir) -> (WalkState, run_dir). run_state(state, run_dir, config, *, session_id) and resume(run_dir, config, *, session_id, docs) are async and return the list of output Paths. Bad input raises WalkError. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:91-92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L91), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:286-287`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L286))
- agent_call_structured(prompt, schema, *, allowed_tools, model, effort, max_turns=60, cwd, budget_label, call_timeout=1200.0) -> an instance of schema. It raises AgentCallError after the retry fails, and RepoTrustError when the repo is not trusted. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py:106-117`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py#L106))
- symbols.collect(target, snapshot_root) -> (entries, warnings). setting_definitions and outbound_api_paths add settings anchors and normalised API paths. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py:274-275`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/symbols.py#L274))
- target.build_target(path, run_dir, *, name, depth, key) -> (TargetInfo, RepoInfo). snapshot_repo(repo, dest) -> Path is a no-op when the .walkthrough-head marker already matches the sha. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:254-255`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L254), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:155-157`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L155))
- render.write_outputs(state, out_dir) -> list[Path] writes the Markdown file and, outside agent mode, the JSON file. - pr_pipeline.__main__.main(argv) -> int, and commands.run(args) -> int, which maps GateFailed to 1 and Refused or any other exception to 2. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:403-419`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L403))
- render_body(repo, change_dir, *, base, head, ticket_url, section_files, config) -> Rendered, holding the body, template path, sections, assembled parts, sources and diagnoses. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:52-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L52))
- markers.spans(body) -> dict of key to (start, end), raising MarkerError on nested, unpaired or repeated markers. merge(current, owned, template_keys, tails) -> Merge with body, changed, not_restored and not_added. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py:60-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py#L60), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py:124`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/markers.py#L124))
- template.discover(repo, config=None) -> Discovery(path, standard, diagnosis), parse(text) -> list[Section], and load_config(repo) -> PrConfig(template, draft=True). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:114`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L114), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:46-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L46))
- render_report.render(payload, repo=None) -> HTML string. main(argv) takes --in (required), --repo, --out and --open. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py:93-98`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/render_report.py#L93))
- find.build(target, dimension, diff, conventions='', files=None, facts=None) -> str returns the finder prompt. For a tree target, `files` is the group of files this call reviews. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:225-226`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L225))

([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:299-300`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L299))
- The other prompt builders: merge.build(target, findings), repeats.build(target, shown, rejected) and verify.build(target, finding), each returning a str. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py:15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/merge.py#L15), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py:30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/repeats.py#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:82`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L82))
- Shared block helpers: workspace_block(target, *, web=False), deps_block(target), fence(text, source), diff_block(target, diff), description_block(target), settings_block(target). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L29))
- run_merge(state, ctx) -> ReviewState and run_verify(state, ctx) -> ReviewState are the stage entry points. run_verify raises VerifyIncomplete(finding_ids, last_error). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:201`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L201), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:126`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L126))
- merge.linked(a, b), link_groups(findings) and apply_duplicate_sets(state, answers, *, by='') can be reused outside the merge stage. The `by` argument names the calling stage in the merged reason. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:170-171`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L170))
- verify.important_assessments(state) -> dict[str, Assessment] is a pure function of the verdicts, so it is safe to recompute. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:69`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L69))
- calls.dispatch(tasks, schema, call, answers, attempts, ledger, save, *, call_kwargs) takes (key, prompt, label) tuples. It raises the first BackendUnavailable or RepoTrustError, otherwise BudgetStopped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py:66-72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/calls.py#L66))
- explore.run(state, run_dir, *, call, ledger, model) and explore.finish(state, run_dir, now, git_target) -> (answered, units). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:98`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L98), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py:203`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/explore.py#L203))
- group.gate(answer, symbols) -> Grouping is a pure function. group.finish(state, run_dir) returns a Grouping, or None when the run is not grouped. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:213`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L213))
- render.render(state, run_dir, grouping, checked, scope_sections) -> int returns the number of capability files written. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py:101-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/render.py#L101))
- sdk.agent_call_structured(prompt, schema, *, allowed_tools, model=None, effort=None, max_turns=30, cwd=None, budget_label='', call_timeout=1200.0) -> T is the CallFn that code_facts calls. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py:196-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/sdk.py#L196))
- Run-folder API in target.py: resolve(repo, *, tree, path, pr, dir, fetch), run_folder(root, target, lock), snapshot(target, run_dir) and restart(run_dir, now). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:245-246`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L245), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py:364`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/target.py#L364))
- state.load(run_dir) returns the State, None when there is no state.json, and raises Unreadable when the file cannot be read. state.save(state, run_dir) writes it atomically. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py:81-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py#L81))
- scope.compute(target) -> TargetScope reads git only. scope.check(scope, max_units) raises ScopeRefused when no Python file is found or there are too many units. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:208-213`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L208))
- code-facts publishes the importable package `code_facts`. buildme, codebase-walkthrough, review and spec-extract depend on it by name (`code-facts`) through an editable path source. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:29-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L29), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:51`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L51))
- Every LLM call in the pipelines goes through `multiplai_core.run_agent()`. code-facts takes a caller-supplied call function built on it instead of calling the SDK itself. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:7-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L7), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:7-8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L7))
- Each buildable pipeline exposes one wheel package: build_pipeline, walkthrough_pipeline, pr_pipeline, review_viewer, review_pipeline, spec_extract_pipeline and research_pipeline. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml:41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/pyproject.toml#L41), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L35))
- buildme has a `build_pipeline setup` command that edits specs/config.yaml. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml:18-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/pyproject.toml#L18))
- Other code also uses `multiplai_core.env.load_env` (reads .env) and `multiplai_core.untrusted` (the shared untrusted-content fence). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:27-28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L27))
- gitdata.resolve_target(spec, repo, *, base_branch=None, fetch=False, cwd=None) -> Resolved turns a parsed TargetSpec into a Target, plus PrInfo for a PR. It raises TargetError with a message saying what to pass. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:899-900`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L899))
- gitdata.parse_target(text, *, cwd=None) -> TargetSpec runs no git. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:523`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L523))
- gitdata.file_view(target, path, findings=None, allowed=None) -> FileView. FileView.to_dict() gives path, language, rows, cited_ranges and the truncated/deleted/binary flags. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:352-353`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L352), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:72-83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L72))
- gitdata.definitions(target, name, allowed, limit=50) -> list[{path, line, text}]. It raises ValueError when the name is not an identifier. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:300-304`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L300))
- Mailbox(directory) provides create(), append_inbox(InboxRow), append_outbox(OutboxRow), read_outbox(since) -> (rows, total), write_decision(finding_id, decision, note) and set_viewed(path, viewed). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:121-134`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L121), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L143))
- viewer models.load_findings(path) and load_checks(path) parse and validate, raising pydantic.ValidationError. schema_text(), checks_schema_text() and walkthrough_schema_text() produce the committed JSON Schemas. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:292-295`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L292), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:305-307`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L305))
- netinfo exposes detect_container(), bind_host(), probe_host() and display_urls(port) -> list[(url, label)]. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py:55-56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/netinfo.py#L55))
- budget.start(max_usd, state) begins the ledger for the current context. budget.record(usage, *, label) never raises. budget.check(*, stage) raises BudgetExceededError past the ceiling. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py:42-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/budget.py#L42))
- sdk.agent_call_structured(prompt, schema, *, allowed_tools, model, effort, max_turns=60, cwd, budget_label, call_timeout=1200.0) -> T returns a validated pydantic instance or raises AgentCallError. RepoTrustError is never retried. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py:153-164`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/sdk.py#L153))
- The gates all return GateResult(passed, reason, action): citation_gate(target, citation), finding_gate(target, finding), verdict_gate(target, verdict) and need_gate(need). gated_need(need) -> Need never drops the need. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:62`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L62), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:240`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L240))
- The orchestrator's entry points are review(spec, out_dir, config, *, session_id), resume(target_dir, config, *, session_id), batch(specs, out_dir, config, *, parallel=2), plan_only(spec, out_dir, config) and assess_only(target_dir, config). ReviewError means exit code 2 and InterruptedRun means exit code 4. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:527`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L527), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:53-59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L53))
- config.load_config(out_dir, *, max_cost_usd) -> ReviewConfig. run_config(cfg) -> dict reports each stage's model and effort, showing "session default" when unset. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:117`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L117))
- ledger exposes started(*, slug, target_dir, head_sha, session_id), finished(*, producer, …, stopped, run), interrupted(target_dir, head_sha), read() and reviews_root(). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:122-128`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L122))
- rounds exposes keep_round(target_dir, slug, new_head_sha) -> Path | None, load_rounds(target_dir, current_head, elsewhere) -> list[EarlierFinding], load_decisions(target_dir), round_name(target_dir, findings_file) and history_elsewhere(...). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:200`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L200), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:402`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L402))
- runlock.hold(target_dir) is a context manager that raises runlock.Locked(target_dir, holder) when another process holds the lock. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py:62-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py#L62))
- target exposes resolve(repo, *, branch, pr, range_, tree, path, base_branch, fetch) -> Resolved, build_target(resolved, …) -> TargetInfo, write_target_files(target, diff, target_dir) and snapshot_head(target, dest). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:427-429`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L427), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:674`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L674))
- state.save_state(state, target_dir) -> Path and load_state(path) -> ReviewState | None. timings.open_interval(state, key) and close_interval(state, key) bracket each stage. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py:18-23`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py#L18), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py:28-38`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py#L28))
- composite.render(edl: EDL, out_path: Path, work_dir: Path | None = None) -> Path is the render entry point. It runs ffmpeg and returns out_path. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:502`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L502))
- composite.build_filter_complex(edl, clips, title) returns (filter_complex, cmd_inputs, video_label, audio_label). cmd_inputs is a flat list of [-i, path] pairs. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:256-258`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L256))
- EDL.load(path) and EDL.from_dict(d) are classmethods that build an EDL from JSON. EDL.validate(source_size=None, words=None) -> list[str] returns warnings and raises ValueError on errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:214-220`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L214), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:286-287`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L286))
- EDL.xfade_before(segment_index) -> float and EDL.total_duration() -> float are the shared timing helpers used by composite and timeline.
