- prep.prep(source, prompt_hint='', language=None, model=None) -> PrepResult runs the analysis. prep.cache_dir_for(source) -> Path is how other stages find a source's cache. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:623-624`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L623), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:111-113`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L111))
- timeline.place_segments(edl) -> list[Placed], to_output(t, placed) -> float | None, map_span(start, end, placed) and map_words(words, placed) -> list[dict]. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:70-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L70), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:104`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L104))
- captions.build_ass(words, captions, headline, W, H, font_name, brand) -> str. The words must already be in output time. captions.caption_lines(words, captions, brand) -> list[dict] gives the lines that will be burned in. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:287-288`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L287), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py:270`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/captions.py#L270))
- brand.load(path) -> Brand and brand.default() -> Brand, plus brand.ass_colour(hex_rgb, alpha=0) -> str. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:53-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L53))
- transcript.load(path) -> dict checks the contract keys. from_whisper_json(data, engine, language=None) and from_transcribe_skill(data) convert raw output. write(contract, path) saves the JSON. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:148-153`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L148), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py:90`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/transcript.py#L90))

- layouts builders: pad_chain(W, H, bg), crop_chain(W, H, fx, fy), blur_graph(W, H), panel_chain(panel, W, H, fx, fy), stack_graph(a, b, W, H, bg, fx_a, fx_b), speaker_graph(runs, panels, W, H, focus). ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py:49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py:168-169`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py#L168))
