([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py:254-266`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py#L254))
- sdk.llm_call_structured(prompt, schema, *, ..., max_retries=1, label='structured') -> T is async and returns a validated Pydantic instance. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py:361-373`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py#L361))
- atomic.atomic_write_text(path: Path, text: str, *, encoding='utf-8') -> None. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/atomic.py:36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/atomic.py#L36))
- ResearchState.new(query, output_file, state_file), .checkpoint(), .load(state_file), .advance_to(stage), .is_complete(stage), and mark_source_extracted/mark_source_failed, both of which checkpoint. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py:110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py#L110), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py:156-159`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/state.py#L156))
- ResearchConfig.from_cli_args(args: argparse.Namespace) builds the run configuration. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py:378-380`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/config.py#L378))
- Gate functions: query_diversity_gate(queries, min_clusters=3, similarity_threshold=0.6), min_sources_gate(source_count, min_sources), coverage_gate(findings, sub_questions), critical_source_gate(sources, findings, sub_questions), reassess_gate(reassessment). Each returns a GateResult. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:148-150`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L148), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:350`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L350))
- Node entry points: synthesize(config, state) -> str, write_incomplete_report(config, state, reason, metadata) -> str, and verify_verdicts(config, claims, new_findings) -> list[ClaimVerdict]. All except write_incomplete_report are async. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L26), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py:29-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py#L29))
- untrusted.is_fetchable_url(url) -> bool, safe_url_text(url) -> str and defang_untrusted(text) -> str are the fencing helpers that other nodes import. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py:31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/untrusted.py#L31))
- ProgressWriter(path) has initialize(query, preset, fetch_budget), log_stage(stage, details), log_source(url, status, finding_count, error) and cleanup(). ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/progress.py:40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/progress.py#L40))

## Conventions

- Each generator module creates its logger with `logging.getLogger(__name__)`. The standalone script uses setup_logging instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L27), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L26), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L21), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L31))
- Catalog, state and summary files are written atomically, either with atomic_write_json or with a tmp file plus os.replace. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:585-588`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L585), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:323-325`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L323), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py:275-277`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/synthesize_now.py#L275))
- Problems are collected as strings in GenerationResult.errors rather than raised. Unreadable or corrupt inputs degrade to empty defaults plus a warning. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:494-497`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L494), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:138-141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L138), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:102`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L102))
- Catalog paths always go through `Paths.resolve().catalogs_dir()`, never through env vars read directly. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:155`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L155), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:191`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L191), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py:133`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/dispatcher.py#L133))
- Log lines meant for grepping start with an UPPER_SNAKE tag, and shell messages start with `[multiplai-context]`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py:322`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/base.py#L322), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:269`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L269), [`plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh:143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/hooks/run.sh#L143))
- Generator constructors and run() take keyword-only arguments, and every run() accepts force_enable. A test pins that contract. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py:183`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/banks.py#L183), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L110), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:1062-1065`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L1062))
- Tests put scripts/ on sys.path at module top, then import inside each test (for example `from generators.dispatcher import ...`). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:31-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:33-36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L33))
- Tests isolate file I/O by pointing CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA (and memory dir options) at pytest's tmp_path via monkeypatch.setenv. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:65`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L65), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:62-63`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L62))
- Tests are grouped into `Test*` classes, one per requirement, under `# ---` banner comments. Test names read as sentences (`test_a_dropped_field_is_reported`) and docstrings often use WHEN/THEN. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:121-130`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L121), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:94-102`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L94))
- Model calls are faked: either generator run() is patched with an async mock_run returning a GenerationResult, or the client is an AsyncMock returning a ModelResponse. Async code runs via asyncio.run or @pytest.mark.asyncio. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:76-79`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L76), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:65-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L65), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:178`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L178))
- Assertions are plain `assert` statements with f-string failure messages naming what went wrong. Error cases use pytest.raises. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L208), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py:371`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_dispatcher.py#L371), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py:248`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_hand_field_regression.py#L248))
- Comments and docstrings explain why a choice was made, often citing the incident that caused it (issue numbers, dates, measurements). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py:168-169`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/config.py#L168), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py:48-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/generators/memory.py#L48))
- Readers of optional or untrusted files fail open: they catch OSError/JSONDecodeError/ValueError and return None or an empty value instead of raising. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:259-262`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L259), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:214-217`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L214), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py:30-33`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/hook_input.py#L30))
- Code that runs in hooks never raises. Public functions wrap their body in except Exception, log a warning with exc_info, and return False or 0. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:264-266`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L264), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:584-585`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L584))
- Whole-file rewrites go through temp file plus os.replace, cleaning up the temp file on failure. Append-only logs use O_APPEND instead. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py:37-42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fsio.py#L37), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py:227-233`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/learnings_ledger.py#L227), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py:193-199`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py#L193))
- Each module defines logger = logging.getLogger(__name__) and logs with %-style lazy arguments, not f-strings. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py:66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet.py#L66), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py:48-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/job_effort.py#L48), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py:49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/runtime.py#L49))
- An invalid plugin option logs a warning and falls back to the default; it never breaks the hook. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:1150-1155`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L1150), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py:138-142`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_router.py#L138))
- Module-level constants are UPPER_CASE, private helpers and compiled regexes start with an underscore, and modules list their public API in __all__. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py:84-94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/memory_write_floor.py#L84), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py:50-59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/rejections.py#L50), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py:90-92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/routing_validation.py#L90))
- Closed vocabularies are module-level tuples or frozensets, and normalizers return None for out-of-set values rather than coercing them. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py:191-194`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/taxonomy.py#L191), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py:56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/session_registry.py#L56))
- Tests are pytest classes named Test<Subject> with descriptive test_<behaviour> methods, and use @pytest.mark.parametrize for value tables. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py:34-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py#L34), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py:57-59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py#L57), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py:51-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py#L51))
- Tests put scripts/ on sys.path, import modules as 'from lib import ...', use the tmp_path fixture as the data dir, and patch with unittest.mock patch.dict or patch.object. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py:23-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py#L23), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py:86-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py#L86), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py:16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py#L16))
- Tests use plain assert statements and small module-level helpers (for example _hook_input and _read_entry). Where the reason for an assertion matters, the test explains it in a docstring. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py:35-44`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_registry.py#L35), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py:82-91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_learning_taxonomy.py#L82))
- Each module creates a module-level logger with logging.getLogger(__name__). In multiplai-context it is named `logger`; code_facts names it `log`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L87), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L26))
- Collectors and I/O helpers never raise. They catch specific exceptions (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) and fall back to an empty value, zero, or an `error` string. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:88-91`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L88), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:42-45`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:100-101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L100))
- Records are @dataclass classes with defaults, and derived status is exposed as @property (empty, clean, running, approved). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py:30-31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/backlog.py#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:64-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py:62-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/jobs.py#L62))
- Tunable limits are UPPER_CASE module constants, each with a comment explaining the measurement or reasoning behind the value. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:23-28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L23), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:33-36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L33), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py:35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/git_repos.py#L35))
- Helpers private to a module are prefixed with an underscore (_probe, _rollup, _one, _load, _count_files), and tests call them directly. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py:131`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/prs.py#L131), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:138`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L138))
- Timestamps are timezone-aware UTC, with a trailing 'Z' converted to +00:00 before fromisoformat. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py:26`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/timeparse.py#L26), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:196`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L196))
- Read-modify-write on shared files is serialised with fcntl.flock, and the code proceeds unlocked if the lock cannot be taken. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py:122-125`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/fleet_sources/common.py#L122), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py:287-290`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/utilisation.py#L287))
- Tests are plain pytest functions with sentence-style names (test_<behaviour>). A one-line docstring gives the reason and often cites a past incident. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:176-177`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L176), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py:39-40`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py#L39))
- Tests use the tmp_path fixture for any filesystem work (never the real .multiplai/), a fixed NOW datetime, and small builder helpers such as _pr(**kw) and pr(**kw). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:17`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L17), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py:25-28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py#L25))
- Tests replace external calls by monkeypatching module attributes (prs.run, prs.gh_available, git_repos.REPO_BUDGET) instead of using mocking libraries. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:170-171`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L170), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:104`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L104))
- Test files are split into sections with `# ---` comment banners, and single results are taken by tuple unpacking, e.g. `(state,) = ...`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py:118-120`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_sources.py#L118), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py:110`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_fleet_digest.py#L110))
- Newer modules start with `from __future__ import annotations`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py:57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/thinking.py#L57), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py:14`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/explore.py#L14), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py:7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/code_facts/partition.py#L7))
- Each module that logs creates a module-level `logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)` and logs with %-style lazy arguments. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L46), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L59), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L42))
- Code on a hook path never raises. It catches broadly, logs, and returns a sentinel (GitResult(False), None, 0, (False, reason), DrainResult(0, 0)), and the module docstrings say so. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py:16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py#L16), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L28), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:1072-1079`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L1072))
- Results and value objects are frozen dataclasses. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:59-60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L59), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:201-202`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L201), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:335-336`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L335))
- Modules declare an explicit `__all__` and start with `from __future__ import annotations`. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:31-39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L31), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L22), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L84))
- Files are written atomically through lib.fsio (atomic_write / atomic_write_json), and queue moves use os.rename or os.replace so racing processes cannot both claim a file. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:852`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L852), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py:236`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/costing_collector.py#L236), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py:64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint_drain.py#L64))
- Plugin options are read through multiplai_core.plugin_options accessors (option / option_int / option_float) with a module constant as the default, never through os.environ directly. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py:135`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/banks.py#L135), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:146`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L146), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:271`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L271))
- Heavy or optional imports are done inside the function so that hooks which do not need them do not pay the import cost. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py:637`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/checkpoint.py#L637), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py:932`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/dream_triage.py#L932))
- Tunable constants are module-level UPPER_CASE names, often documented with `#:` comments that explain why the value was chosen, including measured incidents. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py:47-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_collisions.py#L47), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py:48-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/bank_git.py#L48), [`plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py:59`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/scripts/lib/extraction_drain.py#L59))
- Tests are pytest classes named `TestX`, with `test_` methods whose names read as sentences describing behaviour, and they use plain `assert`s. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py:105-109`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py#L105), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py:90-92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py#L90), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py:126-130`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py#L126))
- Test fixtures build a temporary data dir or workspace from tmp_path, point env vars at it with monkeypatch, and call multiplai_core.paths._reset_cache() before and after. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py:34-49`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py#L34), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py:40-47`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py#L40))
- Tests use @pytest.mark.parametrize for input tables. They replace side effects with small hand-written fakes (_Item, _SpawnRecorder) installed via monkeypatch.setattr, and get helpers from conftest (SCRIPTS_DIR, import_script). ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py:81-83`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py#L81), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py:50-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py#L50), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py:94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py#L94))
- Test modules open with a docstring that explains what the tests can and cannot prove, and which assertions (usually refusals) matter most. ([`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py:3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_memory_banks.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py:15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-context/tests/test_session_end_checkpoint.py#L15))
- Each module defines `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)`. Log calls use %-style lazy arguments, not f-strings. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:69`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L69), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:43`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L43), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:288`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L288))
- Side-effecting bookkeeping (board writes, budget accounting, commits) never raises. It catches the error, logs a warning, and returns None or False so the build continues. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:219-221`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L219), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py:134-135`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/budget.py#L134), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:623-625`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L623))
- When something can't be determined, it is treated as unsafe or a refusal, never as success: None exit codes, unknown ancestry, unknown commit counts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:817-818`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L817), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:76-77`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L76))
- Machine-readable progress goes to stdout as `PREFIX:field:field` lines printed with flush=True (BOARD:, BUDGET:WARN:, REFERENCES:, SPEND:). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:269`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L269), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1799`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1799))
- Files are written atomically: write a temp sibling, then replace it over the target. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py:261-263`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/board.py#L261), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py:536-538`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/code_analysis.py#L536))
- Config loaders validate each value. An invalid one logs `Invalid <key> value %r in config.yaml — using <default>` and keeps the default. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:409-410`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L409), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:1433-1434`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L1433))
- Gates return GateResult(passed, reason, action, metadata) and never raise. `action` is a snake_case recovery hint. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:227-232`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L227), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:1173-1177`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L1173))
- Docstrings and comments explain why a decision was made, often naming the incident or failure mode behind it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py:42-44`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/config.py#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py:594-598`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/git_ops.py#L594))
- Tests are grouped into `class TestXxx` classes with long, descriptive `test_<behaviour>` methods. Each method's docstring states the requirement it proves. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py:88-90`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py#L88), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:487-492`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L487))
- Tests use pytest's `tmp_path`, `monkeypatch` and `capsys`, plus autouse fixtures that stub model calls and agents, so no test spawns a live agent. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py:42-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py#L42), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:61-74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L61))
- Tests make plain `assert` statements on observable results (the card JSON on disk, stdout lines, config properties) and patch collaborators with unittest.mock patch/AsyncMock. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py:176-182`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py#L176), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py:433-434`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_board.py#L433), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:493-496`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L493))
- Imported names that start with `Test` are aliased so pytest doesn't collect them. The same rule (no `test` prefix on helpers) is followed in gates.py. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L56), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py:706-708`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/gates.py#L706))
- Log lines use a structured key=value style with START/DONE/SKIP/FAIL(ED) verbs, for example 'START phase=X', 'SKIP phase=X reason=...'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:391`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L391), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py:194`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/sdk.py#L194))
- Every module gets its logger with log = logging.getLogger(__name__) and starts with `from __future__ import annotations`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py:7-16`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/rubric.py#L7), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py:33-42`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/stop_record.py#L33))
- Best-effort steps catch Exception, log a warning ending in '(non-fatal)', and carry on. They never fail the build. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:587-588`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L587), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:966-967`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L966))
- Writes to the progress file are wrapped so that an OSError is only logged and never breaks a phase. _progress_note is the shared helper for this. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:782-787`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L782), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:409-410`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L409))
- Communication with the wrapper goes through flushed stdout markers of the form TOKEN:TOKEN:value. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:1299-1300`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L1299), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:532`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L532))
- New state fields always get defaults (None, False or an empty list) and a comment saying old checkpoints still load, so older .build-state.json files stay readable. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:202-207`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L202), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py:254-259`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/state.py#L254))
- Imports of sibling modules (llm_steps, board, intake) are often done inside functions, to avoid import cycles and to keep patch targets stable for tests. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py:384-385`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/intake.py#L384), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:209-210`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L209))
- Git commits made by the pipeline use explicit pathspecs through git_ops.commit_stage, with conventional-commit messages such as docs(specs): ... or chore(specs): .... The whole tree is never staged. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py:244-248`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L244), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py:1004-1006`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/spec_generator.py#L1004))
- Tests are pytest classes named Test<Feature> holding async methods named test_<behaviour_in_words>, marked @pytest.mark.asyncio. They use the tmp_path, monkeypatch and capsys fixtures and plain assert statements. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py:200-202`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py#L200), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py:302-305`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py#L302))
- Tests fake every model call (AsyncMock, a FakeCall class, or patching sdk functions) but use real temp git repos and the real orchestrator. They check outcomes on disk or in git (git show HEAD:<path>) and in stdout markers. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py:48-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py#L48))
- Tests build their inputs with module-level helpers (_args, _config, _spec_stage_mocks) and use autouse fixtures to switch off cross-cutting phases such as epic split and spec-extract. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py:49-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py:57-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_intake.py#L57))
- Stop/resume behaviour is tested in a real subprocess driver that patches the phase functions by dotted path and sends itself real SIGKILL or SIGTERM signals. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py:72`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py#L72), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py:124-128`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py#L124))
- Each module creates a module-level logger with `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:78`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L78), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:36`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L36))
- Log lines use a key=value format with a START/DONE/FAIL prefix (e.g. `FAIL block=%d name=%s phase=...`). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1148-1149`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1148), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:235`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L235), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:111`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L111))
- Git reads never raise. They return None (meaning 'git could not tell') or '' and log a warning, and callers treat None as 'not checked'. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:216-223`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L216), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:67-68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L67), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:166-170`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L166))
- Failures are raised as domain-named exception classes that carry a human-readable reason naming the file or command, and the docstrings say exactly what is raised. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py:75-76`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/fetch.py#L75), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:45-46`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L45), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:84-88`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L84))
- Status is reported on stdout as colon-separated markers (PREFIX:KIND:detail) that other processes parse. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:580`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L580), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:1682`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L1682))
- State and records are written atomically (temp file plus os.replace, or checkpoint.save_model), and records.py reuses store._write_atomic. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py:244-251`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/store.py#L244), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py:22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/records.py#L22))
- Cross-package imports are often done lazily inside functions to avoid import cycles. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py:49-52`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/review.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:342-344`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L342))
- Agent and model calls pass a per-step model, effort and budget_label taken from config.model_for/effort_for using the same step name. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:84-88`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L84), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py:802-806`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/epic/merge.py#L802), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py:701-703`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/tdd_engine.py#L701))
- Model and agent calls can be replaced in tests through injectable seams (`call=` parameters or the Seams dataclass), with None meaning the real call. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py:76-79`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/triage.py#L76), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py:103-108`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/build_pipeline/findings/loop.py#L103))
- Test names are full-sentence snake_case descriptions of the behaviour, and each test has a docstring explaining the scenario, often citing plan item numbers. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:296`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L296), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:756-757`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L756), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py:88-89`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py#L88))
- Tests use real git while faking every model, review and GitHub call. The fakes are classes (FakeAgent, CostlyReview) wired in with monkeypatch, and the tests use tmp_path. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py:20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py#L20), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:99-101`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L99), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:552`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L552))
- Async tests are marked @pytest.mark.asyncio, scenarios vary through @pytest.mark.parametrize, and assertions are plain `assert` statements, often comparing tuples or exact marker lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:537-538`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L537), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:585-586`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L585), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py:100-102`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py#L100))
- Fixtures are pytest fixtures, some autouse and some grouped in Test* classes, and they reset global state such as locks or the process spend. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py:64-67`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_merge.py#L64), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py:81-85`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_epic_fixer_cap.py#L81))
- Every module begins with a docstring explaining why it exists (often naming where it was copied from), then `from __future__ import annotations` and a module-level `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py:3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/netguard.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py:9-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/fetcher.py#L9), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py:21-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/template.py#L21))
- Each module defines small domain exception classes whose docstrings name the exit code or meaning. Lower-level errors are re-raised as these with `raise ... from e`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:49-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:26-27`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L26), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:63-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L63))
- Gates return a result object (passed/reason, or a list of errors) instead of raising, and the caller decides what to do with it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:35-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L35), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/gates.py:44-50`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/gates.py#L44))
- Subprocess calls are fixed argv lists with shell=False and check=False. Failures come back as values (a Result, or None) or as a domain error. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py:68-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/target.py#L68), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py:60-61`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py#L60))
- Model output gets exactly one retry that carries the failure reason. After that the gap is recorded or the call raises; there are no unlimited loops. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py:120-121`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/sdk.py#L120), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:87`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L87), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py:208`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py#L208))
- Agent stages re-raise RepoTrustError, and record any other AgentCallError both in the answers dict as {'error': first line[:200]} and in state.errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py:89-94`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/trace.py#L89), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py:191-196`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/stages/write.py#L191))
- Events go through multiplai_core.log_utils.log_event with a component, an event name and structured fields. Prompt text and PR body text are never logged. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py:254-255`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L254), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py:3-5`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/commands.py#L3))
- Source and test files use `# --- name ---` divider comments to group functions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py:54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/gates.py#L54), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py:74`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pr_pipeline/github.py#L74), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:63`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L63))
- Test names are plain sentences describing the behaviour (test_<what happens>). Tests are plain pytest functions, and async ones are written as `async def test_...` without a visible marker. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L66), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:241`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L241))
- Tests build real git repos under tmp_path with a fixed author environment (ENV) and a small git() helper, instead of mocking git. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:23-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L23), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:29-31`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L29))
- Tests replace model calls by monkeypatching sdk.agent_call_structured with a fake callable that records labels and prompts and returns canned schema instances. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:239-247`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L239), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:168-173`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L168))
- Tests assert with bare `assert`, check CLI exit codes directly (main([...]) == 2) together with captured stderr, and use pytest.raises with match= for errors. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:132-133`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L132), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:500-503`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L500))
- Shared fixtures and constants come from conftest (SCHEMA, target_info, high_finding). Module-local fixtures use @pytest.fixture, and some return an installer function. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py:232-233`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_tree.py#L232))
- Pydantic answer models ignore unknown keys so that extra model output does not fail parsing. State records use Field(default_factory=...) for collections. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:30-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py:168-170`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/walkthrough_pipeline/models.py#L168))
- Each module starts with a long docstring explaining the design and the reasons behind it, often pointing to plan items, rows or decisions. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:1-6`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L1), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:1-3`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L1))
- Each module gets its logger with `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)`. Failures that are recovered from are logged at warning or error level rather than raised. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:51`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L51), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py:29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py#L29), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:136`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L136))
- Agent failures are handled the same way everywhere: RepoTrustError is re-raised first, then AgentCallError becomes a conservative outcome, with the error's first line cut to 200 characters. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py:221-225`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/merge.py#L221), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:147-150`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L147))
- Custom exceptions subclass Exception and build their user-facing message in __init__, naming the fix (for example --restart or --out). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py:27-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/state.py#L27), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py:34-41`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/runlock.py#L34), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:58-66`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L58))
- Prompts are built as a list of string blocks, empty blocks are filtered out, and the rest are joined with "\n\n". Every prompt ends with the schema and then JSON_ONLY. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:84`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L84), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py:320-323`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/prompts/find.py#L320))
- Model answer schemas are pydantic models derived from a private `_Model` base that sets ConfigDict (extra='ignore' for model output, extra='forbid' for saved data). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py:53-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/group.py#L53), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py:29-30`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/write.py#L29), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py:64-65`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/scope.py#L64))
- For resumability, state is saved as each answer arrives, and outputs are rebuilt from state rather than read back, so a resumed run writes the same bytes. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py:193-194`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/stages/verify.py#L193), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/report.py:6-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/spec_extract_pipeline/report.py#L6))
- In the video-edit CLI, a failure prints '✗ <message>' to stderr and returns a nonzero int. Results go to stdout as `LABEL: value` lines. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:166-168`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L166), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py:145-146`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pipeline.py#L145))
- Tests replace the model by monkeypatching `sdk.agent_call_structured` (or `sdk.run_agent`) with a fake async callable. The fakes route on budget_label or on text in the prompt. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:168-174`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L168), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py:41-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py#L41))
- Test names are full sentences describing the behaviour (test_a_..., test_the_...). Async tests are plain `async def` with no marker. Files are split into sections by `# --- ... ---` comments. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:302`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L302), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py:56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py#L56), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:187`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L187))
- Tests use shared fixtures and finding factories from conftest (target_info, high_finding, SCHEMA), build real git repos under tmp_path with a fixed env, and use plain asserts plus pytest.mark.parametrize. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:12`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L12), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py:70-79`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_trim.py#L70), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py:136`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_checks.py#L136))
- Every member that uses multiplai-core repeats the same `[tool.uv.sources]` git line (branch main) together with the standard 'Member-local source' comment. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml:14-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml#L14), [`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml:16-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml#L16))
- Members that call a model depend on `multiplai-core[sdk]` and never pin claude-agent-sdk directly. Members that make no model calls use bare `multiplai-core`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:7-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L7), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml:7-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/pyproject.toml#L7))
- Buildable pipelines are version 0.1.0 and use hatchling with `allow-direct-references = true` (needed for the git reference to multiplai-core). Their wheel package name is the project name in snake_case. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml:30-35`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/pr/scripts/pyproject.toml#L30), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml:29-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/spec-extract/scripts/pyproject.toml#L29))
- Plain script-only directories are named `<skill>-scripts`, use version 0.0.0 and set `[tool.uv] package = false`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml:3-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/deepen/scripts/pyproject.toml#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml:3-9`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/skill-creator/scripts/pyproject.toml#L3))
- Tests use pytest with pytest-asyncio in `asyncio_mode = "auto"`. The dev tools are declared both as a `dev` extra and as a `dev` dependency group, so a bare `uv run --directory <dir> python -m pytest tests/` works. CI uses `--package <name> --extra dev`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml:19-24`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/pyproject.toml#L19), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml:38-39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/codebase-walkthrough/scripts/pyproject.toml#L38), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml:16-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/lib/code_facts/pyproject.toml#L16))
- Dependencies are declared with lower-bound pins (`>=`), adding `<next-major` caps for external API SDKs. The single exact pin is playwright in video-edit. ([`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml:9-10`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/gmail/scripts/pyproject.toml#L9), [`plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml:8`](../../../plugins/multiplai-messaging/skills/slack/scripts/pyproject.toml#L8))
- Comments in the manifests explain why a dependency is there, often with a date and a measured cost. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml:6-7`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/pyproject.toml#L6), [`pyproject.toml:6`](../../../pyproject.toml#L6))
- Each module opens with a docstring that explains its behaviour and contracts, then `from __future__ import annotations` and a module logger `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py:10-19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/gates.py#L10), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py:19-32`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/runlock.py#L19), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:12-28`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L12))
- Subprocesses always run with a fixed argv, shell=False and stdin=DEVNULL. Errors become domain exceptions (GitError or TargetError) that carry stderr. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py:55-64`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/target.py#L55), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:100-103`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L100))
- Errors are custom Exception subclasses whose messages tell the user what to do or pass next (e.g. "pass --out", "pass --repo <path>"). ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:430-433`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L430), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:771-772`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L771))
- Append-only files get one os.write per row on an O_APPEND descriptor. Whole-file JSON state is written to a temp file and moved into place with os.replace. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:79-81`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L79), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:49-51`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L49), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py:18-20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/state.py#L18))
- Reads are tolerant. An unreadable or non-dict JSON file returns None or {} and is logged, rather than raising. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:50-57`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L50), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:515-522`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L515), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:136-141`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L136))
- A broad `except Exception` is allowed only with a `# noqa: BLE001 — <reason>` comment that justifies it. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py:107`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/config.py#L107), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py:166`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/orchestrator.py#L166), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:329`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L329))
- Timestamps are UTC ISO strings ending in `Z`, made by small `now()` helpers in each module. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py:19-21`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/timings.py#L19), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py:69-70`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/ledger.py#L69), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py:36-37`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/mailbox.py#L36))
- Comments and docstrings name the multiplai-dev version where a field or layout appeared, so old files stay readable. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py:109-111`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/models.py#L109), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py:32-34`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/models.py#L32))
- Long modules are divided with `# --- topic ---` separator comments. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py:318`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/render.py#L318), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py:436`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review-viewer/scripts/review_viewer/gitdata.py#L436))
- Tests are plain pytest functions named `test_<behaviour as a sentence>`, using tmp_path, monkeypatch and fixtures, with bare `assert`s on CLI exit codes and file contents. Files are grouped under numbered `# --- N. topic ---` headers. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:57-60`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L57), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:75-78`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L75))
- Tests never call a real model. They monkeypatch `sdk.agent_call_structured` with a fake, or make every model-calling sdk name raise in driver scripts. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:33-39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L33), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py:48-53`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py#L48))
- Kill and stop behaviour is tested with real signals in a child-process driver: a script run with sys.executable that stops at a named point with SIGKILL, SIGTERM or os._exit. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py:62-73`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/stop_resume_driver.py#L62), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:1236-1242`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L1236))
- Variants are covered with @pytest.mark.parametrize, giving readable `ids`. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:443-444`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L443), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:517`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L517))
- A test that guards a structural rule fails when someone adds a new case without deciding how to handle it. For example, every top-level directory a run writes must be in rounds.NOT_KEPT. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py:1711-1712`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/tests/test_data_dir.py#L1711), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py:68`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/review/scripts/review_pipeline/rounds.py#L68))
- Video-edit stages raise ValueError (or FileNotFoundError and RuntimeError) whose message tells the user what to change, not just what failed. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py:315-319`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/edl.py#L315), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:401-402`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L401), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:372-374`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L372))
- Video-edit code reports progress and warnings with print, using '→' for steps and '⚠' for warnings, and does not use logging. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:506-507`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L506), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:632`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L632))
- The research pipeline logs through a module-level `log = logging.getLogger(__name__)` with %-style arguments. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:15`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L15), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py:20`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py#L20), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py:39`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/sdk.py#L39))
- Every module begins with `from __future__ import annotations` and uses modern type hints (X | None, list[...]). ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py:19`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/brand.py#L19), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/atomic.py:11`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/atomic.py#L11))
- ffmpeg graph builders are pure functions that return filter strings, or (graph, label) tuples, so tests can compare exact strings. Only the top-level steps run subprocess with check=True. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/layouts.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:424-425`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L424), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py:215`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/composite.py#L215))
- Prep steps are idempotent through 'if the output file exists, reuse it' caching in the source's cache directory. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:124-126`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L124), [`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py:306-308`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/prep.py#L306))
- Video-edit data types are plain @dataclass classes, while research-pipeline data crossing LLM boundaries is Pydantic BaseModel. ([`plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py:21-22`](../../../plugins/multiplai-media/skills/video-edit/scripts/stages/timeline.py#L21), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py:131`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/models.py#L131))
- Gates return a GateResult with passed, reason, an action naming the recovery step, and metadata, and never raise. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:189-194`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L189), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py:237-242`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/gates.py#L237))
- LLM nodes read model, effort and thinking from per-node maps on ResearchConfig, keyed by node name, and give every call a label of the form 'node' or 'node:sub'. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py:58-61`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/verify.py#L58), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py:92`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/nodes/synthesize.py#L92))
- Prompt templates are module-level string constants filled with .format(). Literal JSON braces are doubled. ([`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py:3-4`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/__init__.py#L3), [`plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/verify.py:27-29`](../../../plugins/multiplai-research/skills/deep-research/scripts/research_pipeline/prompts/verify.py#L27))
- Tests are pytest classes named Test<Behaviour>, holding test_<plain-English behaviour> methods that often carry a docstring explaining why. Assertions are plain assert. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:137-143`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L137), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:1002-1003`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L1002))
- Tests build real files and git repos under tmp_path, through `setup` fixtures that return tuples the tests unpack. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:1103-1105`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L1103), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py:111-134`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py#L111))
- Async agent calls are stubbed with unittest.mock.patch on the importing module's path using new_callable=AsyncMock, and async tests use @pytest.mark.asyncio. Autouse fixtures stop any live agent from running. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:61-71`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L61), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py:48-54`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py#L48))
- Tests use @pytest.mark.parametrize over named case tables, often with sorted dict keys or ids=, to cover many inputs. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py:240-241`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_test_change_marker.py#L240), [`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:3186-3189`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L3186))
- Model classes whose names start with Test are aliased on import so pytest does not try to collect them. ([`plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py:56`](../../../plugins/multiplai-dev/skills/buildme/scripts/tests/test_tdd_engine.py#L56))
